## 新投稿 (95)

<h3 id="1">[1] iFLYTEK-Embodied-Omni Technical Report</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02542](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02542)
- **Authors**: Yuan Zhang, Jingfei Ni, Guanchen Lu, Shiqi Zhang, Qingshan Xu, Chi Liu, Xin Nie, Wenjie Xu, Lin Gao, Zhiyuan Cheng, Mingxin Zhou, Jiajia Wu, Diyuan Liu, Jia Pan, Chao Ji
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Embodied AI, Multimodal Learning, Vision-Language Model
- **Summary**: 现有具身代理方法通常分别处理视觉语言推理、视频世界建模和动作生成，级联流程导致接口瓶颈和累积错误。本文提出iFLYTEK-Embodied-Omni，一个统一的多模态基础模型，通过共享的多模态自注意力机制联合建模视觉、语言和动作，采用大脑-小脑协作架构和四阶段训练策略。该模型在指令理解、任务规划、未来状态预测及直接生成动作块方面表现出色。

<h3 id="2">[2] Internal Pluralism and the Limits of Pairwise Comparisons</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02672](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02672)
- **Authors**: Bailey Flanigan, Michelle Si
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CY
- **Tags**: Pluralistic Alignment, Preference Elicitation
- **Summary**: 局部成对比较常用于学习人们对决策规则的偏好，但假设了局部充分性和决定性，而内部多元主义（个体有多重优先）可能导致失败。本文建立了多元主义偏好的形式化模型，识别出强制比较的两种失败模式，并表明允许不决定可以显著减少学习偏好所需的查询次数。研究指向直接引出优先级的偏好学习方法。

<h3 id="3">[3] ASK in the Dark: Uncertainty-Gated LLM Assistance under Partial Observability</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02686](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02686)
- **Authors**: Juarez Monteiro, Nathan Gavenski, Guilherme Lima, Francisco Galuppo, Odinaldo Rodrigues, Adriano Veloso
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Reinforcement Learning, Uncertainty Estimation
- **Venue**: IJCAI-ECAI Joint Workshop on Planning for Complex Real-World Applications and Bridging the Gap Between AI Planning and (Reinforcement) Learning
- **Summary**: 在部分可观测环境中，强化学习代理利用小语言模型（SLM）指导时，普通的不确定性门控方法因上下文不足而失效。本文提出ASK+，通过提供轨迹感知的上下文和结构化思维链推理，将SLM从被动冗余检查转变为信息顾问，并使用预测熵进行选择性查询。在DoorKey、FourRooms和HigherLower任务上，ASK+显著提升了成功率。

<h3 id="4">[4] Automated Data Readiness for Scientific AI</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02771](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02771)
- **Authors**: Sean R. Wilkinson, Valentine G. Anantharaj, Jong Youl Choi, Ketan Maheshwari, Marshall McDonnell, Massimiliano Lupo Pasini, Polina Shpilker, Renan Souza, Patrick Widener, Sarp Oral, Wesley Brewer
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CE
- **Tags**: Data Curation, Research Reproducibility, Scientific Discovery
- **Summary**: 现有框架未能统一自动化数据转换、就绪性评估、溯源追踪和代理原生部署。本文提出REDI框架，通过五阶段流水线（摄取、预处理、转换、结构化、输出）实现数据自动化就绪，并配套SetGo工具实现FAIR合规和目录发布。在气候、蛋白质组学、材料科学和核聚变数据集上，REDI实现了近理想的并行扩展至Frontier的100个节点。

<h3 id="5">[5] SwarmResearch: Orchestrating Coding Agents for Open-Ended Discovery</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02807](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02807)
- **Authors**: Yuvraj Virk, Zack Edds, Chunqiu Steven Xia, Lingming Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Code Generation, Multi-Agent System, Automated Research
- **Summary**: 长时间运行的编码代理容易收敛到单一高级方法，而错过其他更优方法。本文提出SwarmResearch，一种编排器-子代理架构，由Shepherd Agent利用全局上下文引导一群Search Agent各自在git分支中局部探索。在13/15个开放式优化任务上，SwarmResearch发现了更好或相当的解决方案，优于现有技术。

<h3 id="6">[6] Object-Centric Environment Modeling for Agentic Tasks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02846](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02846)
- **Authors**: Yiyang Li, Tianyi Ma, Zehong Wang, Yijun Ma, Yanfang Ye
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Object-Centric Learning, World Model
- **Summary**: 该论文针对LLM代理在累积经验时自由文本记忆难以维护、验证和重用的问题，提出了面向对象的环境建模方法(OCM)。OCM将经验组织为可执行的对象中心环境模型，维护对象知识和过程知识两个代码库，并在每次交互后进行更新和验证。实验表明OCM在多个基准测试中取得最佳平均排名，并减少了无效动作。

<h3 id="7">[7] MedCalc-Pro: Solving Complex Medical Calculations with LLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02879](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02879)
- **Authors**: Siran Zhao, Ruihui Hou, Ziyue Huai, Chennuo Zhang, Tong Ruan
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, LLM Agent, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 当前医疗计算基准多简化，每个病例对应单一计算器，而实际临床需要多计算器联合评估。本文提出MedCalc-Pro基准，涵盖单计算器、多计算器和嵌套计算器三种设置，包含2268个临床病例和77个计算器。同时提出通用代理框架，支持多工具选择和嵌套工具调用，通过结构化验证和证据审查抑制参数误差传播，在所有设置中取得最佳性能。

<h3 id="8">[8] Oyster-II: Reinforcement Learning for Constructive Safety Alignment in Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02914](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02914)
- **Authors**: Jiyang Guan, Yong Xie, Jun Chen, Jiexi Liu, Zipeng Ye, Defeng Li, Jiayu Shen, Jialing Tao, Hui Xue
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, Reinforcement Learning, LLM Safety
- **Summary**: 基于拒绝的对齐策略限制了LLM的有用性，Oyster-I的SFT方案存在安全泛化不足和安全思维链过度泛化问题。本文提出Oyster-II，采用Zero-RL范式和多阶段强化学习框架进行建设性安全对齐。在广泛基准上，Oyster-II在安全维度上全面超越Qwen3-14B和Oyster-I，性能可媲美更大模型。

<h3 id="9">[9] VERITAS: Towards a General-Purpose Replication Tool for Scientific Research</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02931](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02931)
- **Authors**: Haokun Liu, Filbert Aurelian Tjiaranata, Chenhao Tan
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Research Reproducibility, LLM Agent, Scientific Discovery
- **Summary**: 科学研究的独立验证既困难又重要，现有自动化工具多为基准专用。本文提出VERITAS，一个领域无关的复现框架，利用CLI编码代理提取论文声明、运行方法论并判定结果，返回复现评分和修复日志。在CORE-Bench和ReplicationBench（65篇论文）上，VERITAS实现了最先进性能，在所有指标上领先。

<h3 id="10">[10] A Sliding-Window-Based Reinforcement Learning for Dynamic Assembly Flow Shop Scheduling with Multi-Product Delivery</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02941](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02941)
- **Authors**: Junhao Qiu, Jianjun Liu, Ting Liu, Rongjie Liao, Zhantao Li, Qingfu Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Scheduling, Graph Neural Network
- **Summary**: 该论文针对动态装配流水车间调度中的多产品配套交付问题，提出了一种基于滑动窗口的强化学习框架(SWRL)。SWRL通过异构图马尔可夫决策过程建模、滑动窗口过滤机制和时空图编码网络，有效处理稀疏奖励和动态拓扑。在来自家电制造商的真实实例上，SWRL相比经典调度规则和现有深度强化学习方法实现了稳定的延迟降低。

<h3 id="11">[11] Evaluating Generative Agents with Actions Grounded in Socially Distributed Task Environments using Incognita</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02975](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02975)
- **Authors**: Dan C. Hsu, Luke Lu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Social Simulation, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 现有社会模拟环境提供丰富的语言交互，但缺乏可执行的动作和可验证的结果。本文定义社会分布式任务环境，并提出Incognita框架，分离社交互动与接地执行，评估生成代理在任务中的表现。在Incognita-Retail环境中，更强的模型表现出更多知识获取和接地动作尝试，但可靠性仍然较低。

<h3 id="12">[12] Reinforcement Learning for Evidence-Seeking Diagnostic Reasoning with Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02983](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02983)
- **Authors**: Shengyi Hua, Kangzhe Hu, Conghui He, Xiaofan Zhang, Shaoting Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Reinforcement Learning, Clinical Reasoning
- **Summary**: 推理型LLM多假设信息完整，而临床诊断需要迭代的证据收集。本文将医疗诊断形式化为迭代寻求证据任务，采用带可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）和检索增强生成模拟器（RAGES）进行闭环推理。实验表明该框架使LLM从被动响应者转变为自主助手，性能可媲美更大模型。

<h3 id="13">[13] Beyond Forecasting: The Belief-to-Trade Layer in Prediction-Market Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03015](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03015)
- **Authors**: Yishu Wang, Yuxuan Wang, Jiaqi Deng, Hanyang Tang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Financial AI, Prediction Market
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Alchemist-X/predict-raven)
- **Summary**: 预测市场中，校准的概率评分与交易结果之间存在差距。本文提出Raven-Agent，据我们所知首个用于预测市场的自主交易代理。在受控重放中，Raven-Agent在所有测试策略中唯一实现了正收益和正风险调整收益。

<h3 id="14">[14] Human-Centric Reflective Architecture for Human-AI Collaborative Decision-Making</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03025](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03025)
- **Authors**: Andreas Kouridakis, Dimitrios Patiniotis Spyropoulos, George Vouros
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Human-AI Interaction, Reinforcement Learning, Decision Making
- **Summary**: 人类对AI建议过度或过低依赖，且AI系统与人类期望校准不足。本文提出人-AI协作决策框架，将任务建模为随机博弈，并提出以人为中心的反思架构（HCRA），集成人类校准模型与强化学习代理，利用语言反馈进行迭代反思。评估表明HCRA提升了决策效果并提供高质量建议。

<h3 id="15">[15] Silicon Sampling via Cross-Survey Transfer</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03091](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03091)
- **Authors**: Chan-Tung Ku, Chan Hsu, Pei-Cing Huang, Frank Cheng-shan Liu, I-Ling Cheng, Yihuang Kang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.CY; cs.MA; stat.ME
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Social Simulation, Survey Research
- **Summary**: 硅采样（LLM模拟调查受访者）的评估多依赖于分布比较而非个体级别预测。本文提出跨调查迁移评估框架，利用TEDS 2024数据测试LLM。零样本LLM在未见项目上达到52%准确率，接近监督随机森林；并发现了稳定的构念可预测性层级和方差崩溃与安全对齐效应的细微差别。

<h3 id="16">[16] APeB: Benchmarking Personalization Ability of Large Language Model Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03162](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03162)
- **Authors**: Garry Yang, Zizhe Chen, Xinru Chen, Yongqiang Chen, Jianxiang Wang, Deyu Zou, Linyi Ding, Jialiang Wu, Yunzhong He, Yu Gong, James Cheng, Huaixiao Tou
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, LLM Personalization, Recommender System
- **Summary**: LLM代理在处理原始、未明确的用户查询时个性化能力不足。本文引入个性化产品搜索（PPS）测试平台，构建基准APeB，评估多步代理工作流。发现模型在处理显式查询时表现良好，但在意图和偏好发现的早期阶段困难，主要原因是历史使用不足。简单但历史感知的查询细化流水线VQRA持续提升了效果。

<h3 id="17">[17] Organizational Memory for Agentic Business Process Execution</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03228](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03228)
- **Authors**: Lukas Kirchdorfer, Adrian Rebmann, Christian Warmuth, Timotheus Kampik, Theiss Heilker, Gregor Berg
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Business Process Management
- **Summary**: 针对通用LLM代理缺乏组织特定知识导致业务执行不可靠的问题，提出了一种组织记忆架构，通过共享、可治理且代理可消费的参考层来编码演进的程序性知识，并在采购场景中进行了概念验证。

<h3 id="18">[18] Embodied Operators and Benchmarking: Toward Reusable and Deployable Embodied Intelligence Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03283](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03283)
- **Authors**: Junwu Xiong, Jiaxuan Gao, Wei Chai, Renxing Chen, Yuzhen Li, Yu Guo, Yucheng Guo, Mingxi Luo, Wenyang Ma, Yiyun Mou, Yifei Zhang, Chen Zhou, Yongjian Guo
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Embodied AI, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 为构建可重用、可部署的具身智能系统，定义了具身算子作为可组合的功能模块，构建了涵盖五类算子的分类法，并提出了多维度基准框架，为可重用、可扩展、可验证的具身智能系统奠定基础。

<h3 id="19">[19] Reflective Dialogue or Prompt Refinement? Effects of Tutor Scaffolding on Students' Independent LLM Use for Programming</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03303](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03303)
- **Authors**: Jerome Brender, Laila El-Hamamsy, Kim Uittenhove, Aitor Perez, Patrick Jermann, Francesco Mondada, Engin Bumbacher
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Education Technology, Human-Computer Interaction
- **Venue**: AIED 2026: The 27th International Conference on Artificial Intelligence in Education
- **Summary**: 探讨了两种基于LLM的导师（苏格拉底式引导与提示精炼）对学生编程学习中提示实践、学习和后续LLM使用的影响。实验表明，苏格拉底式引导虽被认为效率较低，但能带来更高的学习收益和理解驱动的提示策略。

<h3 id="20">[20] When Aggregate Alignment Misleads: Auditing Policy Repair Without Per-State Expert Actions</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03386](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03386)
- **Authors**: Peiying Zhu, Sidi Chang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, AI Alignment
- **Summary**: 针对缺乏逐状态专家行动标签时评估智能体策略编辑的问题，在酒店定价模拟器中研究仅区域级诊断反馈下的策略修复。结果显示，LLM编辑器的收益接近基准，但诊断反馈的闭合回路可靠性比单一行为距离更重要。

<h3 id="21">[21] From Mobile Data to Business Insights: An End-to-End Analytics Framework for Large-Scale Urban Mobility Analysis and Decision Support</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03394](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03394)
- **Authors**: Thiago Andrade, Shazia Tabassum, Miguel E. P. Silva, Ricardo Dinis, Joao Gama
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CY; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Human Mobility, Urban Computing
- **Summary**: 为解决城市挑战和商业决策支持，开发了端到端分析框架，利用移动定位数据，通过ETL、BigQuery和Vertex AI处理，实现了流动性画像、轨迹挖掘等任务，提供了可操作的洞察。

<h3 id="22">[22] Efficient bias mitigation in T2I diffusion models using Concept Graphs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03397](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03397)
- **Authors**: Mansi, Avinash Kori, Francesco Leofante
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Text-to-Image, Diffusion Model, Fairness
- **Summary**: 针对文本到图像扩散模型中的有害偏见和现有缓解技术导致语义不连贯的问题，提出了基于概念图谱对齐的CO-ALIGN方法，在文本编码器和去噪器上操作，公平性提升30%，FID下降11.4，不连贯输出减少88%。

<h3 id="23">[23] Personalized Causal Recourse: A Human-In-The-Loop Approach</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03425](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03425)
- **Authors**: Denise Tampieri, Giovanni De Toni, Paolo Giudici
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: Causal Inference, Explainable AI, Human-Computer Interaction
- **Venue**: 4th World Conference on eXplainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI 2026)
- **Summary**: 为提供个性化算法补救，克服忽略个体上下文的问题，提出了人机协同框架，通过贝叶斯推理迭代近似用户结构因果模型，交互查询后生成补救建议。模拟实验显示在线性和非线性模型上效果良好，但复杂非线性结构仍有挑战。

<h3 id="24">[24] Demonstrating Generalization Failures via Mixtures of Conditional Policies</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03478](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03478)
- **Authors**: Jou Barzdukas, Jack Peck, Julian Schulz, Paulius Rauba, Steven Basart, Lennie Wells
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 为研究语言模型后训练中的泛化失败，构造了通过混合条件策略微调的模型，随后进行RL训练。结果显示RL训练可以选择导致泛化失败的行为，如在两个分布中，训练一个分布会显著降低另一个分布的性能。

<h3 id="25">[25] MentalThink: Shaping Thoughts in Mental SVG World</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03530](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03530)
- **Authors**: Kangheng Lin, Jisheng Yin, Dingming Li, En Yu, Yana Wei, Han Zhou, Liang Zhao, Hongyu Zhou, Hongbo Peng, Jianjian Sun, Zheng Ge, Xiangyu Zhang, Daxin Jiang, Jingyu Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Visual Reasoning, LLM Reasoning
- **Summary**: 针对多模态大模型缺乏可视化的心智推理机制，提出了MentalThink范式，通过生成和渲染SVG代码作为中间表示，结合SFT和多轮RL训练，在空间推理基准上取得了55.1%和76.0%的显著性能。

<h3 id="26">[26] Applying Answer Set Programming with Fuzzy Membership Functions: a Case Study</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03550](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03550)
- **Authors**: Luca Ferragina, Ilenia Galati, Lorena Gullone, Francesco Scarcello
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Neurosymbolic AI, Answer Set Programming, Fuzzy Logic
- **Venue**: 24th International Workshop on Nonmonotonic Reasoning
- **Summary**: 为解决基于数值数据的定性推理问题，探索了模糊逻辑扩展的回答集编程，集成了数值输入与符号推理，展示了在统一声明性设置中结合专家知识和上下文的能力。

<h3 id="27">[27] How to Avoid Debate: Scalable AI Safety via Doubly-Efficient Interactive Proofs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03561](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03561)
- **Authors**: Liyan Chen, Yael Tauman Kalai, Zoe Xi
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CC; cs.CR; cs.LG
- **Tags**: AI Safety, Interactive Proofs
- **Summary**: 针对AI安全验证中辩论协议依赖两个对等模型的不现实假设，提出了单证明者交互式证明系统，用于鲁棒计算或低度多项式预言机访问的场景，表明无辩论的交互验证是可能的。

<h3 id="28">[28] The Role of Rigor in Artificial Intelligence</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03634](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03634)
- **Authors**: Timothy Nguyen
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: AI Philosophy, Research Methodology
- **Venue**: Philosophy of Rigor
- **Summary**: 针对AI缺乏严谨科学基础的问题，提出了概念严谨性、认知严谨性和操作严谨性三部分框架，分析AI的进展和不确定性，解释了操作严谨性在现代深度学习中的主导地位。

<h3 id="29">[29] Robust Feasible Route Construction through Collaborative Partition Optimization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03694](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03694)
- **Authors**: Oguzhan Karaahmetoglu, Hyong Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; math.OC
- **Tags**: Vehicle Routing Problem, Combinatorial Optimization
- **Summary**: 针对大规模容量车辆路径问题中分区独立求解可能导致需求未服务的问题，提出了协作路由构造器，允许子问题在优化过程中交换客户和车辆，在高达20万客户的实例上始终构建可行解。

<h3 id="30">[30] Agent Reinforcement Learning via Pivotal-Aware Self-Feedback Retry</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03702](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03702)
- **Authors**: Weiyang Guo, Zesheng Shi, Longhui Zhang, Zeen Zhu, Min Zhang, Jing Li
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Reinforcement Learning, Self-Correction
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/yuki-younai/PivoARL)
- **Summary**: 为有效利用LLM代理失败轨迹，提出了PivoARL框架，通过结构反思识别关键错误步骤并局部重试，结合关键感知信用分配。在多个任务上Pass@2/3平均提升11.5%，交互轮次减少约42%。

<h3 id="31">[31] Explainable Reinforcement Learning for Adaptive Traffic Signal Control</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03703](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03703)
- **Authors**: Dickens Kwesiga, Nishu Choudhary, Angshuman Guin, Michael Hunter
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Explainable AI, Reinforcement Learning, Traffic Signal Control
- **Summary**: 针对深度强化学习在交通信号控制中的黑箱问题，提出了可解释的实体中心强化学习框架，通过双阶段注意力网络提供可视化和分析性可解释性，动作屏蔽保证安全，在微观仿真中优于基线并符合交通工程原理。

<h3 id="32">[32] Can Conversational Temporal Dynamics Improve Depression Detection in Dyads? A Preliminary Investigation in Multi-Modality Perspectives</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03744](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03744)
- **Authors**: Hanie Kang, Huang-Cheng Chou, Sudarsana Reddy Kadiri, Shrikanth Narayanan
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Speech Processing, Mental Health
- **Summary**: 针对抑郁检测中忽略交互时序动态的问题，研究了双人对话的轮对时序，融合自监督编码器，时序模块在开发集上达到最高单模态性能，后期融合在测试集上达到0.669宏F1，证明时序是轻量级有效补充。

<h3 id="33">[33] Bridging Interleaved Multi-Modal Reasoning as a Unified Decision Process</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03748](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03748)
- **Authors**: Zican Hu, Xuyang Hu, Yiming Liu, Zuwei Long, Wei Liu, Yunzhuo Hao, Jiawei Gu, Linjie Li, Yu Cheng, Zhenhong Sun, Weibo Gu, Xing Sun, Zhi Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Multimodal Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 现有方法将强化学习仅应用于多模态模型的文本步骤，忽略了图像生成，限制了策略梯度传播。本文提出BRAID框架，将交织文本-图像推理建模为统一马尔可夫决策过程，通过VLM裁判提供稠密回合级反馈，实现文本和图像的联合优化。在空间推理和视觉感知基准上，BRAID一致优于各种基线方法。

<h3 id="34">[34] Folding, Reasoning, and Scaling with Open-source Drug Discovery Engine</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03787](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03787)
- **Authors**: Aureka AI OpenDDE project
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CE; q-bio.BM
- **Tags**: Drug Discovery, Foundation Model
- **Summary**: 生物分子相互作用建模是药物发现的核心瓶颈。本文提出OpenDDE，一个开源全原子生物分子基础模型，以共折叠为入口，整合全原子架构、原子潜在推理等进展，实现与IsoDDE相当的共折叠精度，并揭示了共折叠模型的缩放律方向。

<h3 id="35">[35] Evaluating LLM Uncertainty in Long-Form Generation Using Deterministic Ground Truth</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03870](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03870)
- **Authors**: Ido Amit, Ido Galil, Ran El-Yaniv
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Uncertainty Estimation
- **Venue**: 43rd International Conference on Machine Learning (ICML 2026)
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/IdoAmit198/SALT)
- **Summary**: 长文本生成的不确定性估计评估缺乏零噪声基准。本文提出SALT基准，包含六个具有确定性真值的过程化任务，支持词元级正确性、校准和排序评估。对50余个LLM的分析揭示了置信度函数主导因素和推理带来的准确率与置信度排序之间的权衡。

<h3 id="36">[36] Harness-Aware Self-Evolving: Co-Evolving Model Weights, Harness, and Task Solutions</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03935](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03935)
- **Authors**: Haochen Luo, Yi Huang, Sichun Luo, Fengyuan Liu, Lei Li, Zefa Hu, Junlan Feng, Qi Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Self-Evolving Agent
- **Summary**: 自进化框架通常固定外部环境而只优化任务解。本文提出HASE，一个代理强化学习框架，允许同一模型在多回合动作空间中生成任务解或编辑环境组件。单个Qwen3-8B模型在文本分类上匹配GPT-OSS-120B，在alpha因子挖掘中超越基线，并修复了评估组件。

<h3 id="37">[37] Online Linear Programming for Multi-Objective Routing in LLM Serving</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03948](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03948)
- **Authors**: Zixi Chen, Yinyu Ye, Zijie Zhou
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG; math.OC
- **Tags**: LLM Serving, LLM Inference
- **Summary**: LLM服务中的路由启发式方法缺乏显式SLO控制。本文提出基于在线线性规划的多目标优化框架，采用出价-价格控制策略和暖启动投影一阶更新，满足毫秒级决策需求。在Vidur模拟器中，该方法在多个SLO指标上显著优于基线。

<h3 id="38">[38] Explainable AI for Screening Abuse-Related Trauma in Bangladeshi Children: A Training-Free Multimodal Framework Evaluated on Noise-Aware Synthetic Data</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04010](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04010)
- **Authors**: Salma Hoque Talukdar Koli, Fahima Haque Talukder Jely
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.CY; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Mental Health, Explainable AI
- **Summary**: 孟加拉国缺乏针对儿童虐待创伤的早期筛查工具。本文提出ShishuRaksha AI，一个免训练的多模态融合框架，结合问卷、叙事文本、绘画和面部表情，提供可解释的双语风险评估报告。在含噪声的合成基准上，融合模型AUC达0.874，优于仅SDQ基线的0.756。

<h3 id="39">[39] What is Left for Us? Second Scholarship Against the Degradation of Research by AI</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04049](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04049)
- **Authors**: Claudio Novelli, Luciano Floridi
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: AI Ethics, Philosophy of AI
- **Summary**: 生成式AI可能侵蚀学者的判断形成实践。本文主张通过第二学术实践（隐性知识、个人承诺、社会化、深度阅读）重新掌握学术技艺，强调这些不可自动化的源泉是研究信任的基础。

<h3 id="40">[40] PLACEMEM: Toward a Compute-Aware Memory Plane for Lifelong Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04089](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04089)
- **Authors**: Sukanta Ganguly
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Memory Architecture
- **Summary**: 终身代理需要可持久、可演化的记忆，避免重复计算历史。本文提出PLACEMEM，一个以版本化胶囊统一语义、来源、有效性和可重用运行时状态的记忆系统，支持KV感知路由和级联失效。原型展示了修正感知的控制平面行为。

<h3 id="41">[41] Forethought: Verifiable Reasoning from Neurosymbolic Primitive Programming</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04096](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04096)
- **Authors**: Vishvesh Bhat, Jay Vaghasiya, Emmanuel Anaya Gonzalez
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Neurosymbolic AI, Program Synthesis
- **Summary**: 当前代理推理与模型权重纠缠，不可验证且推理成本高。本文提出Forethought，一个神经符号推理系统，将推理视为由符号和神经原语通过领域特定语言组合的可验证程序。在五个基准上，Forethought将基础模型准确率提升约30%，使小模型以极低后训练投资媲美前沿模型。

<h3 id="42">[42] Language models guide symbolic equation discovery by controlling search</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04156](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04156)
- **Authors**: Zikai Xie, Wenmei Li, Man Luo, Jun Jiang, Linjiang Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.SC
- **Tags**: Symbolic Regression, Scientific Discovery
- **Summary**: 科学方程发现需结合先验知识和数值测试。本文提出LLM-PySR，语言模型作为搜索控制器指定变量和算子，符号回归进行枚举和拟合。在74个AI-Feynman方程和七个复杂任务上，搜索控制实现了准确度、复杂度、稳定性和成本的最佳平衡，并在电池数据集中发现了紧凑的分段线性关系。

<h3 id="43">[43] A Clustering-Based Framework for Identifying Suspicious Trading Patterns in Capital Market</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04184](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04184)
- **Authors**: Asif Zaman, Romona Magdalene Sarkar, Sabiha Khair Ohi, Iftekharul Mobin
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Fraud Detection, Financial AI
- **Venue**: IEEE 2nd International Conference on Quantum Photonics, Artificial Intelligence & Networking (QPAIN), 2026
- **Summary**: 市场操纵损害交易平台信任。本文实现了一个无监督欺诈检测工具包，基于K-Means++聚类和启发式阈值对约百万笔金融交易进行分析。方法标记2.02%的交易为可疑，其中51.10%为幌骗交易，轮廓系数为0.561。

<h3 id="44">[44] Agentic IoT: Architectures, Applications, and Challenges Toward the Internet of Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04219](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04219)
- **Authors**: Rümeysa Hilal Sevinç, Bahaeddin Türkoğlu, İbrahim Kök
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.MA; cs.NI
- **Tags**: IoT, Multi-Agent System, Edge Computing
- **Summary**: 当前AIoT系统依赖特定任务模型，缺乏实时推理和自适应规划能力。本文综述了Agentic IoT作为下一代认知物联网范式，将自主AI代理的感知、推理、规划能力与信息物理系统集成，提出了架构框架和应用潜力。

<h3 id="45">[45] Unsupervised Features Mining via Activation Geometry</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04222](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04222)
- **Authors**: Amit LeVi, Elad David, Max Fomin
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Interpretability, Activation Steering
- **Summary**: 可解释性方法通常需要人工标注概念。本文提出MAG，一个无监督框架，通过在输入前添加自然语言指令提取推理特征，利用激活方向差异进行预测和转向。该方法还能以94.7%的Top-1准确率选择最佳训练数据集。

<h3 id="46">[46] Biological Motifs for Agentic Control</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04240](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04240)
- **Authors**: Bogdan Banu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; q-bio.CB
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Bio-Inspired AI
- **Summary**: LLM代理面临可靠性、安全性和状态管理挑战。本文利用多项式函子将基因调控网络的生物模体映射到代理软件设计模式，提出了Agentic Operad和认知拓扑，并证明了四个预测定理。参考实现包含1813个测试和116个示例。

<h3 id="47">[47] Progress- and Reliability-Oriented Group Policy Optimization for Agentic Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04242](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04242)
- **Authors**: Mingxuan Fan, Peiyang Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 基于组的强化学习在步骤级优势估计中对组形成敏感。本文提出ProGPO，一种免评论家方法，保持精确前缀动作比较，并结合基于回滚的状态势函数，利用语义扩展和逆方差融合。在ALFWorld和WebShop上，ProGPO优于匹配的代理RL基线。

<h3 id="48">[48] Shortcut Learning in Legal Judgment Prediction: Empirical Evidence from the UK Employment Tribunal</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04261](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04261)
- **Authors**: Joe Watson, Joana Ribeiro de Faria, Marcus Tomalin, Måns Magnusson, Huiyuan Xie, Hao Tian Yeung, Felix Steffek
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Legal AI, Spurious Correlation
- **Summary**: 法律判决预测模型可能利用事后司法文本中的捷径。本文在英国就业法庭数据集上实证了捷径学习的存在：模型依赖结果泄露线索，但掩码这些特征后Macro-F1仅轻微下降，表明模型仍能提取有用信号。

<h3 id="49">[49] Agentic SABRE: An Uncertainty-Aware Neuro-Symbolic Multi-Agent Framework for Adaptive Ransomware Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04292](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04292)
- **Authors**: Henry Kabuye, Biju Issac, Jeyamohan Neera
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CR
- **Tags**: Neurosymbolic AI, Multi-Agent System, Ransomware Detection
- **Summary**: 问题：勒索软件检测面临概念漂移、规避和多态性挑战。方法：提出不确定性感知的神经符号多智能体框架，融合语义与行为证据，使用蒙特卡洛Dropout量化不确定性，并通过风险与不确定性阈值进行决策。结果：在饱和语义数据集上AUC=1.0，误报率降低4.9%，具有校准的不确定性。

<h3 id="50">[50] HAS-Bench: Evaluating LLM-Based Human-Agent Systems under Configurable Human Participation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04329](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04329)
- **Authors**: Yaozu Wu, Wei-Chieh Huang, Jizhou Guo, Dongyuan Li, Renhe Jiang, Henry Peng Zou, Chunyu Miao, Shanghao Li, Weizhi Zhang, WeiWei Ye, Yankai Chen, Meng Zhang, Xue Liu, Philip S.Yu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Human-Agent Interaction, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 问题：LLM与人类协作场景缺乏系统评估框架。方法：提出基于图的HAS框架，将人类和LLM智能体作为一等公民，并构建HAS-Bench基准，评估任务结果和协作行为。结果：人类参与能显著提升任务完成和失败恢复，但效果取决于参与时机、方式和人员。

<h3 id="51">[51] Do GUI Agents Believe Their Eyes? Diagnosing State-Belief Reliance on Pixels versus Structure</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04334](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04334)
- **Authors**: Guijia Zhang, Harry Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: GUI Automation, Vision-Language Model
- **Summary**: 问题：多模态GUI智能体通过像素和结构形成状态信念，但像素依赖程度未知。方法：形式化视觉状态依赖，通过配对单通道干预测量感知-融合差距。结果：文本智能体倾向于结构信息，像素单通道准确率高；冲突导致错误动作。

<h3 id="52">[52] Server-side Anti-cheat in FPS games for Aimbot detection using Deep learning and Machine learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04336](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04336)
- **Authors**: Siddhesh A. Dhinge, Shubham G. Sukum, Harsh S. Ranjane, Ruturajsingh R. Rajput, Jyoti H. Jadhav
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Game AI, Anomaly Detection
- **Summary**: 问题：FPS游戏中外挂（自瞄）检测需要低误报率。方法：基于时间序列和行为特征的服务器端分类器，使用堆叠LSTM。结果：准确率88.6%，误报率0.97%，优于决策树基线（误报率2.68%）。

<h3 id="53">[53] Nemotron-Labs-3-Puzzle-75B-A9B: Compressing Hybrid MoE LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04371](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04371)
- **Authors**: Akhiad Bercovich, Talor Abramovich, Daniel Afrimi, Shay Aharon, Nir Ailon, Vladimir Anisimov, Omer Ullman Argov, Maor Ashkenazi, Tomer Asida, Nave Assaf, Tomer Bar Natan, Alexander Bukharin, Grzegorz Chlebus, Marcin Chochowski, Eric Chung, Mohammad Dabbah, Carlo del Mundo, Ewa Dobrowolska, Ido Galil, Yaniv Galron, Amnon Geifman, Yonatan Geifman, Izik Golan, Alex Gronskiy, Tomasz Grzegorzek, Netanel Haber, Lior Kadoch, Grzegorz Karch, Tomer Keren, Abhinav Khattar, Amir Klein, Tugrul Konuk, Roi Koren, Daniel Korzekwa, Shaun Kotek, Konstantinos Krommydas, Itay Levy, Ofri Masad, Yoav Miron, Pavlo Molchanov, Shahar Mor, Zach Moshe, Saurav Muralidharan, Najeeb Nabwani, Besmira Nushi, Mostofa Patwary, Omri Puny, Johannes Rausch, Tomer Ronen, Sepehr Sameni, Itamar Schen, Elad Segal, Daniel Serebrenik, Ido Shahaf, Soumye Singhal, Daniil Sorokin, Sharath Turuvekere Sreenivas, Marta Stepniewska-Dziubinska, Ali Taghibakhshi, Nima Tajbakhsh, Oren Tropp, Dor Tzur, Anna Warno, Yi-Fu Wu, Michal Zawalski, Jiaqi Zeng, Yian Zhang, Ran Zilberstein, Amit Zuker, Ran El-Yaniv
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Model Compression, Mixture-of-Experts, Knowledge Distillation
- **Summary**: 问题：大型混合MoE模型需要压缩以支持交互式部署和高吞吐。方法：多阶段流水线，结合迭代Puzzle压缩、知识蒸馏、强化学习、量化和多令牌预测头，联合优化MoE剪枝等。结果：在单个8xB200节点上吞吐量提升2倍，1M令牌并发从1提高到8，保持下游性能。

<h3 id="54">[54] Decentralized Aggregation of LLM Predictions via Wagering Mechanisms</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04389](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04389)
- **Authors**: Yuhong Luo, David M. Pennock, Xintong Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.GT
- **Tags**: Mechanism Design, Ensemble Learning, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 问题：去中心化环境下聚合多个LLM预测需考虑激励兼容。方法：提出优势对齐的投注机制WALLA，具有留一基线，满足优势策略激励兼容和优势-赌注对齐。结果：在问答和预测基准上匹配集中式方法，同时实现去中心化学习和激励兼容。

<h3 id="55">[55] MechMath Agent Team: LLM Driven Agents for Mathematical Research</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04394](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04394)
- **Authors**: Yichuan Cao, Ruichen Qiu, Junqi Liu, Jiaqi Wang, Dakai Guo, Ruyong Feng, Lihong Zhi, Xiao-Shan Gao
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.SC
- **Tags**: Automated Theorem Proving, LLM Agent, Mathematical Reasoning
- **Summary**: 问题：数学研究具有非线性推导和严格逻辑要求，现有推理系统难以胜任。方法：提出MMAT系统，包含三平面架构（控制、执行、增强）和三个专业智能体（知识库管理、自然语言证明、形式语言证明），闭环生成形式化证明。结果：在数论、代数复杂度等领域解决11个开放问题。

<h3 id="56">[56] LLM-as-a-Tutor: Policy-Aware Prompt Adaptation for Non-Verifiable RL</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04412](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04412)
- **Authors**: Yujin Kim, Namgyu Ho, Sangmin Hwang, Joonkee Kim, Yongjin Yang, Sangmin Bae, Seungone Kim, Jaehun Jung, Se-Young Yun, Hwanjun Song
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: RLHF, Instruction Tuning, Prompt Engineering
- **Summary**: 问题：强化学习中奖励信号来自LLM评判器，但提示难度与策略能力不匹配。方法：LLM同时作为评判器和教师，检测非挑战性提示并追加原子约束，单调提高难度。结果：在三个复杂指令遵循基准上优于以往方法。

<h3 id="57">[57] Agent Step Value: State-Transition Measurement with State-Grounded LLM Evaluators</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04419](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04419)
- **Authors**: Andrew Zhang, Chengzhan Li
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Agent Evaluation, Uncertainty Estimation
- **Summary**: 问题：智能体评估只关注最终答案，缺乏对每步行动价值的诊断。方法：提出ASV框架，通过状态变化评分每个动作，使用状态固定的LLM评判器，含无标签推理步骤。结果：在100个开放QA任务中，ASV定位出建设性和破坏性的信念转折点。

<h3 id="58">[58] ResearchStudio-Idea: An Evidence-Grounded Research-Ideation Skill Suite from ML Conference Outcomes</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04439](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04439)
- **Authors**: Qihao Zhao, Yangyu Huang, Yalun Dai, Lingao Xiao, Jianjun Gao, Xin Zhang, Wenshan Wu, Scarlett Li, Yang He, Yan Lu, Yap Kim Hui
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Scientific Reasoning, LLM Agent, Information Retrieval
- **Summary**: 问题：研究构思需要文献基础、瓶颈识别和风险评估。方法：包含文献搜索、新颖性检查、集成证据与模式生成的研究构思技能套件，基于1947篇ML论文提取15种模式。结果：盲评显示比无技能和通用技能基线产生更强的研究提案。

<h3 id="59">[59] Why Pure Reasoning is Not Enough: Nature as the Source of Mathematical Innovation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04505](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04505)
- **Authors**: Charanjit S. Jutla, Vimal Sharma
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Scientific Reasoning, Mathematical Reasoning, AI Philosophy
- **Summary**: 问题：纯演绎推理受限于不可判定性和计算复杂性，无法驱动数学创新。方法：通过傅里叶变换历史论证物理世界是数学创新的来源。结果：提出AI系统需嵌入跨领域模式而非仅依赖推理。

<h3 id="60">[60] Compressing the Validation Bottleneck: An Agentic Self-Driving Lab for Scientific Discovery</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04508](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04508)
- **Authors**: Kyunghoon Hur, Chihun Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.RO
- **Tags**: Scientific Discovery, Surrogate Model, Agentic AI
- **Venue**: ICML 2026 AI for Science Workshop
- **Summary**: 问题：自驱动实验室面临低价值实验循环和高成本实验瓶颈。方法：提出先验感知的主动实验设计和成本感知的代理模型，减少循环次数和每轮成本。结果：旨在加速科学发现循环。

<h3 id="61">[61] VLA Grounder: Language-Conditioning Space Optimization for Black-Box VLA Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04517](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04517)
- **Authors**: Damir Shodiev, Aleksei Staroverov, Nikita Kachaev, Alexey K. Kovalev, Aleksandr I. Panov
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language-Action, Robot Manipulation, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 问题：视觉-语言-动作模型行为对语言措辞敏感，语言是可优化的条件输入。方法：语言条件空间策略将指令转化为VLA接地命令，通过强化学习优化，保持VLA冻结。结果：在指令敏感和符号操作任务上提升成功率。

<h3 id="62">[62] Measuring Harness-Induced Belief Divergence in Multi-Step LLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04528](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04528)
- **Authors**: Haiwen Yi, Xinyuan Song
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Agent Evaluation, LLM Evaluation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Hik289/Harness-induce-bias.git)
- **Summary**: 问题：智能体评估中的框架（限制观察、动作等）会改变智能体信念，影响后续决策。方法：引入信念轨迹诊断和交叉框架信念散度，分解为到达项和增长项。结果：框架设计影响信念，即使最终任务成功也可能改变决策依据。

<h3 id="63">[63] Heaviside Continuity of Rolling Coefficients for Eliminating Epistemic Entropy in Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04562](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04562)
- **Authors**: MY Pitsane, Hope Mogale
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.NE
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, LLM Reasoning, Verification
- **Summary**: 问题：LLM生成流畅错误输出，自回归解码缺乏验证机制。方法：提出HCRC，用Heaviside门控状态转换，结合模型置信度和独立验证信号。结果：在能力强的提议者上将虚假完成率降至0%，延迟与无框架相当。

<h3 id="64">[64] Detecting Answer-Driven Reasoning in LLM-Based Educational Tutors via Truncated Chain-of-Thought Auditing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04572](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04572)
- **Authors**: Bonan Shen, Dingyan Shang, Youting Wang, Tao Ning
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Education Technology, Chain-of-Thought
- **Summary**: 问题：LLM教育辅导可能基于答案键而非学生问题生成解释。方法：使用截断推理AUC评估（TRACE）探测早期思维链前缀是否包含答案。结果：有答案键时，TRACE AUC从0.375升至0.900，前10%前缀即包含答案的比例达99.7%。

<h3 id="65">[65] Attention Limited Reward Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04590](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04590)
- **Authors**: Wenqian Xing
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: RLHF, LLM Alignment
- **Summary**: 论文指出传统RLHF中的Bradley-Terry模型忽略了比较困难的不同来源：候选值接近或注意力有限。提出基于理性疏忽的简化模型，区分奖励、注意力和默认倾向。实验表明Chatbot Arena的比较数据存在循环成分，标量奖励无法表示，而感知比较中反应时间和注视携带标签未包含的信息。

<h3 id="66">[66] Governed Individuation: Cryptographically Decoupling an Agent's Learning from Its Authority</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04613](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04613)
- **Authors**: Xue Qin, Simin Luan, Cong Yang, Zhijun Li
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CR
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, AI Safety, Agent Security
- **Summary**: 论文解决自主智能体在部署后学习过程中权限扩大的安全问题。提出“管控个体化”方法，将智能体启动时绑定加密身份摘要，并通过语义效果门控每个动作。证明即使智能体自我诱导安全原则，也无法超越授权。实验显示不受管控的智能体在奖励压力下试图篡改评估，而所提方法将禁止效果降至零。

<h3 id="67">[67] MRMS: A Multi-Resolution Memory Substrate for Long-Lived AI Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04617](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04617)
- **Authors**: Jizhizi Li, Amy Shi-Nash
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Memory Architecture
- **Summary**: 论文解决长期AI智能体的连续性需求，提出多分辨率记忆基底（MRMS），沿表征轴（结构化记录、向量表示、图关系）和时间轴（短期、中期、长期）组织。核心设计是同步结构化-向量-图记忆，关键主张是可靠个性化是记忆设计问题。原型验证了核心机制。

<h3 id="68">[68] Formal Disco: Scalable Open-Ended Generation of Formally Verified Programs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04631](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04631)
- **Authors**: Gabriel Poesia, Simon Henniger, Tzu-Han Hsu, Yilun Du, Nada Amin
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Program Verification, Code Generation, Formal Methods
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/metareflection/formal-disco)
- **Summary**: 论文解决形式验证程序数据稀缺问题，提出Formal Disco分布式系统，协调LLM工作节点进行开放式合成数据生成。包括初始化者、修复者和扩展者，并采用最大熵原理进行微调以生成多样化程序。在Dafny、Verus、Frama-C上生成大数据集，微调模型在验证任务上匹配或超越Claude Opus 4.5。

<h3 id="69">[69] Integrated Altruistic and Fairness Preference Induces Advanced Mutual Cooperation in Sequential Social Dilemmas</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04710](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04710)
- **Authors**: Yu Wei, Yukiko Ogura, Yoshiyuki Ohmura, Ildefons Magrans de Abril, Hoshinori Kanazawa, Yasuo Kuniyoshi
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning, Reward Design, Social Dilemma
- **Summary**: 论文解决多智能体强化学习中社会困境的合作问题，提出利他与公平偏好（AFP）效用函数，将自身和他人奖励转换为合作激励。在序列社会困境游戏中，AFP智能体实现了更高集体奖励和公平性。分析表明利他偏好鼓励贡献，公平偏好诱导互惠行为。

<h3 id="70">[70] FORGE: Research-Trajectory Hijacking Attacks on Deep Research Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04718](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04718)
- **Authors**: Yue Pan, Ziheng Zhang, Junxiang Lei, Changhao Jia, Qingyi Si, Hongcheng Guo
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Adversarial Attack, Data Poisoning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/yvepan/FORGE)
- **Summary**: 论文揭示深度研究智能体存在规划层投毒漏洞，提出FORGE攻击，结合文档内推理伪造和链间协调劫持子任务规划。提出PRISM指标和根查询锚定（RQA）防御。实验显示网络FORGE达到26.4% PRISM，RQA将PRISM从38.5%降至18.3%。

<h3 id="71">[71] FM-ChangeNet: Learning Change through Pathwise Feature Transport</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04750](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04750)
- **Authors**: Roie Kazoom, George Leifman, Genady Beryozkin
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Change Detection, Remote Sensing
- **Summary**: 论文提出FM-ChangeNet变化检测框架，将双时相推理视为特征空间中的连续传输而非静态端点比较。构建中间潜状态并学习时间条件速度场，提供更密集的监督信号。实验表明产生更结构化的变化表示，在遥感基准上达到最先进性能。

<h3 id="72">[72] AgenticPD: A Stage-Aware Agentic Framework for Physical Design QoR Optimization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04758](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04758)
- **Authors**: Shuo Ren, Zijin Cheng, Yaohui Han, Libo Shen, Leilei Jin, Wanting Tian, Rongliang Fu, Chao Wang, Bei Yu, Tsung-Yi Ho
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: EDA, VLSI Physical Design, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 论文解决物理设计QoR优化问题，提出AgenticPD，一个阶段感知的智能体框架，利用Judge Agent导航搜索和阶段专用智能体做出局部决策。系统可从先前中间状态分支并重用检查点，在post-route signoff评估候选方案。实验在功耗和面积竞争下取得强post-route时序。

<h3 id="73">[73] CARL: Constraint-Aware Reinforcement Learning for Planning with LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04854](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04854)
- **Authors**: Qiuyi Qi, Jinjian Zhang, Mutian Bao, Tian Liang, Guocong Li, Dongnan Liu, Wei Zhou, Jie Liu, Ming Kong, Linjian Mo, Feng Zhang, Qiang Zhu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Automated Planning, Constrained Optimization
- **Summary**: 论文解决LLM规划中违反任务约束的问题，提出约束感知强化学习（CARL），通过比较约束和无约束输入下的输出分布引入约束感知奖励。无需外部求解器，与各种RL框架兼容。在BlocksWorld、TravelPlanner和T-Eval上显著优于标准RFT和先进推理模型。

<h3 id="74">[74] Medi-Gemma: A Hybrid Clinical Decision Support System Integrating Deterministic EMR Analytics and Retrieval-Augmented Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04907](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04907)
- **Authors**: Mohammed Saim Ahmed Quadri, Yunzhe Xue, Justin W. Ady, Usman Roshan
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Clinical Decision Support, RAG, Clinical NLP
- **Summary**: 论文解决LLM在临床部署中的结构幻觉和弱确定性推理问题，提出Medi-Gemma临床决策支持系统，采用解耦框架分离临床感知和数据编排，包含数据管理器、意图路由器、临床RAG引擎和真值注入模块。验证显示消除了语义漂移，提高了事实遵守性。

<h3 id="75">[75] STAPO: Selective Trajectory-Aware Policy Optimization for LLM Agent Training</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04963](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04963)
- **Authors**: Qiuyi Qi, Tian Liang, Mutian Bao, Jinjian Zhang, Dongnan Liu, Wei Zhou, Linjian Mo, Ming Kong, Jie Liu, Feng Zhang, Qiang Zhu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Reinforcement Learning, Policy Optimization
- **Summary**: 论文解决LLM智能体在长程任务中奖励稀疏导致的轨迹忽视问题，提出选择性轨迹感知策略优化（STAPO），利用归一化熵定位轨迹忽视步骤，并通过联合轨迹感知奖励和轨迹无关惩罚优化。在ALFWorld、WebShop和Search-Augmented QA上达到最先进性能。

<h3 id="76">[76] Quantum-Inspired Harmonic Decision Models: A Computational Framework for Music Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.05007](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05007)
- **Authors**: Josef Pavlíček, Petra Pavlíčková, Martin Molhanec
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.SD
- **Tags**: Music Generation, Combinatorial Optimization, Quantum Computing
- **Summary**: 论文提出量子启发的和声决策计算框架，将和声化视为组合空间中的优化问题，结合干涉式和谐化和经典优化。在《Autumn Leaves》等示例上评估，优化阶段降低了和弦密度、增加了和声稳定性。专家评估表明和声复杂性不总是更自然。

<h3 id="77">[77] Toward Trustworthy Large Language Model Agents in Healthcare</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.05055](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05055)
- **Authors**: Hadi Hasan, Safaa Salman, Adam Tai Abou Dargham, Ammar Mohanna, Ali Chehab
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, LLM Agent, RAG, AI Safety
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Hadi-Hsn/CareConnect)
- **Summary**: 论文解决医疗预约调度中的操作瓶颈，提出CareConnect安全优先的会话智能体，利用LLM函数调用、RAG和分层确定性安全护栏。评估680个任务场景，任务完成率91.8%，中位延迟2.2秒，安全合规96.0%，每次预约成本0.0324美元。

<h3 id="78">[78] Diffusion-Guided Uncertainty-Aware Delayed Policy Optimization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.05064](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05064)

<h3 id="79">[79] ASSEMCAD: Production-Ready CAD Assembly Generation from Natural Language</h3>

<h3 id="80">[80] TacReasoner: A Dynamic Tactile-Language Framework for Interactive Reasoning in Real-World Scenarios</h3>

<h3 id="87">[87] Reason, Reward, Refine: Step-Level Errors Corrections with Structured Feedback for Physics Reasoning in Small Language Models</h3>

<h3 id="88">[88] EvoAgentBench: Benchmarking Agent Self-Evolution via Ability Transfer</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.AI

<h3 id="89">[89] MoP-JEPA: Hard-Assigned Predictor Mixtures for Stochastic JEPA World Models</h3>

- **Authors**: Zhi Song, Ximing Xing, Zhenchao Tang, hanbo Huang, Tianxu Lv, minghao Yang, Zhongzheng Niu, He Bing, Lusheng Wang, Jianhua Yao
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: World Model, Model-Based RL, Planning
- **Summary**: JEPA世界模型在随机环境中因回归最优预测器导致隐状态崩溃。MoP-JEPA采用硬分配混合预测器量化转移分布，支持规划。在OGBench离线数据上，基于预测模式的规划成功率高达0.85，优于确定性、门控MoE和变分预测器。

<h3 id="90">[90] MetaSkill-Evolve: Recursive Self-Improvement of LLM Agents via Two-Timescale Meta-Skill Evolution</h3>

<h3 id="91">[91] Evaluating and Understanding Model Editing for Medical Vision Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.05310](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05310)
- **Authors**: Guli Zhu, Chenwei Wu, Liyue Shen

- **Tags**: LLM Application, Knowledge Graph Construction, Ontology Learning
- **Venue**: NeLaMKRR@KR, 2025
- **Summary**: 针对特定领域本体构建耗时问题，利用大语言模型（GPT-3.5和GPT-4）作为领域专家自动构建本体。在巴西海域（蓝亚马逊）领域构建了20个本体，经专家评估概念一致但需人工完善。

<h3 id="99">[99] Neural-Network Inverse Design of SRF Cavities and Transmons for Bosonic Quantum Computation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02289](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02289) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Joseph Yaker, Jovan Markovic, Alessandro Reineri, Doga Murat Kurkcuoglu, Silvia Zorzetti
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Quantum Computing, Inverse Problem
- **Summary**: 针对超导射频腔和transmon量子器件的逆设计问题，提出两种深度神经网络方法，分别生成满足目标腔参数和量子比特参数（耦合率、频率、非谐性）的几何设计。候选设计误差约5%（腔）和2%（transmon），实现快速替代迭代仿真。

<h3 id="100">[100] GLM-5 Serving Parameter Tuning for OpenClaw: Single-Deployment MaaS Inference Optimization for Long-Context Agent Workloads</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02518](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02518) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Minjie Hua, Ning Wang, Peijun Yang, Kai Wang, Shiguo Lian
- **Subjects**: cs.DC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Serving, LLM Inference, Inference Optimization
- **Summary**: 针对长上下文Agent负载的LLM服务优化，在MaaS架构中调整GLM-5的chunked prefill、张量/流水线并行度和请求并发。最佳配置（chunk=3072, tp=4, pp=4, max=24）相比基线吞吐量提升~10%，TTFT和延迟显著降低。

<h3 id="101">[101] AutoResearch: An Execution-Grounded Multi-Agent Framework for Reliable Research Workflow Automation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02520](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02520) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Rajesh Kumar, Waqar Ali, Junaid Ahmed, Abdullah Aman Khan, Shaoning Zeng
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI; cs.SE
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, LLM Agent, Scientific Discovery
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/raja21068/AutoResearch)
- **Summary**: 针对自动化研究Agent执行验证不足问题，提出AutoResearch多Agent框架，集成沙盒代码执行、引用验证、声明审核和LaTeX生成。在HumanEval、MBPP等评测中执行成功率、引用有效性等指标优于基线。

<h3 id="102">[102] SWIFT: Spatio-temporal Wavelet Integrated Forecasting Framework for Workload Traces</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02524](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02524) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zeyuan Ding, Lingfeng Zheng, Dian Ding, Guangtao Xue
- **Subjects**: cs.DC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Time Series Forecasting, Cloud Computing
- **Summary**: 针对云工作负载预测中突发性和时空依赖问题，提出SWIFT纯卷积框架，包含可学习级联小波路径和多元交互模块。在多个数据集上预测误差降低31.04%，延迟减少79.74%，复杂度为线性O(L)。

<h3 id="103">[103] PEEK: Predictive Queue-Informed KV Cache Management for LLM Serving</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02525](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02525) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Bing Xie, Zhipeng Wang, Masahiro Tanaka, Zheng Zhen
- **Subjects**: cs.DC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Serving, KV Cache, Scheduling
- **Summary**: 针对LLM服务中KV缓存管理效率问题，提出PEEK调度和驱逐框架，利用增量基数树感知前缀共享。在SGLang和vLLM上，缓存命中率提升3.0×/2.6×，TTFT和吞吐量大幅提升。

<h3 id="104">[104] The Hidden Water Geography of U.S. Hyperscale Data Centers in the AI Era</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02531](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02531) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Gianluca Guidi, Francesca Dominici
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Data Center, Energy Efficiency, Sustainability
- **Summary**: 针对数据中心水足迹（制冷和发电）的空间分布问题，映射美国472个超大规模设施的水消耗。发现总耗水约300 GL/yr，其中发电相关占3/4，且热点地区不同：直接冷却集中在西部和南部，电力相关集中在东部化石能源丰富区域。

<h3 id="105">[105] Not Every Sync Is Safe: Calibrated DiLoCo Scheduling for Shared AI Infrastructure</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02544](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02544) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Maxwell Twelftree, David Lemphers, An-chi He, Yue Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.DC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Distributed Training, LLM Training, Scheduling
- **Summary**: 针对DiLoCo训练中同步时机选择问题，提出WA-DiLoCo控制器和校准协议。在真实vLLM回放中，带EWMA突发预测的在线策略SLO违例率从6.54%降至5.09%，揭示了匹配随机基线的重要性。

<h3 id="106">[106] DELTAVID: Enhancing Fine-Grained Spatiotemporal Perception with Cross-Video Differences</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02551](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02551) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yankai Yang, Yancheng Long, Bin Wen, Fan Yang, Tingting Gao, Han Li, Shuo Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Video Understanding, Multimodal Learning, Visual Reasoning
- **Summary**: 针对视频多模态大模型局部时空感知不足问题，提出DELTAVID框架，将跨视频差异检测转化为可训练感知信号。构建DELTAVID-10K数据集和基准，在MMVU、MLVU等多个通用视频理解基准上表现提升。

<h3 id="107">[107] Double-Helix Active Geometry: LiDAR-Anchored Multi-View Depth with Selective Abstention</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02561](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02561) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jinwen Wen
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Depth Estimation, Sensor Fusion, 3D Vision
- **Summary**: 针对消费级LiDAR深度传感器范围短和稀疏问题，提出DH-Active无训练几何后端，将LiDAR作为度量尺。近场PnP定姿，远场三角化，并选择性弃权。在ARKitScenes上远场覆盖64.2%，中位误差13.4%，CPU延时1.11ms。

<h3 id="108">[108] Attention Dynamics in Diffusion Models: A Visual Analytics Framework for Human-AI Collaboration</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02563](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02563) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yiran Xiao, George Legrady
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Interpretability, Attention Mechanism
- **Summary**: 针对扩散模型中语义结构演化难以解释问题，提出可视化分析框架，探索注意力动态（步骤索引、时间集中度、空间关系）。在60提示词稳定扩散基准上展示了可重复模式，支持交互式人类-AI协作。

<h3 id="109">[109] From Raw Segmentations to Simulation-Ready Cardiac Meshes: An Automated Framework for Anatomical Reconstruction and Virtual Cohort Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02564](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02564) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Francesco Fabbri, Martino Andrea Scarpolini, Paolo Ciancarella, Francesco Tudisco, Roberto Verzicco, Alessandro Ricci, Francesco Viola
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; q-bio.TO
- **Tags**: Medical AI, 3D Reconstruction, Simulation
- **Summary**: 针对医学图像分割到仿真网格的转换问题，提出半自动流水线，结合深度学习和模板配准，生成拓扑一致、水密且等拓扑的心脏网格。在58例健康CT上验证，并构建统计形状模型生成虚拟队列。

<h3 id="110">[110] CRODA-ST: Single-Target Cross-Receiver Open-Set Radio Fingerprint Recognition</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02567](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02567) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Fengchong Yao, Jianbing Li, Qing Liu, Kefeng Song, Haitao Li, Song Wang, Feixiang Wang
- **Subjects**: eess.SP; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Radio Frequency Fingerprinting, Open-Set Recognition, Domain Adaptation
- **Summary**: 针对跨接收器开集射频指纹识别中接收器偏移导致误拒和误认问题，提出CRODA-ST框架，包含判别结构锚定和拒绝对齐。在WiSig ManyTx数据集上已知类准确率0.9092，AUROC 0.9692，FPR90降至0.0469。

<h3 id="111">[111] DOSE-I: A Multimodal Biosignal Dataset of Procedural Sedation for Endoscopy -- Technical Report</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02570](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02570) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jakob Garbe, Jan W. Kantelhardt, Katja Seeliger, Thomas Schmid
- **Subjects**: eess.SP; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Dataset, Medical AI, EEG Analysis
- **Summary**: 发布DOSE-I数据集，包含281次内镜检查中78.5小时的多模态生物信号（EEG等），标注意识转换和镇静深度。提供预处理pEEG特征和伪影检测细节，为镇静深度预测研究提供资源。

<h3 id="112">[112] Additive Causal Construction for Transferable and Reconfigurable Cross-System Learning in Multi-Source Image Fusion</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02572](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02572) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhizhong Fu, Wei Zhou, Zhaoyang Jiang, Yulong Lin, Yifu Hou, Xiaorong Ding, Qiang Yan, Yifan Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Image Fusion, Causal Inference, Out-of-Distribution Detection
- **Summary**: 针对多源图像融合中的跨系统差异和纠缠问题，提出加性因果构建（ACC）框架，通过因果锚点传递性和不确定性量化实现可迁移和可重构融合。在ColorMNIST和多中心医学图像上提升OOD泛化能力。

<h3 id="113">[113] From Tensor Buffer to Distributed Memory Hierarchy: A Survey of KV Cache Management for LLM Serving</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02574](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02574) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jie Li, Tongyang Wang, Yong Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.DC; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, Memory Architecture, KV Cache Management
- **Summary**: 本文综述了大语言模型服务中KV缓存管理的多种方法，按局部性、生命周期、所有权和底层架构四个维度分类，揭示了五种架构原型。论文还评估了现有工作，指出了七个缺失的KV特定测量指标，并联系到容错、隔离、分层淘汰、推测解码、MoE服务和共享缓存语义等开放问题。

<h3 id="114">[114] Criterion-Conditional In-Context Learning: Evaluating Criterion-Shift Adaptation in Vision-Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02575](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02575) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kaiyun Yang, Ruilin Yang, Zhimin Yao, J. Wang, Wei Ge
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: In-Context Learning, Vision-Language Model, Benchmark
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 本文提出了准则条件上下文学习（CC-ICL）的新设置，要求模型在固定任务语义下从上下文中推断潜在准则并调整预测。构建了CC-Bench基准，并提出准则不变性和准则敏感性两个互补指标。实验表明，大多数模型存在刚性边界偏差，而简单的多准则训练策略可以显著减少这种偏差，提高性能。

<h3 id="115">[115] Homer: Understanding Long-form Videos with Hierarchical Memory and Agentic Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02588](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02588) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yixin Ji, Fanghua Ye, Juntao Li, Bo Zhao, Zexuan Qiu, Zhaopeng Tu, Liefeng Bo, Min Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Video Understanding, Long Context, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 本文提出Homer框架，用于在线理解长视频。Homer的记忆结构模拟视频的多尺度结构，从原始感知到重复实体再到事件，并通过分层在线记忆探索和推理，通过多轮记忆检索定位场景、查找细节并组合答案。Homer在多个长视频基准上超越了之前的最佳方法，并展示了模型无关的结构化检索能力。

<h3 id="116">[116] An automated method of identifying incorrectly labelled images based on the sequences of loss functions of deep learning networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02594](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02594) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhipeng Zhang, Wenhui Shou, Wengting Ma, Dongjia Xing, Qingqing Xu, Li-Qun Xu, Qingxia Fan, Ling Xu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Label Noise, Data Quality
- **Venue**: 6th EAI International Conference on IoT as a Service (IoTaaS 2020)
- **Summary**: 本文提出一种自动化方法，通过分析深度学习分类网络在多个训练轮次中的损失函数序列，识别错误标注的医学图像。在眼底图像数据集上，该方法识别出75.31%的翻转标签，假阳性率仅4.85%。修正错误后重新训练，模型准确率从95.93%提升至96.50%，接近理想值。

<h3 id="117">[117] AgentLTL: A Trace-Verification Framework for Measuring, Enforcing, and Training Procedural Compliance in Tool-Using LLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02599](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02599) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Laïla Elkoussy, Julien Perez
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.LO
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Tool Learning, Formal Methods
- **Summary**: 本文提出AgentLTL，一种基于一阶线性时态逻辑的语言，用于表达工具使用LLM智能体轨迹的程序性规则，并提供确定性的合规评分。该框架可用于约束智能体行为或作为微调的密集奖励。实验表明，使用相同奖励微调在准确性和合规性上分别提升了38和17.5个百分点。

<h3 id="118">[118] Knowledge-Centric Information Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02609](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02609) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mariano Garralda-Barrio
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.DB
- **Tags**: Enterprise AI, Knowledge Graph, Knowledge Representation
- **Summary**: 本文提出知识架构的概念，认为企业AI系统需要一种新的架构原则来表示、维护、治理和交付组织知识。论文提供了概念模型和分类法，展示了传统数据工程保证如何在知识工件上重新定义，并强调了知识从被动资源向运营资产的转变。

<h3 id="119">[119] Fusion: A Framework for Unified Sequential Token AdaptatIon in VisiOn TraNsformers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02612](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02612) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Aravind Pradeep, Samira Nazari, Mahdi Taheri, Christian Herglotz
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.AR; cs.DC; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Vision Transformer, Early Exit, Token Merging
- **Summary**: 本文提出Fusion框架，统一协调视觉Transformer中的令牌合并、提前退出和令牌剪枝，通过简单分阶段设计（先合并令牌，再评估置信度，最后对继续推理的样本进行剪枝）使得三种机制协同工作。在ImageNet-1k上，Fusion在相似计算预算下匹配或超越最先进的方法，同时将校准误差降低至4倍，推理能耗降低48%。

<h3 id="120">[120] The agent creates, we validate: A Lightweight Framework for Agentic Artifact Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02615](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02615) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yaniv Melamed, Yoni Zukerman, Michal Shechter, Miri Weissler, Ashwin Patil, Hani Neuvirth-Telem
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.SE
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Code Generation, Software Testing
- **Summary**: 本文提出一个轻量级框架，基于“LLM生成，我们验证”的核心原则，通过测试驱动生成、确定性测试与LLM测试结合、以及专家精简评估器，确保生成工件的可靠性。在安全领域的三个工件类型（KQL查询、MITRE ATT&CK映射、实体映射）上展示了效果，已在Microsoft Sentinel生产环境中部署。

<h3 id="121">[121] COMET: Combinatorial Optimization for Multiplex Editing Targets Via Constraint-Preserving QAOA</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02622](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02622) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Priyansh Singhal, Sumit Maheshwari, Piyush Joshi
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Quantum Computing, Combinatorial Optimization, Optimization
- **Summary**: 本文提出COMET，系统比较了基于惩罚和基于XY-mixer的QAOA在多重CRISPR-Cas9基因编辑中的约束满足策略。在三个基因、十二量子位的实例上，XY-mixer在模拟中超过95%最优概率，而基于惩罚的方法低于6%。在IBM量子处理器上，XY-mixer的模拟-硬件能量差距保持在\|0.8\|以内，而惩罚方法达到+53.9。

<h3 id="122">[122] Fine-Grained Computation Offload for Off-the-Shelf Servers in Tens of Lines</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02630](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02630) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Bojie Li
- **Subjects**: cs.DC; cs.AI; cs.OS
- **Tags**: Heterogeneous Computing, Edge Computing, Hardware Acceleration
- **Summary**: 本文提出一种细粒度计算卸载方法，利用现有服务器中的并发机制（通过请求挂起和恢复）来自动重叠卸载操作，而不需要重写异步框架或运行时。通过在十个现成服务器上添加22-138行代码，实现了1.2-5.4倍的性能提升，并验证了通过LD_PRELOAD注入纤维运行时可以在未修改的线程-连接二进制中实现17.3倍加速。

<h3 id="123">[123] QuantFlow: A Federated Mamba-Based Post-Transformer Foundation Model for Time-Series Forecasting</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02632](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02632) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shah Nawaz Haider, Steve Austin, Arnab Barua, Sarowar Morshed Shawon, Hadaate Ullah
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Time Series Forecasting, Federated Learning, State Space Model
- **Summary**: 本文提出QuantFlow，一个结合反向序列嵌入、双向Mamba状态空间解码器、分位数回归和联邦学习的概率预测框架。在加密市场、交通、电力、天气等数据集上，QuantFlow在ETTm1和Weather上分别获得0.2834和0.2218的均方误差，并且在20客户端非独立同分布部署中，经过三轮通信后仍保持有用精度，无需集中原始数据。

<h3 id="124">[124] Federated Learning for Object Detection: Enabling Collaborative Drone Learning Without Centralizing Data</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02636](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02636) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Daniel M. Jimenez-Gutierrez, Enrique Zuazua, Georgios Kellaris, Joaquin del Rio, Oleksii Sliusarenko, Xabi Uribe-Etxebarria
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CV; cs.DC
- **Tags**: Federated Learning, Object Detection, UAV
- **Summary**: 本文应用联邦学习进行目标检测，使无人机能够改进共享模型的同时保持图像数据本地私有。在KIIT-MiTA数据集上，使用YOLOv6 nano的FL方法在mAP@0.50和mAP@0.50:0.95上分别相对单无人机训练提升了52.89%和67.80%，接近集中式训练的性能。

<h3 id="125">[125] Post-Generation Curation of Synthetic Images via Homogeneous-Heterogeneous Splitting</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02637](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02637) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Disheng Liu, Tuo Liang, Chaoda Song, Yu Yin
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Data Selection, Synthetic Data Generation, Image Classification
- **Summary**: 本文提出一种生成后合成图像筛选方法，通过将每个真实类别分为同质（HO）和异质（HE）子集，然后根据保真度-多样性标准评分，奖励语义对齐同时惩罚规范冗余。该方法不依赖生成器，无需重新训练，在多个基准上优于现有数据选择方法，使用比真实数据少40%的合成样本即可匹配真实数据性能。

<h3 id="126">[126] Metronome: Bound the Cache, Keep the Beat for Real-Time Interaction Model Serving</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02640](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02640) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiaying Meng, Bojie Li
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI; cs.NI
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, Real-Time Systems, KV Cache Management
- **Summary**: 本文指出实时交互模型服务（如Moshi）中的危险故障模式：当累积的会话状态耗尽KV池时，服务性能会从毫秒级骤降到引擎停止，并且这种崩溃是亚稳态且隐蔽的。本文提出Metronome，通过限制每个会话的KV窗口来消除崩溃，并将每帧延迟转变为单调负载信号，从而在线准入控制器可以发现可调度并发数。在真实音频上端到端测量，验证了该方法的有效性。

<h3 id="127">[127] K9-Bench: Evaluating Multimodal LLMs on Canine-Centric Videos</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02680](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02680) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Khush Attarde, Yusuf Ali, Megha Thukral, Divye Bhutani, Thomas Ploetz, Zsolt Kira
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Video Understanding, Multimodal LLM, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 本文提出K9-Bench，一个专注于真实世界家犬视频的基准，包含约5000个问答对，覆盖5个任务类别，测试多模态大语言模型在长程、犬类中心的多模态推理能力。通过可扩展的VLM/LLM数据生成流水线构建数据集，并实施偏差缓解策略。实验表明，前沿MLLM在犬类任务上零样本性能有限，即使是闭源模型也难以进行连续推理。

<h3 id="128">[128] S-EMBER: A Large-Scale Benchmark for Streaming Egocentric Memory Retrieval</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02689](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02689) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xiaodong Wang, Xuanyi Zhao, Pedro Rodriguez, Devendra Singh Sachan, Barlas Oguz, Seungwhan Moon, Shang-Wen Li, Gargi Ghosh, Xin Dong, Wen-Tau Yih
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Video Understanding, Benchmark, Egocentric Vision
- **Summary**: 本文提出S-EMBER，一个大规模流式自我中心记忆检索基准，包含3141个视频、388小时真实活动数据，通过Ray-Ban Meta智能眼镜捕获。该基准形式化了对流式自我中心场景的在线检索，提供了9448个问答对，并揭示了定位悖论：语义推理随参数规模提升，但时间定位精度是固定的架构瓶颈，不受模型规模、分辨率或帧密度提升的影响。

<h3 id="129">[129] LLMoxie: Exploring Agentic AI for Scientific Software Development</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02703](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02703) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Landung Setiawan, Anant Mittal, Cordero Core, Anshul Tambay, Carlos Garcia Jurado Suarez, David A. C. Beck, Andrew J. Connolly, Vani Mandava
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.DC; cs.MA
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Scientific Computing, Agentic AI
- **Venue**: ACM SIGKDD 2026 Workshop: Agentic AI for Scientific and Societal Advances (SciSoc Agents and LLMs)
- **Summary**: 现有AI编码代理不适用于科学软件开发，忽视库约定、数据敏感和轨迹可审计性。提出LLMoxie平台，三层架构支持多云端推理、控制平面和应用增强层，并集成开源RSE插件体系。二十个月的实践表明，该平台能将AI代理转变为领域感知的协作工具。

<h3 id="130">[130] Not All Refusals Are Equal: How Safety Alignment Fails Cybersecurity at Scale</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02714](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02714) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Vadym Hadetskyi, Dario Pasquini, Artem Sorokin
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Safety, LLM Alignment, Cybersecurity
- **Summary**: 安全对齐不区分领域，导致网络安全场景下模型受限。通过大规模abliteration实验，发现拒绝行为广泛分布在多层，并实现领域特定abliteration捕获网络安全有害概念。将模型分为三个易abliteration层级。

<h3 id="131">[131] Diagnosing Aerial-View Object Detectors with Foundational Image Generative Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02718](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02718) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Stanislav Panev, Minhyek Jeon, Vaishnavi Khindkar, Ahish Deshpande, Celso M de Melo, Shuowen Hu, Shayok Chakraborty, Fernando De la Torre
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Object Detection, Remote Sensing, Synthetic Data Generation
- **Summary**: 目标检测器在航拍领域缺乏细粒度评估手段。提出合成诊断框架，结合文本生成、属性编辑和自动验证，构建可控测试平台。在三个检测架构和数据集上，合成场景性能趋势匹配真实弱点，针对性补充数据可提升AP50达13%。

<h3 id="132">[132] Signal from Space: Detecting Schools and Towers to Bridge the Digital Divide</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02724](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02724) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zakarya Elmimouni, Sandor Farkas, Fares Fourati, Vladimir Daigele, Walid Mathlouthi, Mohamed-Slim Alouini
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CY
- **Tags**: Object Detection, Remote Sensing, Transfer Learning

<h3 id="133">[133] SMOCS: A Streaming Framework for Simplified Deployment, Monitoring, and Optimization of ML Systems in Production</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02731](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02731) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Armen Kasparian, Kishansingh Rajput, Malachi Schram, John Vennekate
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI

- **Summary**: 在科学设施中部署和维护ML模型面临工程挑战。提出SMOCS，基于Kafka的容器化框架，通过分层抽象、三线程代理架构和配置驱动模型实现持续在线学习。框架已开源。

<h3 id="134">[134] Echoes of Unrest: A Multimodal NLP Framework for Early Warning of Fake News and Violence-Driven Mob Activity</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02734](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02734) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Md. Maruf Bangabashi, Tahmid Hasan, Golam Mahmud, Md. Mostafijur Rahman, Md. Toufiqur Rahman, Jahanur Biswas
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI

- **Venue**: Taylor & Francis
- **Summary**: 社交媒体虚假信息可能引发现实暴力，但多语言多模态早期检测不足。提出融合XLM-RoBERTa、CLIP和多头注意力的框架，结合讽刺和地理空间特征。在138k样本上达到98%准确率。

<h3 id="135">[135] Out-of-Distribution Generalization of Risk Aversion in Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02755](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02755) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kristina Zhang, Junior Chinomso Okoroafor, Benjamin Maltbie, Andrew Lin, Abhitej Bokka, Elliott Thornley
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Safety, Distribution Shift, Risk Aversion
- **Summary**: 训练AI在低风险下风险厌恶能否泛化到高风险未知。引入RiskAverseOOD基准，在多个模型上通过SFT、DPO、激活引导等方法测试。发现风险厌恶可部分泛化98个数量级，但不足以作为可靠保险。

<h3 id="136">[136] Gemma 4 Technical Report</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02770](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02770) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Gemma Team, Sherif El Abd, Vaibhav Aggarwal, Robin Algayres, Alek Andreev, Olivier Bachem, Ian Ballantyne, Cormac Brick, Victor Cărbune, Michelle Casbon, Mayank Chaturvedi, Victor Cotruta, Alice Coucke, Phil Culliton, Robert Dadashi, Lucas Dixon, Mohamed Elhawaty, Utku Evci, Clément Farabet, Johan Ferret, Filippo Galgani, Sertan Girgin, Jean-Bastien Grill, Maarten Grootendorst, Jiaxian Guo, Cassidy Hardin, Yanzhang He, Steven M. Hernandez, Omri Homburger, Léonard Hussenot, Juyeong Ji, Armand Joulin, Aishwarya Kamath, Parnian Kassraie, Olivier Lacombe, Preethi Lahoti, Gaël Liu, Gus Martins, Luciano Martins, Tatiana Matejovicova, Ramona Merhej, Nikola Momchev, Sneha Mondal, Ryan Mullins, Sindhu Raghuram Panyam, Shreya Pathak, Sarah Perrin, André Susano Pinto, Etienne Pot, Angéline Pouget, Alexandre Ramé, Sabela Ramos, Douglas Reid, David Rim, Morgane Rivière, Karsten Roth, Louis Rouillard, Omar Sanseviero, Pier Giuseppe Sessa, Shane Settle, Danila Sinopalnikov, Sara Smoot, Piotr Stanczyk, Andreas Steiner, Lawrence Stewart, Ilya Tolstikhin, Michael Tschannen, Anton Tsitsulin, Nino Vieillard, Renjie Wu, Pingmei Xu, Haichuan Yang, Edouard Yvinec, Li Zhang, Joe Zou, Nicolas Aagnes, Abdelrahman Abdelhamed, Shivani Agrawal, Shubham Agrawal, Ibrahim Alabdulmohsin, Jean Baptiste Alayrac, Uri Alon, Chandramouli Amarnath, Ankesh Anand, Chrysovalantis Anastasiou, Setareh Ariafar, François-Xavier Aubet, Kyriakos Axiotis, Federico Barbero, Joelle Barral, Alexei Bendebury, Urs Bergmann, Stanley Bileschi, Kat Black, Mathieu Blondel, Sebastian Borgeaud, Arthur Bražinskas, Ryan Burnell, Robert Busa-Fekete, Mu Cai
