<h3 id="137">[137] Safe Inference-Time Alignment via Lagrangian Reward Augmentation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02781](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02781) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yaswanth Chittepu, Ativ Joshi, Sohini Chintala, Scott Niekum
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, LLM Safety, Inference-Time Alignment
- **Summary**: 现有推理时对齐方法忽略安全约束。提出拉格朗日奖励增强(LARA)，将约束问题转化为一维凸问题，定义增广奖励。实验表明LARA改善了helpfulness-harmlessness权衡，Best-of-N接近微调基线。

<h3 id="138">[138] A Preliminary Study on Explaining Risk of Code Changes using LLM-Based Prediction Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02782](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02782) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yalin Liu, Kosay Jabre, Rui Abreu, Zachariah J. Carmichael, Vijayaraghavan Murali, Akshay Patel, Jun Ge, Weiyan Sun, Cong Zhang, Audris Mockus, David Khavari, Peter C. Rigby, Nachiappan Nagappan
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Code Quality Assessment, Explainable AI, Software Engineering
- **Summary**: ML预测代码变更风险时缺乏可解释性。利用Diff Risk Score模型的注意力权重突出高风险代码单元。在专家标注数据上，top-2 hunks覆盖53.85%故障行，需审查26.28%变更行。

<h3 id="139">[139] Seduced by the Narrative: Assessing Rule Adherence in Semi-Open Textual Sandboxes</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02802](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02802) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Weiying Chen, Junlong Shen, Zhanyuan Guo, Xiaoou Zhou
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Safety, Adversarial Robustness, Prompt Injection
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/answerrtx/CoC-Seduce)
- **Summary**: LLM作为规则裁判易受叙事框架攻击。提出CoC-Seduce多代理基准，基于TRPG生成5376样本，测试20个裁判模型。结果显示模型规模和推理机制无法保证鲁棒性，伪逻辑是最强攻击。

<h3 id="140">[140] Training Hybrid Block Diffusion Language Models with Partial Bidirectionality</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02805](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02805) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Pranshu Chaturvedi, Parth Shroff, Tarun Suresh, Hangoo Kang, Kaiyue Wen
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Language Modeling, Diffusion Model, Long Context
- **Summary**: 长上下文生成受内存带宽限制。提出BDLM Mamba-Attention混合，限制反向Mamba扫描到当前去噪块，实现精确缓存。在350M参数下，长上下文吞吐比全序列DiffuMamba-H提升19.7倍。

<h3 id="141">[141] SovereignNegotiation-Bench: Evaluating User-Owned Personal Agents In Delegated Bargaining Under Privacy, Consent, Evidence, And Institutional Pressure</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02814](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02814) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dylan Zongmin Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.MA; cs.AI; cs.CY
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Multi-Agent System, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 现有谈判基准忽略用户隐私、同意等关键指标。提出SovereignNegotiation-Bench，多轮谈判基准，评估协议成功、用户效用、隐私等七项指标。结果显示协议成功最大化的代理反而低效用高风险。

<h3 id="142">[142] Vision Token Manipulation Attacks on Cloud-Edge Inference of Large Vision-Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02819](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02819) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zikai Zhang, Rui Hu, Olivera Kotevska, Jiahao Xu
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Adversarial Attack, Cloud-Edge Inference
- **Summary**: 云边LVLM推理中中间视觉token传输易受攻击。提出黑盒中间人攻击，优化token选择。实验表明操纵10%的视觉token可使准确率下降88.31%。

<h3 id="143">[143] JavaVulBench: A Java Vulnerability Benchmark with Realistic Splits, a Unified Multi-Backend Harness, and a Leakage-Aware Evaluation Mode</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02825](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02825) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Norbert Sandor Szolnoki, Gabor Antal
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vulnerability Detection, Benchmark, Static Analysis
- **Summary**: 现有Java漏洞检测基准缺乏真实分割和污染审计。发布JavaVulBench，含~30600方法、5种分割策略和统一评估框架。12个检测器在相同条件下比较，并提供预训练污染审计。

<h3 id="144">[144] Differential Amplifier-Inspired AmpAttention for Multi-View Robotic Manipulation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02845](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02845) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jin Yang, Ping Wei, Nanning Zheng
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Robot Manipulation, Attention Mechanism, Multi-View Learning
- **Summary**: 多视角机器人操作中注意力漂移问题。提出差分放大器启发的AmpAttention，集成到RVAF模型。在RLBench上取得最优平均成功率，训练时间减少33%，高精度任务成功率91%。

<h3 id="145">[145] Determinants and Limits of LLM Security-Tool Orchestration: A Study with HexStrike-AI</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02873](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02873) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Romain Gerard, Assmaa Zeghaider, Yan Guo
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.CR
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Cybersecurity, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 论文针对LLM安全工具编排中模型能力受限于哪些因素（模型本身、客户端、工具集等）缺乏系统性研究的问题。提出了HexStrike-AI开源编排器，在86个picoCTF挑战上评估了三种工具访问模式和三组模型/客户端配置（共774次试验），并进行了诊断和针对性改进。总体上，解决率从55.4%提高到72.0%，且客户端是固定模型的首要因素（两个DeepSeek客户端间差距2.1倍），剩余失败主要源于推理或环境限制而非缺少工具。

<h3 id="146">[146] Where do LLMs Fall Short in CBT-Guided Affective Reasoning?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02885](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02885) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Vaishnavi Sinha, Pooja Guttal, Pranay Deep Reddy Katike, Vishal Sinha, Gerald Ndawula, Lira Yoon, Andrea Kleinsmith, Manas Gaur
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.HC; cs.IR
- **Tags**: Affective Computing, LLM Reasoning, Dialogue System
- **Venue**: ACII 2026
- **Summary**: 论文发现LLM熟悉CBT理论（在执照考试中准确率高达96%）但在实际对话中无法有效应用，总是偏向验证与反思。提出一个知识引导框架，将CBT对话分解为Beck认知概念化结构，并采用多链思维（MCoT）策略选择，同时引入协议杠杆力（F）指标衡量行为改变。实验表明：MCoT能改善策略选择，但效果仅在1%左右，模型仍偏向验证与反思。

<h3 id="147">[147] SPLIT: Training-Free AI-Generated and Partially Edited Video Detection via Spatial Patch-Level Incoherence and Temporal Roughness</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02886](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02886) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jongyeop Hyun, Hyounghun Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Deepfake Detection, Video Forensics, AI-Generated Content
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/mldljyh/SPLIT)
- **Summary**: 论文针对AI生成视频检测在实际部署中需要极低假阳率的问题。提出SPLIT，一种无需训练的检测器，利用冻结视觉编码器的补丁令牌计算空间补丁级不一致性和时间粗糙度，通过乘法融合和伽马校正区分真实与假视频。在FakeParts、GenVideo和ViF-Bench三个基准上，SPLIT在FPR=0.1%时取得了最高的假视频召回率，远超监督和无需训练的基线。

<h3 id="148">[148] PPE-Bench: A Benchmark for Evaluating MLLM Unlearning under Private-Public Entanglement</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02897](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02897) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xianren Zhang, Delvin Ce Zhang, Dongwon Lee, Suhang Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Machine Unlearning, Multimodal Learning, Privacy
- **Summary**: 论文指出现有多模态大模型遗忘基准存在两个局限：图像过于简化、假设遗忘集和保留集完全分离。提出PPE-Bench基准，每个图像包含需遗忘的私密人物和需保留的公开信息（公众人物或地标），并引入两种简单方法在遗忘过程中保留公开信息。实验发现现有遗忘方法能减少私密信息泄露，但严重损害相邻公开信息。

<h3 id="149">[149] TIER: Trajectory-Invariant Explanation Regularization for Membership Privacy</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02903](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02903) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Varun Sharma, Kar Wai Fok, Vrizlynn L. L. Thing
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Membership Inference Attack, Explainable AI, Adversarial Robustness
- **Summary**: 论文指出解释接口可能被对手利用进行成员推理攻击，现有防御无法直接缓解此类攻击。提出TIER防御，通过惩罚梯度引导扰动导致的置信度下降波动，并用KL散度最小化成员与非成员之间的分布偏移，实现解释鲁棒性。大量实验表明TIER能有效缓解攻击，同时保持模型效用和解释保真度。

<h3 id="150">[150] Learning Taxonomic Trees with Hierarchical Representation Regularization for Large Multimodal Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02909](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02909) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hulingxiao He, Zhi Tan, Yuxin Peng
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Representation Learning, Taxonomy Learning
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/PKU-ICST-MIPL/HiR2_ICML2026)
- **Summary**: 论文指出大语言模型缺乏分类学知识，导致分层视觉识别一致性低。提出HiR^2正则化器，包括基于双曲蕴含锥的分类学蕴含损失和促进语义嵌入角分离的判别分散损失。实验证明HiR^2能有效捕捉分类学结构，适用于多种多模态大模型和微调方法。

<h3 id="151">[151] CoACT: Action-Preserving Observation Compression for Coding Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02911](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02911) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Haorui Chen, Yuancheng Zhu, Yitong Zhang, Jia Li
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Code Generation, LLM Agent, Context Compression
- **Summary**: 论文针对编码代理中观察结果累积导致高推理成本的问题。提出CoACT方法，利用下一步动作保持（NAP）原则压缩观察，使用基于奖励训练的轻量级压缩器选择不影响代理下一步动作的紧凑候选。在SWE-bench Verified上，CoACT减少平均总令牌消耗33%，同时保持接近未压缩代理的任务解决效果。

<h3 id="152">[152] Bootstrap Flow-Map Tree Sampling Enables Online Feedback Driven Search</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02915](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02915) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Binglin Ji, Anindya Sarkar, Hengchang Lu, Jens Sjölund, Yevgeniy Vorobeychik
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Flow Matching, Reinforcement Learning, Bayesian Optimization
- **Summary**: 论文针对生成模型在有限采样预算下进行全局探索和偏好对齐的挑战。提出Bootstrap Flow-Map-Tree（BFMT）采样框架，通过单次函数评估构建完整树路径，并动态调整时间步长以从全局探索平滑过渡到局部精细化。实验表明BFMT在多种搜索和对齐任务上显著优于基线方法。

<h3 id="153">[153] Harmonic-Aware Transformer for Real-Time Catheter Localization in Interventional Procedures of Magnetic Particle Imaging</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02919](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02919) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Abuobaida M. Khair, Wenjing Jiang, Xiaoli Yang, Moritz Wildgruber, Xiaopeng Ma
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CV; eess.IV; eess.SP
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Medical Imaging, Transformer Architecture
- **Summary**: 论文针对磁粒子成像中介入导管的实时、无辐射追踪需求。提出谐波感知Transformer框架，直接从原始MPI电压信号预测导管尖端位置，避免了图像重建。模型在弯曲数据集上达到最小L2误差0.103 ± 0.092 mm，推理延迟0.55毫秒/帧，吞吐量约1800帧/秒，在各种运动条件下表现稳健。

<h3 id="154">[154] R3D: Quantitative 3D Spatial Reasoning for Egocentric Wearables</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02921](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02921) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Maxwell Horton, Wei Lu, Quan Tran, Yury Astashonok, Kirmani Ahmed, Babak Damavandi, Anuj Kumar, Xiao Zhang, Seungwhan Moon
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: 3D Vision, Spatial Reasoning, Visual Reasoning
- **Summary**: 论文指出现有基准无法处理自然人眼视角视频、RGB-D输入和定量3D空间推理的挑战。提出R3D-Bench基准（3033个问题）和R3D模型无关空间工具调用框架，通过分割和深度提升对象表示构建3D场景。R3D结合Qwen3-VL 235B达到73.5%平均相对准确率，远超深度启用和RGB-only基线。

<h3 id="155">[155] VideoSearcher: Empowering Video Deep Research with Multi-Tool Agentic Reasoning via Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02927](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02927) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhenkun Gao, Yicheng Bao, Jinlong Peng, Xueheng Li, Theo Huang, Bangwei Liu, Kunquan Li, Zhenye Gan, Tao Hu, Chengjun Xie, Mingqian Yang, Xuanhua He, Zhizhong Zhang, Xin Tan, Chengjie Wang, Yuan Xie
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Video Deep Research, LLM Agent, Reinforcement Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Stephen-gzk/VideoSearcher)
- **Summary**: 论文针对视频理解向开放世界证据探索（视频深度研究）的转变，且现有搜索代理主要针对静态图像的问题。提出VideoSearcher闭环代理框架，统一时间定位、空间聚焦和多模态搜索，并设计BiSPO强化学习算法优化知识密集型推理轨迹。构建VideoSearch-QA基准，实验表明VideoSearcher在面向搜索和多模态理解基准上显著优于先前的开源代理基线。

<h3 id="156">[156] Modeling the Impact of Visual Brand Language on Attention, Object Recognition, and Memory Retrieval</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02929](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02929) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Rachel F. Heaton, John E. Hummel
- **Subjects**: q-bio.NC; cs.AI; cs.CV; cs.HC
- **Tags**: Cognitive Science, Object Recognition, Attention Mechanism
- **Summary**: 论文探讨视觉品牌语言对物体识别、注意力分配和功能信息检索的影响。基于JIM和LISA模型建立模拟框架，预测品牌信息会吸引注意力并延缓物体功能类别的识别，品牌化程度越强效应越大。这些结果对设计物体的可用性和体验有潜在启示。

<h3 id="157">[157] PromptPET: Privacy-Utility Optimized Prompt Obfuscation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02932](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02932) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ke Yang, Olivia Figueira, Umar Iqbal, Athina Markopoulou
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.HC; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Privacy, Prompt Engineering, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 论文针对用户与AI聊天机器人交互中的隐私保护与效用之间的权衡问题。提出PromptPET，一个基于LLM的代理，利用强化学习规则优化器为提示中的每个敏感部分选择最佳混淆操作（删除、抽象、替换、加噪/去噪）。在真实聊天数据集上，PromptPET达到了任何单一混淆操作所能实现的最佳隐私-效用权衡，并显著优于先前最优方法。

<h3 id="158">[158] MatPhaseBench: A Semantics-Guided Benchmark for Materials Phase Diagrams Understanding</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02934](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02934) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hanwen Wang, Sihan Liang, Zhiwei Liu, Yangang Wang, Wei Yan, Yuqin Liu, Zongguo Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Scientific Reasoning, Multimodal Learning, Materials Science
- **Summary**: 论文指出视觉语言模型在理解逻辑复杂科学图像（如材料相图）方面缺乏系统评估。构建MatPhaseBench基准，包含200个从3681篇论文中精选的高质量图文对，覆盖189个材料系统和70种元素。实验表明当前VLM远不及专家水平，仅能进行表面视觉感知，缺乏基于热力学机制的深度推理。

<h3 id="159">[159] A Precedent-Guided Co-Scientist for Side-Effect-Aware Drug Redesign</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02944](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02944) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yujin Kim, Charmgil Hong
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Drug Discovery, Knowledge Graph, LLM Agent
- **Venue**: ICML 2026 Workshop on AI for Science
- **Summary**: 论文针对药物重新设计以减轻副作用同时保留治疗功能的问题。提出PRECEDE，一个基于LLM编排器的协同科学家系统，利用药物-副作用关联、生物医学知识图谱和安全驱动优化的先例进行证据基础推理，辅以显式策略和人工审核检查点。该系统被定位为可审计、可证伪且受药理先例约束的人机协同AI科学工作流。

<h3 id="160">[160] Pooling-Based Context Modeling for Convolution-Free Deep Image Prior</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02952](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02952) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Gihyun Kim, Jong-Seok Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Image Denoising, Image Restoration, Image Super-Resolution
- **Summary**: 论文针对深度图像先验（DIP）因过参数化而容易过拟合噪声的问题。提出Pool-DIP，一种无卷积架构，利用基于池化的对比建模捕获空间上下文。实验表明Pool-DIP在多个数据集上获得有竞争力的去噪性能，同时显著减少参数和计算复杂度，并且能泛化到超分辨率和修复任务。频谱分析揭示了Pool-DIP能稳定高频成分的演化并抑制错误高频信号。

<h3 id="161">[161] The Foreign Policy AI Evaluation Gap</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02955](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02955) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Charles Pozniak, Jeba Sania
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI
- **Tags**: AI Safety, AI Evaluation
- **Summary**: 论文指出将AI系统用于外交政策（治国术）是一个高风险领域，现有评估方法无法处理部分可观测性、无界动作空间等结构特性。文章呼吁技术AI治理社区关注该领域，并提出了一个需求侧评估框架，将外交政策工作流分解为可评估的子任务。通过生态系统回顾，发现当前评估在访问、验证、安全性和可操作性方面存在不对称。

<h3 id="162">[162] Parallelized Autoregressive Decoding for Omni-Modal Dense Video Captioning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02963](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02963) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Wenzheng Zeng, Siyi Jiao, Chen Gao, Hwee Tou Ng, Mike Zheng Shou
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.MM
- **Tags**: Video Captioning, Autoregressive Model, Parallel Decoding
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/showlab/PadCaptioner)
- **Summary**: 密集视频字幕生成面临推理效率低下的问题。本文提出并行化自回归框架，利用跨时间不同事件的弱局部依赖性，实现无损并行生成。关键组件包括潜伏全局规划机制和事件因子化并行解码，实验表明在效率和性能上均优于现有基准。

<h3 id="163">[163] Individual Parameters in Weight-Sparse Transformers Appear Interpretable</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02964](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02964) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Arnau Marin-Llobet, Stefan Heimersheim
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Mechanistic Interpretability, Sparse Transformer
- **Summary**: 可解释性研究关注如何理解神经网络组件。本文通过自动化LLM流水线为每个权重编写人类可读描述，并验证其泛化性。在稀疏Transformer中，12%到31%的权重具有可解释性，且稀疏Transformer中可解释权重比例高于密集Transformer。

<h3 id="164">[164] Hierarchical Sparse Attention Done Right: Toward Infinite Context Modeling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02980](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02980) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xiang Hu, Xinyu Wei, Hao Gu, Minshen Zhang, Tian Liang, Huayang Li, Lei Zhu, Yan Wang, Sirui Han, Yushi Bai, Kewei Tu, Haitao Mi, Leo Liang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Sparse Attention, Long Context
- **Summary**: 现有块稀疏注意力方法在块选择上不准确。本文提出Hierarchical Landmark Sparse (HiLS) Attention，一种端到端学习块选择的稀疏注意力机制。实验表明HiLS在域内上下文长度上性能与全注意力相当，并能外推至训练上下文长度的64倍以上，保持90%检索准确率。

<h3 id="165">[165] Enhanced Feature Extraction for IoT Network Intrusion Detection Using GNNs and KAN</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02981](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02981) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Long Zhao, Shixun Ji, Bin Cheng, Bin He
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Graph Neural Network, Intrusion Detection, IoT Security
- **Summary**: 物联网网络安全面临动态拓扑和复杂攻击模式挑战。本文提出SKGFusionKAN，结合GraphSAGE和多尺度选择核注意力，自适应提取节点和边特征，并利用KAN进行分类。在四个NIDS基准上优于现有方法。

<h3 id="166">[166] VISTA: Auditing Semantic Divergence in Vision-Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02995](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02995) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Junchi Liao, Jiawen Deng, Fuji Ren
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Bias Detection
- **Summary**: 视觉语言模型可能对含有特定视觉概念（如人口统计特征）的图像产生异常统一响应。本文提出VISTA，一种黑盒跨模型审计方法，结合语义熵和分布散度检测异常。实验发现选择性拒绝等模式，模型对某些群体拒绝率从0到65%不等。

<h3 id="167">[167] CONFLUX: A Latent Diusion Model for 3D Chest-CT Synthesis with RL Post-Training</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02998](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02998) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Max Van Puyvelde, Halil Ibrahim Gulluk, Wim Van Criekinge, Olivier Gevaert
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Medical Imaging, Diffusion Model, 3D Generation
- **Summary**: 可控3D医学图像生成需要高保真度和忠实条件。本文提出CONFLUX，一种用于胸部CT的潜伏扩散模型，条件基于结构化放射学元数据。模型在三平面FID上领先，并通过在线强化学习后训练减少47%的与真实扫描可靠性差距。

<h3 id="168">[168] PosterHarness: Turning Scientific Poster Generation into an Auditable Instruction-Following Benchmark</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03006](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03006) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tianyi Yang, Dawei Fu, Youpeng Wu, Zixun Kou, Linrui Chen, Ruobing Jiang, Zijian Wang, Qiang Li
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.SE
- **Tags**: Benchmark, Instruction Following, Image Generation
- **Summary**: 文本丰富图像模型可设计海报布局，但缺乏衡量科学通信合同遵守情况的方法。本文提出POSTERHARNESS，将海报生成转化为指令遵循基准，采用占位符合同使模型只设计视觉摘要。在12篇论文上测试，该合同消除了VLMs中的合成图形。

<h3 id="169">[169] Back to Basics: Improving Molecular Understanding in LLMs via SMILES-Graph Translation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03007](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03007) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Wenda Wang, Jinjia Feng, Zhewei Wei
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; q-bio.BM
- **Tags**: Molecular Generation, LLM Application, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 现有分子LLM缺乏可靠结构基础。本文提出MolBasic，结构优先框架，通过SMILES-图转换加强结构理解。采用多级结构感知基准和渐进学习方案。实验表明显著改善结构理解，在属性预测和客观优化上取得稳健收益。

<h3 id="170">[170] Can Model Merging Improve Aggregation in DiLoCo?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03011](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03011) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Stefan Horoi, Benjamin Thérien, Guy Wolf, Eugene Belilovsky
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Model Merging, Distributed Training, Communication Efficiency
- **Summary**: 模型合并技术可与分布式学习结合提高聚合效率。本文在DiLoCo的伪梯度聚合与任务算术模型合并之间建立类比，发现Iso-C方法表现良好。提出IsoLoCo，结合Iso-C与Nesterov动量，在语言模型预训练中显著优于DiLoCo，且差距随工作节点增加而扩大。

<h3 id="171">[171] HyperVAttention: Efficient Sparse Attention with Spatio-Temporal Clustering for Video Diffusion</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03012](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03012) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dongyeun Lee, Amir Zandieh, Vahab Mirrokni, Junmo Kim, Insu Han
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Sparse Attention, Video Generation, Diffusion Model
- **Summary**: 视频扩散Transformer受限于自注意力二次复杂度。本文提出HyperVAttention，训练无关的稀疏注意力框架，通过3D局部窗口聚类和硬件感知聚类合并减少开销。实验在文本到视频生成上端到端延迟降低2.13倍，同时提高保真度。

<h3 id="172">[172] MambaLIE: Scene Light Intensity-Boosted Low-Light Image Enhancement with State Space Model</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03013](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03013) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Wanshu Fan, Xiangyu Li, Cong Wang, Kin-man Lam, Xin Yang, Haiyan Zhang, Dongsheng Zhou
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Low-Light Enhancement, State Space Model, Image Enhancement
- **Summary**: 低光照图像增强中CNN和Transformer各有局限。本文提出MambaLIE，基于状态空间模型，引入场景光强度并设计局部增强SSM分支。实验在合成和真实基准上超越现有方法，在准确率、速度和模型大小上均有优势。

<h3 id="173">[173] OmniFocus: Query-Guided Modality-Balanced Token Compression for Omni-Modal Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03050](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03050) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shijie Cao, Qingyu Zhang, Boxi Yu, Yuzhong Zhang, Boxi Cao, Yaojie Lu, Hongyu Lin, Xianpei Han, Le Sun
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CV; cs.SD
- **Tags**: Token Compression, Multimodal Learning, Inference Optimization
- **Summary**: 全模态LLM处理音视频输入产生大量token导致高推理成本。本文提出OmniFocus，训练无关的查询引导token压缩方法，对视频和音频独立重要性估计，实现模态对称压缩。在Qwen2.5-Omni上25% token保留时保持性能，预填加速1.38倍。

<h3 id="174">[174] LACE-SVD: Loss-Aware SVD with Cumulative Error Correction for LLM Compression</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03057](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03057) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhuowen Liu, Longkun Hao, Shiyu Feng, Xiaowen Chang, Ruiqun Li, Changqun Li
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Model Compression, Low-Rank Compression
- **Summary**: LLM低秩压缩中现有SVD方法忽略损失敏感性和误差累积。本文提出LACE-SVD，通过损失感知的秩分配和传播感知校正减少全局偏差。实验在LLaMA-7B上0.6压缩比下WikiText-2 PPL显著优于Dobi-SVD（32.57 vs 46.18）。

<h3 id="175">[175] Spectral Rewiring for Exploration, Purification, and Model Merging</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03065](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03065) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhilong Zhang, Hongli Yu, Huan-ang Gao, Hanlin Wu, Yuxuan Song, Wei-Ying Ma, Ya-Qin Zhang, Hao Zhou
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Model Merging, Reinforcement Learning, LLM
- **Summary**: RL后训练中密集全参数更新抑制推理性能和多能力合并。本文提出子空间对齐重连（SAR），保留谱空间中推理有效更新。用0.58%参数保留99%性能，改善数学推理和代理编码，并实现专家模型合并。

<h3 id="176">[176] PixCon: Clean-Positive Contrastive Learning for Foundation-Model Semi-Supervised Segmentation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03068](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03068) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ebenezer Tarubinga
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG; eess.IV
- **Tags**: Semi-Supervised Learning, Semantic Segmentation, Contrastive Learning
- **Summary**: 半监督语义分割中基础骨干已能获得高干净伪标签，精度瓶颈在嵌入空间结构。本文提出PixCon，干净正样本像素对比学习，保证无污染正样本集。在Pascal VOC等数据集上匹配或改进强基线，每种子平均改进约+0.2 mIoU。

<h3 id="177">[177] Attention-Guided Efficientnet Architecture For Precise Criminal Identification in Surveillance Images</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03073](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03073) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Savitha N J, Lata B T
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Face Recognition, Attention Mechanism
- **Summary**: 针对监控图像中低分辨率、光照变化等问题导致人脸识别困难，提出基于注意力引导的EfficientNet（AG-EfficientNet）框架，集成CBAM和多尺度特征融合及混合Softmax-Triplet优化。在LFW和SCFace数据集上达到98.2%准确率，优于传统架构。

<h3 id="178">[178] STELLA: Efficient Sensor-to-LLM Translation for On-Device Human Activity Recognition</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03089](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03089) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nirhoshan Sivaroopan, Albert Zomaya, Kanchana Thilakarathna
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Human Activity Recognition, LLM Inference, On-Device Learning
- **Summary**: 针对LLM在边缘设备上部署困难（传感器提示过长、云推理延迟和隐私风险），提出STELLA框架，通过轻量级层次化分词器将多通道惯性数据压缩为潜在令牌，并结合冻结LLM进行标签评分。在7个人类活动识别数据集上达到SOTA，F1提升达11.83%，支持设备端个性化。

<h3 id="179">[179] Don't Wait to Reply: Towards Responsive yet Thoughtful Dialogue through Proactive Thinking</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03093](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03093) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ante Wang, Jiaqi Fu, Xuanyi Chen, Ruotian Ma, Zhaopeng Tu, Weizhi Ma, Yang Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Dialogue System, LLM Reasoning
- **Summary**: 针对LLM在对话中被动思考导致延迟的问题，提出Proactive Thinking框架，让模型在对话空闲期预计算可能的回复，通过前瞻性思考提高交互效率。在多个时间感知基准上证明能有效提升效率而不损失质量。

<h3 id="180">[180] Flow-A11y: Flow-Aware Accessibility Testing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03100](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03100) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nasr Eddine Fliti, Leisan Kokorina, Florian Tambon, Michael Papadakis
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Accessibility Technology, Software Testing
- **Summary**: 针对现代网页的交互流程可访问性难以自动化测试的问题，提出Flow-A11y系统，通过自然语言场景执行流程、记录运行时轨迹，构建证据包进行差异分析。在19个真实场景中达到比通用浏览器代理审计高十余倍的一致性。

<h3 id="181">[181] SNR-Adaptive Unified Diffusion for Multi-Task Medical Image Segmentation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03103](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03103) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiahao Liu, Hang Wei, Shuai Wu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical Image Segmentation, Diffusion Model, Multi-Task Learning
- **Venue**: IEEE SMC 2026
- **Summary**: 针对多数据集和多模态的心脏医学图像分割中标签冲突和梯度不平衡问题，提出UniT-Diff统一扩散分割框架，通过任务特定输出空间、SNR自适应任务条件和条件dropout解决冲突。在三个基准上同时超越单任务基线，LA Dice提升0.87%等。

<h3 id="182">[182] ACPO: Adaptive Credit Policy Optimization via Fine-Grained Surrogate Entropy</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03126](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03126) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zijun Xie, Yuyang You, Yongzhi Li, Enlei Gong, Zeyu Chen, Quan Chen, Yanhua Cheng, Peng Jiang, Yadong Mu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, LLM Reasoning, Credit Assignment
- **Summary**: 针对LLM强化学习中稀疏奖励导致令牌级信用分配困难的问题，提出ACPO框架，基于模式局部代理熵非对称调整策略更新。在数学推理和编程基准上持续优于DAPO等基线。

<h3 id="183">[183] A Multi-Task Deep Learning Framework for Real-Time Intelligent Video Surveillance with Temporal Event Validation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03131](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03131) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Estera Dumitru, Stelian Spînu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Video Understanding, Object Detection, Temporal Reasoning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/DumitruEstera/ai-surveillance-system)
- **Summary**: 针对现代视频监控需要自动分析多种安全事件的问题，提出统一多任务深度学习框架，同时进行人脸识别、车牌识别、武器检测、火灾检测和动作识别，并采用时间事件验证降低误报率。在多个数据集上实现实时运行，误报率从52%降至4%。

<h3 id="184">[184] Detecting Architectural Drift in Safety-Critical Firmware through Runtime Trace Analysis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03135](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03135) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Domenico Francesco De Angelis, Marco De Luca, Domenico Amalfitano, Pasquale Cimmino, Anna Rita Fasolino
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Software Engineering, Runtime Verification
- **Venue**: ICSME 2026
- **Summary**: 针对安全关键固件中架构设计与运行时行为不一致的问题，提出运行时信息方法，通过硬件跟踪执行、提取组件消息交换并与设计时序列图比较，检测架构漂移。在工业固件上验证，生成差异与专家参考高度一致。

<h3 id="185">[185] Text as Partial Constraint: Core-Residual Alignment for Robust Vision-Language Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03143](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03143) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chengzhen Yu, Canran Xiao, Siyuan Ma, Yang Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Adversarial Robustness, Contrastive Learning
- **Summary**: 针对视觉语言对齐中自然语言描述不完整导致相似度脆弱和过度自信的问题，提出文本作为部分约束（TPC）框架，通过核心-残差对齐、不确定性感知对比学习，在零样本识别和对抗鲁棒性上显著提升，ImageNet Top-1达81.42/64.05。

<h3 id="186">[186] CuBAS: Information Geometric Curvature-Based Adaptive Sampling for Supervised Classification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03145](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03145) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Alexandre L. M. Levada
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CV; cs.IT; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Active Learning, Data Selection
- **Summary**: 针对监督分类中训练集采样策略忽视数据几何结构的问题，提出基于信息几何曲率的自适应采样方法CuBAS，利用Potts模型估计局部曲率，选择低曲率和高曲率区域节点构建紧凑训练子集。在60多个基准数据集上一致优于随机采样和不确定性基线。

<h3 id="187">[187] Rethinking Neural Nonlinearity as Gating</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03148](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03148) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Muhammad Sabih, Frank Hannig, Jürgen Teich
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Neural Architecture, Model Compression
- **Summary**: 针对神经网络激活函数的非线性表达，提出阈值门控（TG）基元，证明标准激活函数（ReLU、Sigmoid等）均可视为TG的实例，并支持无重训练转换。在CNN、Transformer等网络上验证，并提升模型压缩和训练速度。

<h3 id="188">[188] Conditional Diffusion Guided Knowledge Transfer for Multi-Domain Knowledge Graph Completion</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03154](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03154) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Graph Completion, Diffusion Model, Multi-Domain Learning
- **Summary**: 针对多领域知识图谱补全中现有方法抑制领域特定信息的问题，提出生成式范式DMKGC，利用条件扩散模型生成领域通用实体嵌入，结合支持KG的等价实体引导。在14个KG上平均MRR提升4.3%。

<h3 id="189">[189] Which Algorithm Specification Formats Help Language Models Implement Machine Learning Algorithms?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03158](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03158) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Masahiro Kato, Taka Kato
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Code Generation, Prompt Engineering
- **Summary**: 针对LLM从论文实现算法时格式影响的问题，比较六种算法描述格式（散文、伪代码等）对LLM初次实现准确性的影响。结果显示LaTeX伪代码效果最好，建议作者明确接口和边界情况。

<h3 id="190">[190] The Role of Prompt Language and Translation-Theory-Driven Prompts in Large Language Models: A Case Study on Spanish-Chinese Journalistic Translation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03160](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03160) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Haohong Lai, Weijia Li
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Machine Translation, Prompt Engineering, LLM Evaluation
- **Venue**: EAMT 2026
- **Summary**: 针对翻译理论驱动提示设计对西-中新闻翻译质量的影响，实验表明人评中简练提示最佳，自动化指标与人类评价不一致，提示语言影响微弱。翻译理论提示可减少特定风格错误。

<h3 id="191">[191] KARMA: Knowledge graph-based Automated Reasoning Materialization and Alignment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03166](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03166) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jinkyeong Choi, Chaebin Jeong, Donghyeon Park
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Knowledge Graph, Preference Optimization, LLM Reasoning
- **Summary**: 针对模板对比合成中分辨率不匹配问题（候选仅在少数实体槽上不同但监督均匀分布），提出KARMA框架，通过知识图路径枚举生成槽对齐候选，并利用槽级目标进行偏好监督。在生物医学等基准上优于基线和序列级方法。

<h3 id="192">[192] Decentralised Federated Learning over Temporal Networks: The Role of Heterogeneities</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03171](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03171) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Arash Badie-Modiri, Chiara Boldrini, Lorenzo Valerio, János Kertész, Márton Karsai
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.DC
- **Tags**: Federated Learning, Decentralized Learning, Dynamic Graph
- **Summary**: 针对去中心化联邦学习中结构和时间异质性的影响，证明学习过程受时态网络上懒惰随机游走扩散过程支配，发现忽略异质性会导致不现实的快速收敛，真实时态网络中异质性显著减缓扩散和收敛。

<h3 id="193">[193] Effectiveness of LLM-based Software Diversity for Reliability Improvement -- an Empirical Study</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03174](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03174) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Gabriel Almeida, Ilir Gashi, Vladimir Stankovic, João R. Campos
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Code Generation, Software Diversity
- **Summary**: 研究LLM生成代码的软件多样性对可靠性提升的有效性。传统软件多样性成本高，LLM可低成本生成多种实现。实验发现结合LLM生成的程序（尤其是异质组合）能带来可靠性增益，受编程语言和生成设置影响。

<h3 id="194">[194] CRRL: A Causality-Based Reinforcement Learning Framework for Autonomous System Recovery</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03177](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03177) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.LG

- **Summary**: 传统强化学习在自主系统恢复中缺乏因果理解，导致在故障状态中长时间停滞。提出CRRL框架，结合因果引导和规则恢复，训练策略与规则协作。实验表明因果训练显著提升奖励、距离和速度，减少恢复干预。

<h3 id="195">[195] Teaming Up with AI: Coordination and Cooperation</h3>

- **Authors**: Nicole Immorlica, Inbal Talgam-Cohen
- **Subjects**: cs.GT; cs.AI

<h3 id="196">[196] AnchorVLA: Bridging Discrete Decisions and Continuous Trajectories for Vision-Language-Action Planning</h3>

- **Summary**: 现有VLA规划器在连续轨迹生成中存在语义-动作对齐困难和推理低效。提出AnchorVLA层次化决策锚定框架，通过轨迹模式锚点连接高层推理和连续执行。在Bench2Drive上取得SOTA结果。

<h3 id="197">[197] Scalable Maximal Frequent Episode Mining with Desbordante</h3>

<h3 id="198">[198] A Bayesian Framework for Evaluating Scenario Compatibility in Generative Population Synthesis</h3>

- **Summary**: 场景化交通分析依赖聚合人口目标，但生成模型与目标兼容性未知。提出基于贝叶斯更新框架，使用条件变分自编码器学习人口结构分布，通过有效样本量量化兼容性。实验揭示目标与学习结构对齐的重要性。

<h3 id="199">[199] Self-Specializing Vision-Language Transmon Chip Calibration in a Physics-Grounded Environment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03193](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03193) (cross-listed)

- **Summary**: 超导transmon芯片校准是噪声和漂移下的序列决策问题。提出视觉-语言智能体闭环校准，结合物理仿真环境、无梯度自适应和设备笔记。实验将最差情况CZ保真度从0.678提升至0.787。

<h3 id="200">[200] Transition Information Density: Morphological Trajectories, Synesthetic Perception, and Structured Interpolation in Neural Training (or: The Synesthetic AI)</h3>

- **Summary**: 标准训练使用离散端点对，忽略中间状态结构。提出过渡信息密度和位置身份概念，通过结构插值实验发现，在语音和语义空间中，结构化插值显著降低内在维度。

<h3 id="201">[201] OpenGlass: A Sensing-Computing Split Architecture for Local MLLM-Driven Real-Time Visual Assistance</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03213](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03213) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mengzhang Li, Yuan Yao
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.HC
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Edge Computing, Accessibility Technology
- **Venue**: ACL 2026
- **Summary**: 云端MLLM助手存在隐私和延迟问题。提出OpenGlass，采用传感-计算分离架构，ESP32眼镜端捕捉视觉，本地设备推理，实现低延迟视觉辅助。中位延迟993ms，97.5%低于2秒。

<h3 id="202">[202] Builder, Defender, Breaker: The Case Against Removing the Human from the AI-Driven Security Lifecycle</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03215](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03215) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mohamed Chahine Ghanem
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.SE
- **Tags**: AI Security, Cybersecurity, Human-AI Collaboration
- **Summary**: 当构建、防御和测试系统来自同一生成模型时，盲点共享，独立性丧失。论文论证人在安全生命周期中不可替代，需要永久保留人类监督，并提出了人机分工原则。

<h3 id="203">[203] CONTRA: Red-Teaming Configurations of Personalizable Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03220](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03220) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jonathan Nöther, Adish Singla, Goran Radanovic
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Security, AI Safety, Red Teaming
- **Summary**: 可个性化智能体存在配置被利用执行恶意动作的风险。提出CONTRA，一种LLM辅助树搜索算法，发现导致恶意动作的配置。实验发现75.1%的技能至少有一个恶意配置。

<h3 id="204">[204] Agentic and Generative AI for Open-Source Intelligence and Cyber Investigations: Taxonomy, Evaluation, Challenges, and Future Directions</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03233](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03233) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Eduardo Almeida Palmieri, Mohamed Chahine Ghanem, Dipo Dunsin, Zubair Baig, Ed de Quincey, Kim-Kwang Raymond Choo
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.IR; cs.SI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Cybersecurity, Survey
- **Summary**: 开源情报分析因数据增长而不足，LLM和智能体成为解决方案但评估框架滞后。系统综述74篇研究，建立11类分类法，识别幻觉验证差距，映射OSINT生命周期，提出10点研究议程。

<h3 id="205">[205] Unbiased Alignment for Large Language Models with Noisy Preferences</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03248](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03248) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jialiang Wang, Xianming Liu, Xiong Zhou, Hui Liu, Haoliang Li
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, Preference Optimization, Label Noise
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/cswjl/unbiased-alignment)
- **Summary**: 人类偏好数据存在噪声，影响LLM对齐。提出无偏奖励模型和无偏DPO损失，从数学上校正噪声导致的偏差，实现无偏训练。理论分析和实验证明方法优于基线。

<h3 id="206">[206] Semantic Segmentation-Driven Image-Level Diagnosis of Liver Cancers in Hematoxylin and Eosin Histopathology Images</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03253](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03253) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ivica Kopriva, Dario Sitnik, Arijana Pacic, Karolina Krstanac, Irena Veliki Dalic, Marijana Popovic Hadzija
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical Imaging, Semantic Segmentation, Cancer Diagnosis
- **Summary**: H&E染色图像自动分析受变异性和标注噪声挑战。提出基于语义分割的图像级诊断框架，使用nnU-Net在三种肝癌类别上训练。五折交叉验证平衡精度达0.975、0.950、1.000。

<h3 id="207">[207] A harmonised dataset for Earth system foundation models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03298](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03298) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Carlos Rodriguez-Pardo, Massimo Tavoni
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; econ.GN
- **Tags**: Foundation Model, Dataset, Earth Observation
- **Summary**: 现有地球系统基础模型主要训练于物理气候数据，缺少人类系统数据。提出WorldTensor，将数百个环境和社会经济变量统一到0.25°网格和年度时间框架，为多模态地球系统基础模型提供资源。

<h3 id="208">[208] Is Agentic Code Review Helpful? Mining Developers' Feedback to CodeRabbit Reviews in the Wild</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03316](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03316) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hong Yi Lin, Mingzhao Liang, Kla Tantithamthavorn, Patanamon Thongtanunam
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Code Review, Software Engineering, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 智能体代码审查在实践中接受度混合。分析31073对审查和开发者反馈，36.4%被接受，56.3%被拒绝。拒绝主要源于无效建议和与开发者意图不匹配。轻量学习方法可预测拒绝。

<h3 id="209">[209] Hierarchical Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Carbon-Aware AI Data Centers in Power Distribution Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03324](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03324) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hyunsoo Lee, Panggah Prabawa, Dae-Hyun Choi, Joongheon Kim
- **Subjects**: eess.SY; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Multi-Agent System
- **Summary**: 针对人工智能数据中心高能耗导致的碳排放问题，提出分层碳感知多智能体强化学习框架（CA-MARL），通过工作负载管理器和本地数据中心智能体协同工作，利用多智能体Transformer进行时空任务分配和冷却控制。在IEEE 33节点配电系统上验证了有效性。

<h3 id="210">[210] From Judgments to Issues: Structured Extraction of Legal Reasoning with Citation-Hallucination Control</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03325](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03325) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Giovanni Piccioli, Alessia Fidelangeli, Piera Santin, Pierpaolo Vivo
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.IR
- **Tags**: Legal AI, Information Extraction
- **Summary**: 针对意大利税务法院判决文书的结构化信息提取问题，提出自动化流水线，利用DeepSeek V3将判决分解为法律问题并生成IRAC框架的XML表示，同时结合Linkoln解析器进行引文幻觉检测。在50份专家标注的判决上验证，达到良好的注释一致性。

<h3 id="211">[211] SPORK: Self-Speculative Forking to Accelerate Agentic LLM Inference</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03333](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03333) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Huajun Bai, Weiwei Lv, Huichuan Zheng, Youyou Lu, Jiwu Shu
- **Subjects**: cs.DC; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Speculative Decoding, LLM Inference
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/baihuajun24/spork)
- **Summary**: 针对LLM智能体在工具调用时GPU空闲导致的延迟问题，提出SPORK，一种无需训练的自我推测分叉控制器，通过提前推测并执行工具调用，与链式思考解码重叠，将Qwen3-32B在GAIA上的P95延迟降低18%（从131.9秒到108.1秒），且不影响任务准确率。

<h3 id="212">[212] FedAvg for HAR: Exploring the Tradeoff Between Personalized and Generalization Accuracy</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03334](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03334) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Andrea De Luna, Susanna Peretti, Chiara Contoli, Alessandro Bogliolo
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Federated Learning, Human Activity Recognition, Personalized Federated Learning
- **Summary**: 针对联邦学习中个性化与泛化能力的权衡问题，基于FedAvg算法在人类活动识别（HAR）领域设计多种测试场景。实验表明，FedAvg在保持较高泛化能力的同时具有更好的个性化，但在类别分布变化等压力条件下优势不明显。

<h3 id="213">[213] Efficient Decentralized Multi-task Dataset Valuation via Model Merging</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03346](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03346) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mohammadsajad Alipour, Mohammad Mohammadi Amiri
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CV; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Model Merging, Data Valuation, Multi-Task Learning
- **Summary**: 针对多任务模型在去中心化环境下的数据集估值问题，提出DMVM框架，利用任务算术从模型组合中推断数据集贡献，无需重新训练或共享数据。通过安全聚合协议支持去中心化部署，在计算机视觉和自然语言处理任务上验证了其可扩展性和效率。

<h3 id="214">[214] PedestrianDiffusion: Multimodal Generative Denoising and Dense State Estimation for Inertial Navigation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03349](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03349) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: I-Hao Lu, Dongsoo Han
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, State Estimation, Inertial Navigation
- **Summary**: 针对消费级惯性导航中MEMS噪声导致的估计抖动问题，提出PedestrianDiffusion，一种多模态频域生成框架，将稠密6D状态估计视为连续条件去噪过程。采用零样本语义条件化和单步确定性ODE求解器，在多个基准数据集上达到最优性能，对脉冲扰动和运动漂移具有强鲁棒性。

<h3 id="215">[215] LLM-Enhanced Hierarchical Heterogeneous Graph Representation Learning for Malicious Python Package Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03350](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03350) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hang Gao, Xiaoyu Chen, Baoquan Cui, Zhen Tang, Peng Qiao, Fengge Wu, Jian Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.SE
- **Tags**: Graph Neural Network, Malware Detection, Supply Chain Attack
- **Summary**: 针对恶意Python包检测中程序实体的层次组织和异构交互建模不足的问题，提出LLM增强的层次异构图表示学习框架。通过构建层次异构代码图并利用LLM推断函数级语义角色，结合层次异构GNN进行类型感知消息传递，在真实数据集上超越现有方法，并提供可解释的恶意行为定位。

<h3 id="216">[216] Brand-as-Memory: Vision-Language Models Encode Causal, Mechanistically Localizable Credibility Priors for News Sources</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03365](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03365) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chih-Ting Liao, Xin Cao
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Mechanistic Interpretability, AI Ethics
- **Summary**: 针对视觉语言模型（VLM）在阅读新闻内容时过度依赖来源品牌信誉而非内容本身的问题，提出CueTrust诊断基准，发现品牌标识（报头、标志）能在约11个log-odds范围内改变可信度，且与专业评级高度相关（rho=0.88）。机械性解释表明该先验形成于第19-21层，通过稀疏自编码器特征可定位，干预可降低41%的覆盖效应。

<h3 id="217">[217] Spectral Signatures of Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03377](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03377) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhuoying Zhang, Ishan V. Prasad, Yuanzhe Hu, Zihang Liu, Hengrui Luo, Pu Ren, Yaoqing Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Model Fingerprinting
- **Summary**: 针对大规模开源LLM集合的管理与量化问题，基于重尾自正则化理论，采用权重经验谱密度的谱形状作为紧凑谱签名。该签名数据无关、计算高效且尺度不变，支持模型血统追踪、无监督聚类和性能量化，在多种开源LLM家族上验证。

<h3 id="218">[218] The S-ICDF Dataset: Sionna-Simulated Dynamic Interference Characterization and Direction Finding</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03411](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03411) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Christian Wielenberg, Lucas Heublein, Jonathan Ott, Alexander Mattick, Nisha L. Raichur, Jonas Pirkl, Lukas Schelenz, Tobias Feigl, George Yammine, Christopher Mutschler, Felix Ott
- **Subjects**: eess.SP; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Dataset, Direction of Arrival, Wireless Networks
- **Summary**: 针对无线和卫星导航中干扰监测（干扰/欺骗）缺乏大规模数据集的问题，利用Sionna模拟生成S-ICDF室内干扰数据集，涵盖102种配置，包括天线阵列模式、带宽、噪声等变量。提供经典测向方法（MUSIC、ESPRIT、CAPON）和机器学习方法的基线结果。

<h3 id="219">[219] DETECT-3B-Omni is Agnostic of Content and Demographics</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03418](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03418) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nicolas M. Müller, Aditya Tirumala Bukkapatnam, Dominik Schnieders, Zohaib Ahmed
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Deepfake Detection, Audio Processing, Fairness
- **Summary**: 针对深度伪造音频检测器可能对内容或说话人人口统计属性存在偏见的问题，使用10240个音频样本进行大规模等价性测试。结果表明，DETECT-3B-Omni在不同内容类型、性别、年龄和地区间的准确率差异不超过2个百分点（99%置信度），实现了语义无关检测。

<h3 id="220">[220] Securing Multi-Tool AI Agent Chains With Dynamic, Real-Time Compositional Policies</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03423](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03423) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chris Schneider, Kriti Faujdar, Philipp Schoenegger, Ben Bariach
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, AI Security, Guardrail
- **Summary**: 针对多工具AI智能体链的复合安全风险问题，提出动态安全控制组合器（DSCC），通过阶段1的静态最限制集算法和阶段2的运行时污点追踪实现深度防御。在32个工具和16项NIST策略上，默认清除模式阻断79.2%的策略对和95.5%的三元组，污点模式分别阻断42.5%和60.5%。

<h3 id="221">[221] Amortising Bayesian Experimental Design for Sequential Information Gathering in LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03426](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03426) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jakob Hartmann, James Harvey, Jhonathan Navott, Erik Y. Wang, Luckeciano C. Melo, Flaviu Cipcigan, Cheng Zhang, Alessandro Abate
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Bayesian Optimization, Active Learning
- **Venue**: FoGen 2026
- **Summary**: 针对LLM在多轮交互中信息收集能力不足的问题，提出摊销顺序信息收集（ASIG）微调方法，通过多轮GRPO将贝叶斯实验设计摊销到LLM策略中。在20 Questions任务上，ASIG将7B基础模型的成功率提升一倍以上，推理成本相比BED-LLM降低超过25倍，并泛化到医学诊断基准MediQ。

<h3 id="222">[222] No Time Like the Present: Agentic Test-Time Training for LLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03441](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03441) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yanbo Wang, Jinhua Hao, Yuze Shi, Kun Yuan, Ming Sun
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Test-Time Adaptation, LLM Inference
- **Summary**: 针对LLM智能体在长轨迹中退化的问题，提出智能体测试时训练（aTTT），通过标记级重加权降低重复n-gram上的损失。利用vLLM的运行时LoRA API，开销仅为无TTT的1.9倍，在ALFWorld和SWE-bench Lite上分别提升成功率达5.0和4.9个百分点。

<h3 id="223">[223] TRIAGE: Trustworthy Retrieval Instrumentation And Graph Evaluation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03447](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03447) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Axel TahmasebiMoradi, Lucas Schott, Martin Royer
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: RAG, Knowledge Graph, Evaluation
- **Summary**: 针对基于图的检索增强生成（Graph-RAG）系统的评估问题，提出TRIAGE框架，为知识图谱实现、验证和使用三个阶段提供阶段感知度量。通过必要条件诊断链定位故障，部分度量无需标注数据，并提供了可复现的评估协议。

<h3 id="224">[224] HiMe: Hierarchical Embodied Memory for Long-Horizon Vision-Language-Action Control</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03449](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03449) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Li Ji, Siyin Wang, Pengfang Qian, Xiaopeng Yu, Yihai Tian, Zhaoye Fei, Jingjing Gong, Xipeng Qiu
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language-Action, Embodied AI, Memory Architecture
- **Summary**: 针对视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在长时域任务中因依赖即时观测而表现不佳的问题，提出分层具身记忆框架HiMe，包括高频执行器、工作记忆哨兵和长期规划器。通过跨模态语义模式和主动管理机制实现记忆灵活性，在长时域任务中显著提升成功率，并具备根据人类偏好自我修正的能力。

<h3 id="225">[225] SkillOpt-Lite: Better and Faster Agent Self-evolution via One Line of Vibe</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03451](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03451) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yifei Shen, Bo Li, Xinjie Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Agent Self-Evolution, Skill Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/EvolvingLMMs-Lab/SkillOpt-Lite)
- **Summary**: 论文针对现有智能体技能优化流程复杂的问题，提出最小化可行方案SkillOpt-Lite，基于零阶优化和PAC学习理论，通过文件系统轨迹探索、共识属性挖掘和独立验证门控加速收敛。在LiveMath上GPT-5.5提升8.8分，GPT-5.4-nano超越标准GPT-5.4，并集成至生产代码智能体。

<h3 id="226">[226] Best-of-Better-$N$: Generating Pre-Aligned Responses with In-Context Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03453](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03453) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Eric Lei, Hsiang Hsu, Chun-Fu Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: In-Context Learning, LLM Alignment
- **Summary**: 针对推理时对齐方法受限于参考LLM响应质量的问题，提出Best-of-Better-N (BoBN) 框架，利用上下文学习从高奖励响应中检索并改写示例，将采样分布向高奖励区域偏移。在安全对齐和数学推理基准上，BoBN在固定N下取得更好性能，或更小N达到目标性能。

<h3 id="227">[227] CaresAI at SMM4H-HeaRD 2026: Predicting TNM Staging</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03466](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03466) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Joseph Itopa Abubakar, Jorge Jarme, Favour Igwezeke, Mary Adewunmi
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Text Classification, Multi-Label Learning
- **Summary**: 该研究旨在利用TCGA病理报告独立预测TNM分期标签，作为多标签分类任务。探索了TF-IDF和多种BERT嵌入结合LR、LightGBM、FFNN和WRN等方法。LightGBM+TF-IDF在训练阶段AUROC达0.9368（T），但测试集性能下降，表明模型泛化性和类别不平衡问题。

<h3 id="228">[228] Towards Diverse and Comprehensive Benchmarks for Mutual Information Estimation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03487](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03487) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Alberto Foresti, Ivan Butakov, Alexander Tolmachev, Giulio Franzese, Alexey Frolov, Pietro Michiardi
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.IT
- **Tags**: Mutual Information Estimation, Benchmark, Information Theory
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/VanessB/mutinfo)
- **Summary**: 针对互信息估计基准过于简化的问题，提出基于copula理论的全面基准框架，包含两类测试族，系统评估三类估计器。发现没有通用获胜者，不同设置下各估计器可系统超越其他，并识别了基本估计障碍。

<h3 id="229">[229] STRATOS: Bridging the Symbolic-to-Numeric Gap in Spatio-Temporal Text-to-SQL for Meteorological Data</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03501](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03501) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yi Zhang, Farhad Nooralahzadeh, Jonathan Fürst, Fabio Scherrer, Antonis Bezes, Vassiliki Kotroni, Kurt Stockinger
- **Subjects**: cs.DB; cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Text-to-SQL, Geospatial AI, Spatiotemporal Reasoning
- **Summary**: 针对气象数据访问需要编程技能且通用Text-to-SQL系统无法处理符号-数值鸿沟的问题，提出STRATOS框架，通过时空分辨率智能体在SQL生成前解决模糊性，并利用复杂度感知查询重写将执行时间从小时级降至秒级。在7520个复杂查询对上进行评估。

<h3 id="230">[230] Reading Between the Dots: Decoding Hidden Computation across Filler Tokens</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03502](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03502) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kaley Brauer, Claudio Mayrink Verdun, Samuel Marks
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Mechanistic Interpretability, LLM Reasoning, KV Cache
- **Venue**: ICML 2026 Mech Interp Workshop
- **Summary**: 针对前沿LLM在填充标记上的多步推理缺乏行为监控的问题，揭示其注意力和残差流中的可读计算模式。提出无监督解码流水线，仅使用隐藏状态恢复中间值，准确率80-95%，证明可监控性存在于完整计算轨迹而非表面标记。

<h3 id="231">[231] CAGE-1: Control, Assurance, and Governance Evaluation for Enterprise Agentic AI</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03510](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03510) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Roopam W. Sure
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.CY
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, AI Governance, Agent Evaluation
- **Summary**: 针对企业级智能体部署缺乏全面评估框架的问题，提出CAGE-1评估框架，涵盖权限、策略执行、检索质量、记忆完整性、工具安全、可审计性等12个维度，并引入预绑定保证概念。该框架测试行动在被绑定前是否受到控制。

<h3 id="232">[232] AGL-1: The Enterprise AI Governance Layer as a Control Plane for Trusted Enterprise Intelligence</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03516](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03516) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Roopam W. Sure
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.CY
- **Tags**: AI Governance, AI Infrastructure, Enterprise AI
- **Summary**: 针对企业AI从实验转向运营依赖时缺乏治理控制面的问题，提出AGL-1参考模型，作为跨模型、检索、编排等组件的控制平面，定义七个治理域。核心主张是企业AI的信任是系统属性而非模型属性。

<h3 id="233">[233] Aligning Language Models with Selective Prediction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03528](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03528) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Gaoxiang Luo, Yifan Wu, Sinian Zhang, Aryan Deshwal, Ju Sun
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, Selective Prediction, Uncertainty Estimation
- **Venue**: ICML 2026 Agents in the Wild: Safety, Security, and Beyond Workshop
- **Summary**: 针对LLM在高风险场景中的可靠性问题，提出通过选择性预测（SP）改善风险-覆盖率权衡，并设计RLSR框架以AURC为目标进行对齐。RLSR在域内和域外任务上取得更优的风险-覆盖率权衡。

<h3 id="234">[234] Latent Clarity: Bridging World-Model Kinematics to Semantic Manifolds for Video Anomaly Anticipation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03558](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03558) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Abu Anas Ibn Samad
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Video Anomaly Detection, World Model, Anomaly Anticipation
- **Summary**: 针对连续视频异常检测中被动MIL方法的问题，提出PULS管线，包含KSD桥和ASP模块。KSD桥将物理张量映射到文本对齐超球面，在UCF-Crime上达到0.8994 AUC，并验证潜在清晰性假说：预期未来表示比观测表示更语义可分。

<h3 id="235">[235] EPRA U-Net: An Efficient Pyramid Residual Attention Framework for Accurate Infarct Segmentation in Diffusion-Weighted MRI</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03568](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03568) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hasan Ulutas, Muhammet Emin Sahin, Mustafa Fatih Erkoc, Esra Yuce, Turker Tuncer, Sengul Dogan, Serkan Kiranyaz
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Medical Image Segmentation, Attention Mechanism
- **Summary**: 针对DWI图像上急性缺血性梗死准确分割的需求，提出EPRA U-Net架构，集成EfficientNet编码器、R2块、ASPP和双注意力机制，采用Tversky损失优化。在167例患者数据上，Dice达0.8984，漏诊率分别降低16%、25%和29%对比三个SOTA模型。

<h3 id="236">[236] Teacher Supervision over Representation Equivalence Classes</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03572](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03572) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sang Il Han
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 针对知识蒸馏中教师表示存在等价类的问题，指出绝对特征匹配是不适定的，应匹配类不变量（Gram结构、CKA等）。实验证明输出函数匹配驱动能力恢复，而隐藏表示匹配仅对齐几何，且恢复仅限于语料覆盖区域。

<h3 id="237">[237] Differentiate the Evaluator, Not the Program: An Efficient Runtime Representation for Neuro-Symbolic Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03574](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03574) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Lucas Sheneman
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.PL
- **Tags**: Neuro-Symbolic AI, Program Synthesis, Scientific Discovery
- **Summary**: 针对程序与参数协同搜索中参数校准成为瓶颈的问题，提出NDVM运时表示，将符号结构与可微分数字状态分离，实现大批量参数向量上单一评估器遍历的梯度记录。在固定预算下，NDVM加速约24倍。

<h3 id="238">[238] PLGSA-Transformer: Periocular Landmark-Guided Attention with Occlusion-Adaptive Cosine Thresholding for Cross-Modal Masked and Unmasked Face Recognition</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03581](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03581) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dana A Abdullah
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Face Recognition, Occlusion Handling, Vision Transformer
- **Summary**: 针对面部口罩遮挡导致传统人脸识别系统失效的问题，提出PLGSA-Transformer框架，利用MediaPipe眼周地标引导空间注意力和混合CNN-Transformer，以及遮挡自适应余弦阈值。在858张图像上达97.22%验证准确率，AUC 1.0。

<h3 id="239">[239] Responsibility Distribution Estimation in Ego-View Accident Videos with Multimodal Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03591](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03591) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ryosei Tamura, Andrew Shin
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CY
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Video Understanding, Autonomous Driving
- **Summary**: 针对现有交通事故事故理解依赖基础设施摄像头难以捕捉驾驶员视角的问题，提出用自车视角事故视频进行责任分配估计的新任务。构建LLM辅助标注管线，微调多模态大语言模型，在多种输入设置下建立初始基准，证明自车视频可有效支持多模态推理。

<h3 id="240">[240] An Interpretable Deep Learning Framework for Discovery and Clinical Validation of Deep Radiomic Signatures in Tumor Classification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03593](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03593) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chengkun Sun, Jinqian Pan, Renjie Liang, Zhengkang Fan, Xin Miao, Yi Guo, Mei Liu, Muxuan Liang, Russell Terry, Jie Xu
- **Subjects**: eess.IV; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Interpretability, Radiomics
- **Summary**: 针对深度学习在影像学特征发现中可解释性不足的问题，提出集成分割、可解释分类和影像组学分析的可解释框架，通过Grad-CAM和互信息自适应阈值提取患者特异性影像学签名。在多个数据集上验证，相比全肿瘤影像组学，签名方法具有更好的判别性能和生物可解释性。

<h3 id="241">[241] Token-Based Affordance Grounding with Large Vision-Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03595](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03595) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Seung Il Lee, Qinqian Lei, Daguang Xu, Dong Yang, Robby T. Tan, Yixin Chen, Bo Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.RO
- **Tags**: Affordance Learning, Vision-Language Model, Zero-Shot Learning
- **Venue**: ECCV 2026
- **Summary**: 针对现有可操作性引导方法在视觉模糊和语义相似动作上定位不准的问题，提出TokAG框架，利用大视觉语言模型中的token级语义-空间信号和空间感知token选择机制生成零样本可操作性热力图。在AGD20K和HICO-IIF基准上，NSS分别提升10.7%和29.7%。

<h3 id="242">[242] They Infer What You Meant: Models Represent Communicative Intent More Reliably Than They Act On It</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03598](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03598) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Alex Kwon
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Interpretability, Intent Understanding, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 针对语言模型常回应表面语义而非发送者交际意图的问题，将意图作为可解释性对象，通过线性探针从隐藏状态解码意图，并发现表示与行为存在深度及模型间差距；通过方向干预可恢复意图行为。跨6个模型验证了意图表示的鲁棒性和干预有效性。

<h3 id="243">[243] A Step Towards Robust Unsupervised Domain Adaptation via Fine-Tuning and Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03600](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03600) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sushant Dagaji Desale, Rahul Mishra, Ashutosh Kumar Sinha
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Domain Adaptation, Adversarial Robustness, Reinforcement Learning
- **Venue**: ECAI 2025
- **Summary**: 针对无监督域适应中对抗鲁棒性差、伪标签噪声等问题，提出SFT+RL两阶段框架，先对抗微调CLIP视觉编码器，再通过置信度引导伪标签和强化学习训练。在OfficeHome、PACS、VisDA上平均提升干净准确率10.2%，对抗鲁棒性15.8%。

<h3 id="244">[244] RADIO1D: Elastic Representations for Condensed Vision Modeling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03624](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03624) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Greg Heinrich, Mike Ranzinger, Collin McCarthy, Natan Bagrov, Eugene Khvedchenya, Bryan Catanzaro, Jan Kautz, Andrew Tao, Pavlo Molchanov
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Knowledge Distillation, Model Compression
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 针对视觉语言模型依赖固定2D特征的问题，提出RADIO1D，将图像压缩为可变长度1D token序列，采用多教师知识蒸馏和自编码器设计。单个token即可理解场景，支持调整token数量实现灵活精度-效率权衡，在多模态基准上性能竞争且开销更低。

<h3 id="245">[245] An AI-Assisted Solution to the Signed BAR Conjecture: Uniqueness in the Harrison--Reiman Class and a Completely-$\mathcal{S}$ Class Obstruction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03639](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03639) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yiping Lu, Youheng Zhu
- **Subjects**: math.PR; cs.AI; cs.NI; math.ST
- **Tags**: Mathematical Reasoning, AI-assisted Proof
- **Summary**: 解决了多维反射扩散平稳分布唯一性的35年未决问题，利用路径可微性和方向导数证明在Harrison-Reiman M-矩阵类中有限符号唯一性，并在完全-S类中构造反例。证明由AI辅助发现并验证。

<h3 id="246">[246] Revealing Hidden Model Behaviors with Task-Specific Self-Reports</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03640](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03640) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Taras Kutsyk, Bartosz Zieliński
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Safety, Interpretability, LoRA
- **Summary**: 针对微调模型可能产生隐藏行为且现有检测方法不可靠的问题，提出SAR轻量LoRA适配器，使模型用自然语言描述自身隐藏行为。在7个植入行为上全部检测到，且幻觉率减半，优于现有基线。

<h3 id="247">[247] Moonstone: A Multimodal Foundation Model and Benchmark for Lunar Remote Sensing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03644](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03644) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ayush Prasad, Swarnalee Mazumder
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Remote Sensing, Vision Foundation Model, Self-Supervised Learning
- **Venue**: ECCV 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/ayushprd/Moonstone)
- **Summary**: 针对月球遥感数据分散、缺乏多模态基准的问题，提出Moonstone，包含28通道全球预训练数据集、MG-MAE（模态分组掩码自编码器）和6个下游任务基准。预训练特征在所有任务上大幅优于基线。

<h3 id="248">[248] ClinOCR-Bench: A Comprehensive Clinical Scanned Document Dataset for Optical Character Recognition Model Evaluation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03650](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03650) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Enshuo Hsu, Jin Zhou, Kirk Roberts
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Document Understanding, OCR, Medical AI
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/ClinOCR-Bench/ClinOCR-Bench)
- **Summary**: 针对临床OCR缺乏公开评估数据集和系统基准的问题，发布ClinOCR-Bench，包含384张扫描图像，覆盖手写、低质量、旋转、表格等伪影，且无隐私信息。评估了多种VLM的基线性能。

<h3 id="249">[249] ELiTeFormer: An Efficient Transformer for FPGAs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03652](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03652) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Victor Agostinelli, Nicolas Bohm Agostini, Antonino Tumeo
- **Subjects**: cs.AR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: FPGA, Quantization, Linear Attention
- **Summary**: 针对Transformer在FPGA部署中计算和内存挑战，提出ELiTeFormer，统一混合线性注意力和三元量化，并设计无乘法PE微架构。实现10倍权重压缩和12.8倍KV缓存压缩，FPGA部署相比GPU能效提升3.2倍。

<h3 id="250">[250] AutoCedar: An Agentic Framework for Verifier-Guided Access Control Policy Synthesis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03656](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03656) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Adarsh Vatsa, Sachi Shome, Yingming Zhou, William Eiers
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.CR
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Code Generation, Program Verification
- **Summary**: 针对LLM生成访问控制策略可能包含未批准权限的问题，提出AutoCedar，将自然语言需求分解为可审查意图原子，通过验证器检查策略并生成修复信号。在CedarBench全部221个任务上收敛，并在医疗、教育等案例中验证。

<h3 id="251">[251] ViPo-MLLM: Visual-Pose Multimodal LLM for Gloss-Free Sign Language Translation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03657](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03657) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ahmed Abul Hasanaath, Bicheng Xu, Mir Rayat Imtiaz Hossain, Leonid Sigal, Hamzah Luqman
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Sign Language Translation, Vision-Language Model, Multimodal Learning
- **Summary**: 针对无标注手语翻译中细粒度建模和特征融合不足的问题，提出ViPo-MLLM，融合时空RGB和人体姿态特征，通过跨模态注意力和结构化提示实现翻译。在PHOENIX14T和CSL-Daily上达到新SOTA，接近有标注方法。

<h3 id="252">[252] A Fair Benchmarking of Deep Relational Database Learning Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03659](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03659) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kazi F. Akhter, Bharath Ajendla, Manar D. Samad
- **Subjects**: cs.DB; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Tabular Learning, Database, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 针对深度关系数据库学习方法缺乏公平比较的问题，进行系统基准测试，统一评估5个数据库的分类和回归任务。发现关系Transformer最优，多表连接提升性能但收益递减。

<h3 id="253">[253] Phase-Preserving Trimodal Transformer for Tropical Forest Biomass Estimation Using Optical and PolInSAR Data</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03663](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03663) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Luiz Felipe Parente Santiago, Rosiane Rodrigues de Freitas, Daniel Rodrigues dos Santos, Felipe Ferrari
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Remote Sensing, Transformer Architecture, Physics-Informed Learning
- **Summary**: 针对热带森林生物量估计中SAR信号饱和和云覆盖问题，提出TCCT，融合光学和极化干涉SAR数据，使用复值编码器保持相位相干性。局部微调后RMSE为3.78米，rRMSE为4.51%，满足ESA任务要求。

<h3 id="254">[254] Don't Blame the Large Language Model: How Scaffolding Evolution Shapes Coding Agent Quality</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03691](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03691) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Oussama Ben Sghaier, Hao Li, Bram Adams, Ahmed E. Hassan
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Code Generation, Software Engineering
- **Summary**: 针对编码代理质量退化常被归因于LLM而忽视脚手架演化的问题，首次纵向控制研究固定模型变化脚手架。评估35个版本，揭示极端发布速度，将质量波动追溯到特定开发模式和架构组件。

<h3 id="255">[255] OmniTacTune: Policy-Agnostic Real-World RL for Tactile Residual Adaptation of Visual Policies</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03723](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03723) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kelin Yu, Haode Zhang, Harish Ravichandar, Yunhai Han, Ruohan Gao
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Robotic Manipulation, Tactile Sensing
- **Summary**: 针对视觉策略在接触丰富操作中失败且触觉数据难收集的问题，提出OmniTacTune，通过自主回滚启动触觉学习，再在线学习轻量残差策略。在4个真实任务中成功率从5-40%提升至85-100%，训练仅40-80分钟。

<h3 id="256">[256] CoGen3D: An Agentic Human-AI Co-Design Pipeline for 3D Asset Generation for Virtual Reality</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03731](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03731) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Weiwei Jiang, Wanyu He, Zheyu Tan, Zheyuan Kuang, Difeng Yu, Shinobu Hasegawa, Sven Mayer, Zhanna Sarsenbayeva
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: 3D Generation, Human-Computer Interaction, Virtual Reality
- **Summary**: 针对VR 3D资产创建需要专业知识的问题，提出CoGen3D，代理型人机协同设计管道，通过对话引导意图、概念图像确认和图像到3D生成。用户研究显示协同设计提升场景参与度，对话模式受环境影响。

<h3 id="257">[257] Attending to Multimodal Generation One Token at a Time</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03738](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03738) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Varun Gupta, Vineet Gandhi, Makarand Tapaswi
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, LLM Inference, Attention Mechanism
- **Summary**: 该论文研究了多模态大语言模型在生成过程中令牌级别的注意力动态，发现注意力集中在图像、指令和历史生成令牌上，并提出了一个简单的测试时干预方法，通过增强相关模态的注意力来显著提升多模态任务性能。

<h3 id="258">[258] A Failure-Mode Benchmark for Polymorphic Sybil Poisoning in RAG</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03739](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03739) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Donghyun Lee, Juntae Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: RAG, LLM Security, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了一个针对RAG检索中毒攻击的基准和评估框架，引入了多态Sybil中毒攻击，比单态攻击的劫持率高出5.7倍，并揭示了仅靠攻击成功率无法捕捉的失败模式。

<h3 id="259">[259] EmCom-Diffusion: Probing Visual Reflection in Emergent Languages via Image Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03752](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03752) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Haruumi Omoto, Tadahiro Taniguchi
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Emergent Communication, Diffusion Model, Vision-Language Model
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了EmCom-Diffusion评估框架，通过微调文本到图像扩散模型从涌现语言消息中重建图像，直接测量涌现语言的视觉反映，捕捉了其他指标遗漏的视觉内容。

<h3 id="260">[260] FedACT: Federated Adaptive Coordinate Trust Modulation for Robust Transformer Training under Data Heterogeneity</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03763](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03763) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shuai Li, Qinglin Wang, Ping Luo, Jiahuan Wang, Hongyang Hu, Haotian Mo, Yigui Feng, Ziang Liu, Qisong Xiao, Jie Liu, Tao Sun
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Federated Learning, Transformer Architecture, Data Heterogeneity
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了FedACT方法，在联邦Transformer训练中通过坐标级别的信任度调制来重新分配更新幅度，有效缓解了数据异构性下的坐标信任不匹配，在视觉Transformer、CNN、LLM预训练和微调上均取得一致提升。

<h3 id="261">[261] Self-Improving Diffusion Classifiers with Minority Preference Optimization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03770](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03770) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hyunsoo Kim, Jungmyung Wi, Soobin Um, Donghyun Kim, Suhyun Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Classification, Self-Improvement
- **Summary**: 该论文揭示了扩散分类器在数据流形低密度区域（少数区域）的性能缺陷，并提出MiPO方法通过少数偏好优化提升扩散分类器的零样本分类能力，在五个标准数据集上验证了有效性。

<h3 id="262">[262] SkillFab: An Agent-Native Skill Production Platform</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03780](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03780) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Anjie Xu, Yifeng Cai, Yi Li, Zixing Wang, Zhiyu Zhang, Jingfan Chen, Ruohan Xu, Leye Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Agent Architecture, Tool Learning
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了SkillFab平台，让智能体将缺失能力转化为可审查、可复用的技能，通过Git仓库和MCP接口实现全生命周期管理。

<h3 id="263">[263] Rethinking Depth Pruning for Vision Transformers: A Heterogeneity-Aware Perspective</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03784](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03784) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhenfeng Su, Kang Zhao, Han Bao, Tao Yuan, Zhongzhe Hu, Xianzhi Yu, Wenxuan Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Model Compression, Vision Transformer, Pruning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Efficient-AI-for-All/HetDPT)
- **Summary**: 该论文揭示了现有深度剪枝方法忽略层间异构性的问题，提出HetDPT方法，在DeiT-B上实现1.58倍加速且保持精度，与宽度剪枝结合后加速比达5.19倍，创下极端ViT剪枝的SOTA。

<h3 id="264">[264] Foundations of Equivariant Deep Learning: Unifying Graph and Sheaf Neural Networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03798](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03798) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yoshihiro Maruyama
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Graph Neural Network, Sheaf Neural Network, Equivariant Learning
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了阶等变神经网络（OENN），统一了图神经网络和层神经网络，并证明了连续阶等变映射的通用近似定理，这是层神经网络的首个通用近似定理。

<h3 id="265">[265] Punching Above Their Weight: Classification-Head Fine-Tuning of Tiny Language Models (TLMs) for Verifiable Multiple-Choice Tasks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03801](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03801) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Bhavesh Sood, Jaromir Savelka
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Small Language Model, Instruction Tuning, Question Answering
- **Summary**: 该论文研究小语言模型在可验证多项选择任务上的适应方法，发现分类头微调优于标签生成，在0.6B和1.7B规模上可靠地优于生成式方法，且与零/少样本GPT-3和PaLM竞争力相当。

<h3 id="266">[266] CineMobile: On-Device Image-to-Video Diffusion for Cinematic Camera Motion Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03803](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03803) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xuyao Huang, Zelai Deng, Xu Wang, Xizhong Xiao, Zhijie Deng
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Video Generation, Diffusion Model, Model Compression, Edge Computing
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了CineMobile，通过蒸馏引导剪枝、扩散蒸馏与强化学习、混合后训练量化策略，将图像到视频扩散模型压缩至1GB以下，在移动端实现40倍加速，生成49帧480p视频只需20秒。

<h3 id="267">[267] Probing Low-Level Acoustic Attribute Encoding in CLAP Audio Embeddings</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03806](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03806) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Héctor Martel, Joe Hennessy-Priest, Taemin Cho
- **Subjects**: eess.AS; cs.AI; eess.SP
- **Tags**: Audio Processing, Representation Learning, Probing
- **Venue**: DAFx 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文通过探测框架分析CLAP音频嵌入，发现混响、响度、相对音调近似线性编码，频谱质心需非线性探测，并且线性方向在数据集间几何一致（除音调外）。

<h3 id="268">[268] CGGS: Consistency-Augmented Geometric Gaussian Splatting for Ego-centric 3D Scene Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03819](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03819) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhenyu Sun, Xiaohan Zhang, Qi Liu, Huan Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.GR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: 3D Generation, Gaussian Splatting, Text-to-3D
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了CGGS框架，通过一致性增强的潜在扩散模型和基于互信息深度损失的几何优化，从文本生成以自我为中心的3D场景，在生成一致性和几何结构上优于先前方法。

<h3 id="269">[269] DualView: Preventing Indirect Prompt Injection in Personal AI Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03821](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03821) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Juhee Kim, Woohyuk Choi, Taehyun Kang, Youngmin Kim, Byoungyoung Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Security, LLM Agent, Prompt Injection
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了DualView防御，通过为每个通道提供智能体视图和人类视图，将不可信数据跟踪从智能体上下文扩展到文件系统、网络等环境，有效防止了存储型间接提示注入攻击。

<h3 id="270">[270] Q-TriM: Question-Guided Tri-Modal Attention for Audio-Visual Question Answering</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03825](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03825) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: SungHun Kim, SeungJun Baek
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Audio-Visual Question Answering, Multimodal Learning, Attention Mechanism
- **Venue**: ECCV 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Sunghun95/Q-TriM)
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了Q-TriM，通过并行而非深层堆叠的方式融合视频、音频和文本模态，避免误差累积，在三个AVQA基准上取得SOTA，并在MUSIC-AVQA-R上展示强大泛化能力。

<h3 id="271">[271] How Do Diffusion Classifiers Decide? A Bias-Centric Evaluation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03831](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03831) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Saba Fathi, Fardin Ayar, Maryam Abdolali, Ehsan Javanmardi, Manabu Tsukada, Mahdi Javanmardi
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Bias Mitigation, Model Evaluation
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了ASOB-Bench，从属性绑定、大小顺序偏差和背景依赖三个维度评估扩散分类器的偏差，发现不同于视觉语言模型的偏差特征，为构建鲁棒扩散模型提供指导。

<h3 id="272">[272] Beyond Static Rules: Automated Discovery of Latent Vulnerabilities in Text-to-SQL</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03833](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03833) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hanqing Wang, Yongdong Chi, Jian Yang, Lei Yang, Jiehui Zhao, Yun Chen, Guanhua Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Text-to-SQL, LLM Security, Vulnerability Detection
- **Venue**: Findings of ACL 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了SAGE框架，通过自动生成漏洞假设并利用持续演化的漏洞代码库设计扰动，自主发现Text-to-SQL潜在失败模式，且发现的模式具有跨模型可迁移性。

<h3 id="273">[273] When Simpler Is Better: Evaluating Translation Pipelines for Medieval Latin Manuscripts</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03836](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03836) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nguyen Kim Hai Bui, Md. Easin Arafat, Tamás Gábor Orosz, Mufti Mahmud
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Machine Translation, Low-Resource NLP, Vision-Language Model
- **Summary**: 针对Vision Language Model在处理中世纪拉丁文手稿时因低资源转写、古语词汇和噪声输入导致的性能下降问题，本文提出了一套评估图像到翻译全流程的系统框架，并引入包含1383条对齐手稿图像、转录和专家翻译的Interpres-Parallel-Corpus (IPC)数据集。实验发现，领域专有的OCR模型相比通用VLM可将字符错误率降低最高4.3倍，而最简单的OCR+VLM流水线优于所有多组件变体，添加RAG或后OCR校正反而因提示饱和和错误传播降低翻译质量。

<h3 id="274">[274] High-Fidelity One-Step Generative Visuomotor Policy via Recursive Correction, Frequency Consistency, and Contrastive Flow Matching</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03865](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03865) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuran Chen, Xinye Cai, Zhonglin Gong, Yang Huang
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Robotics, Diffusion Model, Flow Matching
- **Summary**: 针对扩散模型和流匹配在机器人视觉运动策略中多步采样导致推理延迟的问题，本文提出一种高保真单步生成策略框架，包含递归一致动作流、双时间步频率一致性和对比流匹配三种机制。实验表明，该方法在RoboTwin、Adroit等基准和真实机器人上仅需一次前向传播即可达到甚至超越10步生成策略的性能。

<h3 id="275">[275] GeoSelect: Spatial-Program Execution for Training-Free Referring Remote Sensing Image Segmentation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03869](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03869) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuhang Jiang, Guohui Deng, Miaozhong Xu, Chao Ruan, Jinling Zhao, Linsheng Huang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Remote Sensing, Image Segmentation, Referring Segmentation
- **Summary**: 现有无训练遥感图像指代分割方法对空间、比较和顺序关系控制较弱，本文提出GeoSelect，将指代解析为类型化空间程序的执行，利用冻结语言模型合成领域特定语言并由确定性执行器运行。GeoSelect在RRSIS-D上达到58.86 mIoU，是此前最佳无训练方法的两倍以上，且无需指代监督。

<h3 id="276">[276] Next-Gen Sponsored Search: Crafting the Perfect Query with Inventory-Aware RAG (InvAwr-RAG) Based GenAI</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03880](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03880) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Md Omar Faruk Rokon, Weizhi Du, Zhaodong Wang, Musen Wen
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: RAG, Sponsored Search, E-Commerce
- **Venue**: eCom@SIGIR 2024
- **Summary**: 针对大量搜索查询无法检索到赞助广告的问题，本文提出库存感知RAG生成式AI模型InvAwr-RAG，结合高级语义检索和实时库存数据生成查询。初步结果显示，该模型将填充率提升68%，并保持了平衡的相关性指标。

<h3 id="277">[277] Consistent but Miscalibrated: Evaluating LLM Limitations for Risk Communication in Natural Language</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03882](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03882) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Diego Cerda-Mardini, Sarath Chandar, Sreenath Madathil
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Uncertainty Estimation, Uncertainty Calibration
- **Summary**: 评估LLM在自然语言中可靠传达概率信息的能力，在贝塔分布模拟的上游预测中测试9种LLM选择口头描述符的表现。结果表明LLM总体一致但校准不佳，尤其在不确定性任务上表现更弱，提供汇总统计无法解决校准问题。

<h3 id="278">[278] Enhancement of E-commerce Sponsored Search Relevancy with LLM</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03886](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03886) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Md Omar Faruk Rokon, Andrei Simion, Weizhi Du, Musen Wen, Hong Yao, Kuang-chih Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Fine-Tuning, Sponsored Search, E-Commerce
- **Summary**: 针对电商赞助搜索中关键词-查询匹配的挑战，本文使用LLAMA2 7B模型通过LoRA微调开发广告相关性模型，将查询-广告分类为相关、部分相关和不相关。微调后的模型在测试集上达到89.43%的准确率，优于基线和GPT-4。

<h3 id="279">[279] Advanced Topic Modeling Techniques for Categorizing Software Vulnerabilities</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03887](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03887) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Utkarsh Tiwari, Spoorthi M, Anirudh S, Nidhin Prabhakar T. V
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.SE
- **Tags**: Topic Modeling, Cybersecurity, Vulnerability Detection
- **Venue**: ICCCNT 2025
- **Summary**: 为高效分析和优先处理软件漏洞，本研究利用BERTopic、Top2Vec等先进主题建模技术及LLM从漏洞数据中提取洞察，结合UMAP、HDBSCAN等降维聚类方法。结果支持可扩展的自动化漏洞管理，提升网络安全决策。

<h3 id="280">[280] TabQueryBench: A Query-Centric Benchmark for Synthetic Tabular Data</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03926](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03926) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jialin Zhang, Fenghao Dong, Yajie Zhou, Vyas Sekar, Shinan Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.DB; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Tabular Learning, Benchmark, Data Synthesis
- **Summary**: 现有合成表格数据评估忽略分析查询所需的结构，本文提出TabQueryBench，一个以查询为中心的基准，使用SQL分析查询作为结构评估指标。在49个数据集和11个生成模型上，RealTabFormer取得最高查询保真度（0.75±0.15），模型在高基数离散数据和尾部值上表现不佳。

<h3 id="281">[281] TokAN: Accent Normalization Using Self-Supervised Speech Tokens</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03928](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03928) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Qibing Bai, Shuai Wang, Yuhan Du, Bohan Li, Yannan Wang, Haizhou Li
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI; eess.AS
- **Tags**: Speech Processing, Self-Supervised Learning, Accent Normalization
- **Summary**: 针对口音归一化需要平行语音或合成目标导致质量下降的问题，本文提出TokAN，基于自监督离散语音令牌的口音归一化框架，使用自回归编码器-解码器进行令牌转换，并引入GRPO强化学习后训练。实验显示，WER从12.40%降至9.23%，优于帧级和直接流匹配基线。

<h3 id="282">[282] Probe, Don't Prompt: A Hidden-State Probe for Metadata Filtering in Multi-Meta-RAG</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03929](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03929) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mykhailo Poliakov, Nadiya Shvai
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: RAG, LLM Evaluation, Hidden-State Probe
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/mxpoliakov/Multi-Meta-RAG)
- **Summary**: 针对Multi-Meta-RAG中使用昂贵专有LLM进行元数据提取的问题，本文提出基于小型开源模型隐藏状态的确定性探测头，通过浅层选择、均值池化和多标签训练实现元数据过滤。该探测头达到90.9%的集合精确准确率，超越GPT-3.5的80.9%，且135M模型与1.5B模型性能差距在1.5点内。

<h3 id="283">[283] MPSelectTune: Prompt-type Selection for Fine-tuning improves Concept Unlearning in LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03932](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03932) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shubhadip Nag, Srinjoy Das, Agniva Saha, Anushree Ghosh, Soumi Das, Tarun Kumar, Suparna Bhattacharya, Sourangshu Bhattacharya
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Machine Unlearning, LLM Safety, Adversarial Robustness
- **Venue**: NeurIPS 2025 - Reliable ML Workshop
- **Summary**: 针对LLM概念遗忘中不同提示类型影响鲁棒性的问题，本文提出MPSelectTune，一种对抗性方法，使用联合提示进行主任务和概念任务预测，并选择概念准确率最高的提示类型进行微调。在多基准上，该方法相比基线将主任务准确率提升2-15%，最坏情况概念准确率降低最多17%。

<h3 id="284">[284] Why3-py: A Tool for Formal Verification of Hypothesis Testing and Meta-Analysis in Python</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03951](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03951) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Akira Tanaka, Yusuke Kawamoto
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.LO; cs.PL
- **Tags**: Formal Methods, Program Verification, Meta-Analysis
- **Summary**: 为解决统计方法假设隐含导致误用的问题，本文提出Why3-py，一个Python前端，将Python程序转换为验证导向的WhyML表示以进行形式验证，并扩展StatWhy支持元分析方法验证。该工具能帮助识别被忽视的假设和分析误用。

<h3 id="285">[285] The Remarkable Effectiveness of Providing AI Agents with Natural Language Tools: A Replication Study Validating NLT Performance Across 14 Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03953](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03953) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Alexander Somma, Isabelle Plante, Fred Premji
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Tool Learning, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 本文独立复现并扩展了自然语言工具框架，评估14个模型共8560次试验。结果证实NLT将工具调用准确率提升14.9个百分点，关键错误减少93%，令牌使用减少25.2%，且效果依赖于模型能力：无原生工具调用模型、推理模型和小模型获益更大。

<h3 id="286">[286] NormWorlds-CF: Solver-Verified Counterfactual Normative Reasoning with Metamorphic-Relation GRPO</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03957](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03957) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xinqi Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reasoning, Reinforcement Learning, Normative Reasoning
- **Summary**: 针对语言模型可能以错误理由得出正确规范判断的问题，本文提出NormWorlds-CF，一个求解器验证的反事实规范推理环境，并引入变质关系GRPO进行结构化变化任务训练。实验表明MR-GRPO在保留关系准确性和减少错误上优于稀疏和仅答案GRPO，并实现平衡性能。

<h3 id="287">[287] Worldscape-MoE: A Unified Mixture-of-Experts World Model for Scalable Heterogeneous Action Control</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03964](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03964) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jianjie Fang, Yongyan Xu, Ziyou Wang, Chen Gao, Yuchao Huang, Zhaolu Wang, Rongze Tang, Mingyuan Jia, Baining Zhao, Weichen Zhang, Xin Zhang, Haisheng Su, Yu Shang, Wei Wu, Xinlei Chen, Yong Li
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: World Model, Mixture-of-Experts, Diffusion Model
- **Summary**: 针对当前视频生成世界模型因隔离控制接口（相机、机器人、手部等）而碎片化的问题，本文提出Worldscape-MoE，一个基于扩散Transformer的混合专家世界模型，通过模态感知控制注入和共享/控制特定专家实现统一异构动作控制。实验显示异构监督提升各控制能力，并展现扩展行为。

<h3 id="288">[288] Refused in Chat, Written in Code: Workflow-Level Jailbreak Construction in IDE Coding Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03968](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03968) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Abhishek Kumar, Carsten Maple
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Code Generation, Jailbreak Attack
- **Summary**: 针对编码代理的安全性评估仍像聊天机器人一样单轮判断的问题，本文提出工作流级越狱构建，在软件开发工作流的各阶段组合有害目标。在GitHub Copilot的四个后端上，全工作流下816/816提示产生不安全内容，而在直接聊天基线中几乎完全拒绝。

<h3 id="289">[289] Order-based Causal Discovery for Multistage Processes</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03971](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03971) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Eun-Yeol Ma, Junsub Jung, Heeyoung Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Causal Discovery
- **Summary**: 针对现有因果发现方法在多阶段过程中产生反直觉结果且计算效率低的问题，提出Order-based Causal Discovery for Multistage Processes (OCDM)方法，通过结构知识引导的因果顺序推断算法和随机门控神经网络剪枝技术来推断因果结构。实验表明OCDM有效推断多阶段过程因果结构，优于现有方法。

<h3 id="290">[290] Scalable Semantic Steering of Embedding Projections</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03978](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03978) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Wei Liu, Eric Krokos, Kirsten Whitley, Rebecca Faust, Chris North
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Data Visualization, Text Embedding
- **Venue**: IEEE VIS 2026
- **Summary**: 针对LLM增强的语义引导方法中每个项目独立推理成本高的问题，提出将语义计算从单个项目转移到用户定义组的可扩展方法，通过LLM生成组轮廓并与种子质心结合形成混合原型，再通过嵌入空间软分配传播意图。在5K文献语料上达到与逐项引导相当的全局对齐，LLM调用减少三个数量级，并可扩展到多模态嵌入。

<h3 id="291">[291] BanglaMemeEvidence: A Multimodal Benchmark Dataset for Explanatory Evidence Detection in Bengali Memes</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03981](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03981) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Fatema Tuj Johora Faria, Mukaffi Bin Moin, Md. Mahfuzur Rahman, Pronay Debnath, Asif Iftekher Fahim, Faisal Muhammad Shah
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Low-Resource NLP, Dataset
- **Venue**: ICICV 2026
- **Summary**: 针对孟加拉语模因缺乏上下文理解和证据检测问题，构建BanglaMemeEvidence数据集并提出BengaliMemeEvidenceNet混合多模态框架，整合文本和视觉特征。实验F1得分0.74，是首个孟加拉语模因证据检测研究。

<h3 id="292">[292] NouveauVoice: Generating Novel Pseudo Speakers for Voice Anonymization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03985](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03985) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Meiying Melissa Chen, Anastasia Kuznetsova, Zhenyu Wang, Zhiyao Duan
- **Subjects**: eess.AS; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Voice Cloning, Speaker Anonymization
- **Summary**: 针对现有说话人匿名系统身份变化不足的问题，提出NouveauVoice伪说话人生成框架，基于分层深度变分自编码器（NVAE）生成多样化伪说话人嵌入。实现EER超过38%，在匿名性、多样性和语音质量间取得平衡。

<h3 id="293">[293] Full Glyph Images Beat Token Embeddings: A Controlled Study for Transformers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.03994](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03994) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shuyang Xiang, Hao Guan
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Tokenization, Vision Transformer, Chinese Language Modeling
- **Summary**: 针对中文语言模型中离散token嵌入可能非最优的问题，提出用字符序列的栅格化图像替代token嵌入，通过视觉编码器处理，与索引基线共享解码器。视觉模型准确率0.429 vs 0.355（相对提升21%），收敛更快，对字符损坏鲁棒，但优势依赖中文特性。

<h3 id="294">[294] Separating Representation from Reconstruction Enables Scalable Text Encoders</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04011](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04011) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Megi Dervishi, Mathurin Videau, Yann LeCun
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Pre-training, Masked Language Modeling, Text Embedding
- **Summary**: 针对BERT编码器表示在冻结评估中不可利用的问题，提出CrossBERT分离表示学习和令牌重建，支持高掩码率和互补掩码策略。在MTEB和GLUE上实现单调缩放，吞吐量提升1.5-2倍，样本效率提升2倍。

<h3 id="295">[295] When Does Small Data Work? Accuracy and Efficiency Trade-offs Between Tabular Foundation Models and Conventional Methods for Crowd-State Classification at Hajj and Umrah</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04013](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04013) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: AlJawharh S. AlOtaibi, Mohamed Eltahir, Jude AlSubaie
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Tabular Learning, Foundation Model, Few-Shot Learning
- **Summary**: 针对少量标注样本的表格机器学习问题，在朝圣人群监控场景下比较表格基础模型与传统方法。结果表明无单一最优，标签极少时基础模型领先，标签增多时传统方法超越，总结了选择指南。

<h3 id="296">[296] Finite Reliability Representations: Noise-Calibrated Belief-Space Covers for Reliable Decision-Making</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04019](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04019) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hyung-Jin Yoon, Hunmin Kim
- **Subjects**: eess.SY; cs.AI; cs.RO; math.OC
- **Tags**: POMDP, Decision Making, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 针对物理噪声限制决策系统可靠信念分辨率的问题，提出有限可靠性表示（FRR），用可靠性单元覆盖信念空间，保证最优动作值函数变化在容忍度内。在有限POMDP下支持单元常数策略，次优性有界，并引入可靠性熵。

<h3 id="297">[297] A Unified Algebraic Framework for Classification Performance Evaluation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04028](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04028) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ronaldo C. Prati
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Evaluation Methodology, Model Evaluation
- **Summary**: 针对分类性能评估缺乏统一框架的问题，提出基于二元指示矩阵和三个聚合算子（全局、列向、行向）的代数框架，统一涵盖二元、多类、多标签、有序、成本敏感等设置。建立了理论结果并经验证。

<h3 id="298">[298] Efficient Discovery of Conditional Dependencies with Desbordante</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04030](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04030) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ivan Kozhukov, Dmitry Fedoseev, Maksim Emelyanov, Artem Smola, Pyotr Senichenkov, Pavel Anosov, George Chernishev
- **Subjects**: cs.DB; cs.AI; cs.DC; cs.LG; cs.PF
- **Tags**: Conditional Functional Dependency, Data Cleaning
- **Summary**: 针对条件函数依赖（CFD）发现计算代价高的问题，在CFDFinder基础上引入并行化等改进实现ParCFDFinder，集成到Desbordante。速度提升最高318倍，内存减少23倍，可在普通机器上处理数十万行数据集。

<h3 id="299">[299] OmniOpt: Taxonomy, Geometry, and Benchmarking of Modern Optimizers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04033](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04033) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Siyuan Li, Jiabao Pan, Yumou Liu, Zhuoli Ouyang, Xin Jin, Xinglong Xu, Jingxuan Wei, Shengye Pang, Jintao Che, Xuanhe Zhou, Conghui He, Cheng Tan
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Optimization, Benchmark, Training Efficiency
- **Summary**: 针对优化器选择碎片化的问题，提出OmniOpt统一调查和基准，基于五阶段元管道和范数约束线性最小化oracles形成双维度分类法。在多种模型和训练制度上系统分析各方法家族，提供选择坐标系。

<h3 id="300">[300] The "I Don't Know" Filter: Enhancing Agentic Reliability in Function Calling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04034](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04034) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Stefan Broecker, Mason del Rosario, Boris Selitser, Thomas Strohmer
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, LLM Hallucination, Tool Learning
