- **Summary**: 针对语言模型在函数调用中产生幻觉的问题，提出轻量级可训练过滤器来量化不确定性并移除有潜在危害的函数调用。实现智能体知道何时说“不知道”，增强生产可靠性。

<h3 id="301">[301] Reward-Gated On-Policy Distillation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04037](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04037) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mohammad Sadegh Akhondzadeh, Vijay Lingam, Atula Tejaswi, Chanakya Ekbote, Sujay Sanghavi, Aleksandar Bojchevski
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, LLM Reasoning, Reward Model
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/UoC-tail/RG-OPD)
- **Summary**: 针对在策略蒸馏中教师信号不可靠的问题，提出奖励门控在策略蒸馏（RG-OPD），使用验证器反馈决定是否信任教师logits。在推理和编码基准上优于反向KL蒸馏和TSD-KD基线，在1K生成长度上分别提升2.9和4.9点。

<h3 id="302">[302] Telescope: Improving Zero Shot Detection of LLM Generated Content By Measuring Token Repetition Probability</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04061](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04061) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Christopher Nassif, Josh F. Cooper
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG; stat.ML
- **Tags**: AI-Generated Text Detection, LLM Evaluation, Perplexity
- **Summary**: 针对LLM生成文本检测难题，提出Telescope Perplexity指标测量令牌重复概率，利用LLM对重复的早期厌恶作为“遗留启发式”。实现高效零样本检测，多种数据集上达到最先进或竞争性性能。

<h3 id="303">[303] Speaker-Disentangled Chunk-Wise Regression for Syllabic Tokenization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04064](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04064) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ryota Komatsu, Kota Kawakita, Takuma Okamoto, Takahiro Shinozaki
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.SD; eess.AS
- **Tags**: Tokenization, Speech Processing, Spoken Language Model
- **Venue**: IEEE Open Journal of Signal Processing (OJSP)
- **Summary**: 针对无监督音节标记化中模型预测说话人身份而非语言内容的问题，提出说话人解缠的音节标记化器，对说话人扰动的片段表示回归到干净的教师目标。在音节边界检测和聚类上达到最先进，语音语言模型语法语义理解提升7%。

<h3 id="304">[304] Enhancing Implicit Neural Representations with Image Feature Embedding for Unsupervised Cardiac Cine MRI Reconstruction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04069](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04069) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Donghang Lyu, Marius Staring, Yiming Dong, Keupp Jochen, Hildo J. Lamb, Mariya Doneva
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical Imaging, Implicit Neural Representation, Image Reconstruction
- **Summary**: 针对无监督心脏电影MRI重建问题，提出I-FP-INR框架，在隐式神经表示基础上增加特征处理分支提取互补特征嵌入。在公共和内部数据集上一致优于基线方法。

<h3 id="305">[305] Beyond Multilingual Averages: MTEB-PT, a Benchmark for Portuguese Sentence Encoders</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04071](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04071) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Lucas Hideki Takeuchi Okamura, Alexandre Alcoforado, Anna Helena Reali Costa
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Benchmark, Multilingual Learning
- **Venue**: BRACIS 2026
- **Summary**: 针对葡萄牙语在文本嵌入评估中代表性不足的问题，构建MTEB-PT基准，包含14个数据集覆盖STS、分类、检索和重排序。评估17个模型发现，多语言排名不能可靠预测葡萄牙语性能，任务类型依赖性强，语言特定微调仍有效。使用对比学习和MRL微调三个模型，在STS任务上提升最显著。

<h3 id="306">[306] Benchmarking API Drift in LLM-Generated Quantum Code Across Successive SDK Versions</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04072](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04072) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mohammad Arif Rasyidi, Syahirul Faiz
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Code Generation, Quantum Computing, Benchmark
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/arasyi/quantum-api-drift)
- **Summary**: 针对LLM生成的量子代码在不同SDK版本间API漂移问题，提出quantum-api-drift基准，测量版本忠实度。在Qiskit v0.43, v1.3, v2.0上评估17个模型，发现版本对齐是独立评估轴，文档引导修复对迁移到v2.0更有效。

<h3 id="307">[307] Seeing Once is Enough? Online Geometry-Aware Token Pruning for 3D Question Answering</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04079](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04079) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ruei-Chi Lai, Bolivar Solarte, Chin-Hsuan Wu, Yi-Hsuan Tsai, Min Sun
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, 3D Vision, Token Compression
- **Venue**: ICLR 2026 Workshop on Efficient Spatial Reasoning
- **Summary**: 针对3D问答中多视角输入导致高计算成本的问题，提出首个在线token剪枝方法，利用深度信息和相机位姿投影到共享体素空间，识别空间重叠区域并选择性剪枝冗余图像token。在Qwen2.5-VL-7B等模型上测试，减少50% token使用，性能提升。

<h3 id="308">[308] FedSPM: Routing-Enabled Federated Learning under Dual Heterogeneity via Semiparametric Mixture</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04085](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04085) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zijian Wang, Pengfei Li, Guangyu Yang, Qiong Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.DC
- **Tags**: Federated Learning, Non-IID Data
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/zijianwang0510/FedSPM)
- **Summary**: 针对联邦学习中客户端内和客户端间双重异质性问题，提出FedSPM半参数混合框架，用客户端特定潜在组件表示每个客户端，结合分类预测分布和特征分布进行路由。开发联邦EM算法，在医疗数据上提升路由和预测性能。

<h3 id="309">[309] Submitted and Diagnostic Analysis of Full-Text Temporal Retrieval for LongEval-Sci</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04088](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04088) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yingdong Yang, Haijian Wu
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Information Retrieval, Temporal Retrieval
- **Summary**: 针对科学文献检索随时间推移的集合变化问题，报告LongEval-Sci 2026官方结果和诊断分析。比较多种检索方法，发现全文BM25加时间维度是最强基础，RRF提供最佳深度召回，引用特征未见额外增益。

<h3 id="310">[310] DynaVieW: Schema-Guided World Modeling for Understanding Hierarchical Visual Dynamics</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04112](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04112) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Silin Gao, Hao Zhao, Zeming Chen, Sepideh Mamooler, Antara Raaghavi Bhattacharya, Qiyu Wu, Hiromi Wakaki, Yuki Mitsufuji, Li Mi, Syrielle Montariol, Antoine Bosselut
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.CV
- **Tags**: World Model, Video Understanding, Multimodal Learning
- **Summary**: 针对多模态LLM难以系统建模视频或图像序列的时间演化问题，提出动态模式引导的世界模型DynaVieW，学习交错状态-转换序列，采用混合专家架构和跨专家选择性注意力。在视觉叙事生成和世界模拟任务上提升一致性、可控性和指令跟随能力。

<h3 id="311">[311] Parametric Memory Decoding for Zero-Shot Routing in LoRA-Based External Parametric Memory</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04118](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04118) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Fengxian Ji, Zhuohan Xie, Jingpu Yang, Fan Zhang, Zirui Song, Xiuying Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning, LoRA, Zero-Shot Learning
- **Summary**: 针对基于LoRA的外部参数化内存（EPM）的路由问题，提出参数化内存解码（PMD）框架，将零样本LoRA路由重构为解码外部参数化内存上的激活。实例化PMDRouter，通过单次预填充的响应幅度评分每个LoRA，在PMD-Bench上实现最强内信号性能。

<h3 id="312">[312] SOV-CAD: Stepwise Orthographic Views Guided CAD Modeling Sequence Reconstruction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04119](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04119) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhaopeng Feng, Chen Zhi, Xuhong Zhang, Zhengwen Feng, Xinkui Zhao
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: CAD Generation, Reinforcement Learning, 3D Reconstruction
- **Venue**: ICME 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/LukePhong/SOV-CAD)
- **Summary**: 针对从图像重建CAD建模序列时忽略迭代反馈的问题，提出SOV-CAD框架，利用逐步正交投影视觉反馈，采用离线强化学习与Decision Transformer架构。在CAD序列重建上超越现有方法，具有强数据效率。

<h3 id="313">[313] Conflict-Based Lazy Search for Fast Multi-Manipulator Planning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04124](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04124) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dongliang Zheng, Zhipeng Wang, Siqi Wang, Yuxi Lu, Bin He, Hesheng Wang, Panagiotis Tsiotras
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Robotics, Motion Planning, Path Planning
- **Summary**: 针对多机械臂实时规划挑战，提出Conflict-Based Lazy Search (CBLS)算法，基于CBS框架，结合预计算和延迟搜索。提出LEA*用于高效单臂路径规划，减少边评估次数。实验显示比CBS和RRT-Connect性能优越。

<h3 id="314">[314] CSB: A Counting and Sampling tool for Bit-vectors</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04142](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04142) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Arijit Shaw, Kuldeep S. Meel
- **Subjects**: cs.LO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: SAT Solving, Model Counting
- **Venue**: Acta Informatica
- **Summary**: 针对位向量理论的模型计数和采样问题，开发csb工具，通过位爆破将位向量公式转为CNF，利用CNF计数器和采样器。实验显示性能优于现有方法。

<h3 id="315">[315] !Imperio, smolVLA: The Implications of Data Poisoning on Open Source Robotics</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04146](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04146) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Stefan Bühler, Mark Schutera
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.CR; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Robotics, Data Poisoning, Backdoor Attack
- **Venue**: KI2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/StefanBuhler/ImperioVLAPoisoning)
- **Summary**: 针对开放源码机器人生态系统中的信任假设，证明对视觉语言动作模型进行触发器词语数据投毒是可行的。在LeRobot平台上的真实拾取放置任务中，少量中毒样本即可嵌入后门导致机器人失效。3个中毒轨迹在320个干净轨迹中就足以完全拒绝服务。

<h3 id="316">[316] HCSU: A Dataset and Benchmark for Fine-Grained Historical Calligraphy Style Understanding</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04147](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04147) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yinsheng Yao, Yan Liu, Chen Ye
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Cultural Heritage, Fine-Grained Recognition
- **Venue**: ECCV 2026
- **Summary**: 针对大型视觉语言模型在书法风格细粒度感知上的挑战，构建HCSU数据集，包含39,307幅书法字图像，来自49位历史书法家，10个朝代，解耦墨迹和碑刻模态。提供分层专家美学描述，评估显示LVLMs在书法知识和视觉风格感知间存在差距，难以将审美判断基于笔触证据。

<h3 id="317">[317] Mask-based Predictive Representations for Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04153](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04153) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kai Zhao
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Self-Supervised Learning, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 针对视觉深度强化学习中从高维图像输入和有限样本中提取有效状态的问题，提出基于掩码预测的自监督辅助任务。利用transformer在潜在空间中重构掩码输入序列，结合强化学习模型提高样本效率，在多个连续和离散控制基准上超越SOTA。

<h3 id="318">[318] Information-Geometric Superposed Vowel Evaluation: Part 1. Moraic Syllabary (Japanese)</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04154](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04154) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yusei Tamura, Shigekazu Ishihara, Ken Ito
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Speech Synthesis, Audio Generation, Deepfake Detection
- **Summary**: 针对区分生成式AI合成语音和自然语音的问题，提出基于信息几何的方法，利用Wasserstein距离和持久同调分析日语元音谱分布。合成语音的元音间Wasserstein距离较短，可通过拓扑映射聚类区分。

<h3 id="319">[319] SeeMe: Mitigating Hallucinations in Large Vision-Language Models through Effective Visual Token Engineering</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04163](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04163) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kai Tang, Jinhao You, Bohua Zhang, Yichen Guo, Yiding Sun, Dongxu Zhang, Chenxi Li, Xiande Huang, Shanghang Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, LLM Hallucination, Visual Token Engineering
- **Summary**: 针对LVLMs中由无关或噪声视觉token导致的幻觉问题，提出SeeMe训练无关框架，将特征工程引入LVLMs，通过三阶段token工程抑制幻觉源。在多个基准上减少幻觉，提高输出一致性。

<h3 id="320">[320] Piercing Gilbreath's Conjecture: From Deep Number Theory Insights to Fintech and Cybersecurity</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04166](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04166) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Vincent Granville
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Number Theory, CyberSecurity, Fraud Detection
- **Summary**: 提出攻击Gilbreath猜想的新方法，基于筛法和反向筛法，引入新概念如魔术素数、禁止素数星座等，并展示了在随机性测试、模式检测、欺诈检测、网络安全、合成数据等领域的应用。

<h3 id="321">[321] CritiqueDriveVLM: From Verifier-Guided Reinforcement Learning to Latent Thought Distillation for Autonomous Driving</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04179](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04179) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhaohong Liu, Hao Ye, Xianlin Zhang, Mengshi Qi
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Autonomous Driving, LLM Reasoning, Reinforcement Learning
- **Venue**: ECCV 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/MICLAB-BUPT/CritiqueDriveVLM)
- **Summary**: 针对端到端视觉语言模型在自动驾驶中因标准微调导致的推理幻觉和保守偏差，以及工具增强或思维链方法的高延迟问题，提出CritiqueDriveVLM三阶段框架：先通过多维度验证器引导的多轮强化学习培养稳健的系统2推理教师，再通过潜在思想蒸馏将推理能力压缩到低延迟的系统1学生中。在DriveLMM-01基准上，教师模型将多项选择题准确率从55.54%提升至76.54%，学生模型将推理延迟从3482ms降至416ms（降低88%），平均仅生成28个token。

<h3 id="322">[322] Detecting Hallucinations in Retrieval-Augmented Generation through Grounding-Aware Sensitivity by Perturbation (GASP)</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04223](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04223) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mohamed Aly Bouke
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Hallucination, RAG, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 针对现有检索增强生成（RAG）幻觉检测器仅返回整体得分而无法定位问题句子的不足，提出基于扰动的接地感知灵敏度（GASP）检测器，通过计算每个答案句子在移除不同上下文后的对数似然变化和JS散度来评估接地灵敏度。在RAGTruth基准上，响应级AUC约0.73，句子级AUC约0.67，显著优于困惑度、自然语言推理和自一致性基线。

<h3 id="323">[323] SoftVTBench: A Safety-Aware Visuo-Tactile Benchmark for Physically Constrained Robotic Manipulation of Deformable Objects</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04234](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04234) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Bowen Jing, Mingxin Wang, Ruiyang Hao, Chenchen Ge, Hanwen Shen, Junjie He, Yang Cui, Yiming Hou, Weitao Zhou, Jiawei Wang, Minglei Li, Dandan Zhang, Ding Zhao, Houde Liu, Xiaofan Li, Si Liu, Ping Luo, Haibao Yu
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Robot Manipulation, Benchmark, Tactile Sensing
- **Summary**: 现有操控基准只评估任务成功而忽视物理安全性，为此提出SoftVTBench，一个面向可变形物体操控的安全感知视觉触觉基准，在Isaac Sim中通过有限元模拟可变形物体，分别报告目标成功率和安全性成功率。实验表明，仅评估成功率会高估性能，而触觉传感将物体中心可变形任务的安全性成功率从21.4%提升至35.6%。

<h3 id="324">[324] Hierarchical Multi-to-Single-Modal Knowledge Distillation for Disruption Prediction in EAST</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04241](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04241) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Qiang Chen, Xiao Wang, Hao Si, Qingquan Yang, Meiwen Chen, Jianhua Yang, Xiaofeng Han, Yunhu Jia, Ran Chen, Liang Wang, Jin Tang, Guosheng Xu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, Multimodal Learning, Time Series Forecasting
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Event-AHU/OpenFusion)
- **Summary**: 等离子体破裂预测中，可见光图像可提供互补空间信息，但增加推理代价。提出层次化多模态到单模态知识蒸馏框架，训练时利用图像和时间序列信号的多模态教师，推理时仅保留时间序列学生，通过图结构级、表示级和决策级蒸馏迁移知识。在EAST数据集上，该方法保留了多模态学习的判别优势并降低了推理成本。

<h3 id="325">[325] Signal or Noise? Understanding Generative Models for Real-World Sensor Time Series</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04245](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04245) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zitao Shuai, Zongzhe Xu, Yuntian Wu, Sirui Li, Tianhong Li, Yuzhe Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Time Series Forecasting, Generative Model, Data Synthesis
- **Summary**: 针对传感器时序数据生成建模缺乏系统性理解的问题，提出SensorGen大规模研究，覆盖14个场景、7个数据集和12个信号模态，系统评估5类生成模型。主要发现：流匹配模型总体表现最优；信号属性（如人口协变量、时频建模）影响生成质量；合成数据可提升下游任务性能。

<h3 id="326">[326] HALO-WA: Hybrid-Attention Latent-Guided Online Reinforcement Learning for World-Action Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04265](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04265) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Angen Ye, Weijie Ke, Xiaofeng Wang, Xinze Chen, Chaojun Ni, Guosheng Zhao, Boyuan Wang, Zheng Zhu, Junjie Xie, Dapeng Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Robot Manipulation, Reinforcement Learning, Online Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/YeanRoot/HALO-WA)
- **Summary**: 世界动作模型在精密操控任务中对校准、感知等误差敏感，提出HALO-WA框架，利用混合注意力结构从WA模型潜变量中读取任务相关信息，并通过轻量级演员-评论家适配器进行在线强化学习微调。在四个真实精密操控任务上，将平均成功率从26.4%提升至87.1%，在线训练仅需45–75分钟。

<h3 id="327">[327] LBR: Towards Mitigating Length Bias in Large Language Models for Recommendation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04270](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04270) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hongchen Li, Bohao Wang, Jingbang Chen, Weiqin Yang, Hang Pan, Bingde Hu, Can Wang, Jiawei Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Recommender System, Bias Mitigation, LLM Application
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Void-JackLee/LBR)
- **Summary**: 发现基于LLM的推荐系统中存在长度偏差问题：输入侧长文本获得过多注意力，输出侧解码不利于长物品。提出LBR框架，包括长度感知注意力校准和有效信息长度归一化。在三个Amazon数据集上，平均NDCG@5提升16.82%，有效缓解偏差并提升准确性和公平性。

<h3 id="328">[328] Self-Reference in Large Language Models: The Introspection Threshold for Recursive Self-Improvement</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04277](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04277) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiang Zhang, Bing Yuan, Qian Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, AI Safety, Self-Improvement
- **Summary**: 探讨LLM自主递归自我改进的可持续性条件，类比冯·诺依曼复杂度阈值，提出内省（introspection）是必要功能。基于Kleene第二递归定理证明内省程序的理论存在性，但实证表明当前LLM仅具备准内省能力，受限于缺乏完整自访问、Transformer前馈结构和计算类别约束。指出了跨越该阈值的架构路径。

<h3 id="329">[329] Risk-Constrained Freshness-Aware Semantic Caching for Open-Web Retrieval-Augmented LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04281](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04281) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Muhammad Mansoor, Tahir Ahmad, Yeo-Chan Yoon
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: RAG, Cache Policy, Information Retrieval
- **Summary**: 现有语义缓存忽略开放网络证据的时效性，提出FreshCache三层缓存系统，将缓存复用视为风险约束的时序推理问题，利用指数衰减模型和MLP估计缓存过时概率，仅在概率低于预设计误差预算时复用。在24小时窗口内，基于MLP的方法实现97%的搜索API节省，过时错误率仅0.1%；基于规则的方法实现98%节省，过时错误率3.3%。

<h3 id="330">[330] Agentic-V2X: Small Language Model Agents for Deadline-Aware V2X Scheduling in 5G/6G Networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04290](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04290) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Gerasimos Papanikolaou-Ntais, Alexandros Kaloxylos, Athanasios Kanavos
- **Subjects**: cs.NI; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Wireless Networks, Scheduling
- **Summary**: 针对大语言模型因延迟和幻觉不适合近实时V2X调度的问题，提出Agentic-V2X架构：本地小语言模型作为周期性的非实时策略创建者，轻量级控制器执行验证后的策略。在5G NR V2X调度仿真中，自适应LLM策略在多个工作点具有竞争力，但总体非最优，验证了该架构的安全性而非通用优势。

<h3 id="331">[331] CausalGame: Benchmarking Causal Thinking of LLM Agents in Games</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04293](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04293) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhenhao Chen, Yongqiang Chen, Chenxi Liu, Junchi Yu, Xiangchen Song, Zijian Li, Jialin Li, Philip Torr, Bo Han, Kun Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG; stat.ML
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Causal Inference, Benchmark
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 现有AI科学家基准缺乏对选择偏差、测量误差和隐藏混杂因素的评估，提出CausalGame基准，通过互动游戏评估LLM代理的因果思维能力。包含14个场景，对30个LLM代理测试，最佳模型仅达到68%存活率（分析最优为78-85%），仅5-7%的会话获得因果推理评分。

<h3 id="332">[332] HiFA4: Training-Free 4-bit FlashAttention on Ascend HIF4 NPUs for LLM Inference</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04302](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04302) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hui Dong, Yanzhao Li, Jie Gao, Chunlu Li, Zhiyuan Zhang, Yupeng Sun, Zhenyuan Chen, Zhiqiang Zou
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.AR; cs.CL; cs.PF
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, Post-Training Quantization, Model Compression
- **Summary**: 针对LLM推理中FlashAttention量化问题，提出HiFA4，在Ascend NPU上实现4位HIF4 Cube GEMM执行QK^T和PV，同时在线softmax状态保持FP16。通过Smooth-QK和P-Reordering机制，在Qwen3-8B上恢复37.5%的精度差距，MMLU回归预测减少57%，估计延迟降低35.4%。

<h3 id="333">[333] Fixed-Confidence Best-Arm Identification for Causal Mediation Analysis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04315](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04315) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Harsh Shrivastava, Yuta Kawakami, Junpei Komiyama, Jin Tian
- **Subjects**: stat.ML; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Causal Inference, Multi-Armed Bandit, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 针对因果中介分析中识别最大化期望自然直接潜在结果的处理方法问题，在因果赌博机设定下建立NDPO的识别条件，并基于Track-and-Stop框架提出固定置信度最优臂识别算法，采用割平面法求解半无限优化问题。理论证明算法满足δ正确性和渐近最优性，在IPinYou广告数据集上验证有效性。

<h3 id="334">[334] One Framework for All: Cross-Modal Membership Inference for Generative Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04339](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04339) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dayong Ye, Tainqing Zhu, Kun Gao, Junhao Liu, Yichuan Chen, Shuai Zhou, Hengzhu Liu, Bo Liu, Wanlei Zhou
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CR
- **Tags**: Membership Inference, Generative Model, Privacy
- **Summary**: 现有生成模型成员推断攻击（MIA）方法因模态而异且不可互用，提出首个跨文本-文本、文本-图像、图像-文本模态的统一成员推断框架，利用生成模型输出分布近似训练数据分布的特性，在共享嵌入空间中对生成输出和辅助非成员样本进行似然比检验。在严格黑盒设置下，优于针对单类模型优化的现有方法。

<h3 id="335">[335] IRIS: An Intelligent Vision-Language System for Ocular Surface Diseases via Topic Tree and Scene-Driven VQA Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04344](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04344) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hao Wei, Wenjin Qi, Dasen Dai, Minqing Zhang, Wu Yuan
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Medical AI, Data Synthesis
- **Summary**: 针对眼前表面疾病（OSD）领域大视觉语言模型临床推理受限于高质量多模态指令微调数据的问题，提出IRIS系统，构建OSD视觉问答数据集IRIS-120K，通过主题发现树和场景驱动对话生成注入临床先验，微调4B参数VLM。在多个基准上超越高达34B参数的通用和专用医学VLM，表明结构化知识注入比参数规模更有效。

<h3 id="336">[336] HASSL: Hierarchy-Aware Self-Supervised Learning Framework for Single Cell Microscopy</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04353](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04353) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Julius Riel, Vishwa Mohan Singh, Sai Anirudh Aryasomayajula, Anuun Chinbat, Hannes Leonhard, Moritz Ladenburger, Frederik Alexander, Vishisht Choudhary, Fabio Laredo, Giacomo Masserdotti, Thorben Prein, Carsten Marr, Amirhossein Kardoost
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Self-Supervised Learning, Medical Imaging, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 针对自监督学习模型在单细胞显微图像中抑制层次结构的问题，提出层次感知自监督学习框架，结合分割教师蒸馏和基于HDBSCAN的层次感知对比损失。在208个细胞类别的230万单细胞数据集上，平均top-K准确率提升2.8%，最深层次检索top-9提升6.3%，药物分类F1提升7.8%。

<h3 id="337">[337] Auto-AEG: Scalable Data Construction for Open-Vocabulary Audio Event Grounding</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04383](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04383) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zihan Zhang, Xize Cheng, Wenhao Yan, Tong Zhang, Dongjie Fu, Boyun Zhang, Yongbo He, Tao Jin
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Audio Event Grounding, Data Synthesis, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 针对开放词汇音频事件定位中时间监督数据稀缺的问题，提出Auto-AEG自动化数据构建流水线，通过程序化合成与多模型伪标签结合强化学习进行训练。在DESED和AEGBench上取得性能提升，验证了自动构造数据与区间感知奖励函数设计的有效性。

<h3 id="338">[338] Full-Stack FP4: Stable LLM Pretraining with Quantized Projections, Optimizers, and Attention</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04422](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04422) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Siyu Ding, Mingchuan Ma, Jiabo Tong, Xingrun Xing, Ziming Wang, Guoqi Li
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Quantization, Pre-training, Mixed Precision Training
- **Summary**: 全栈4比特预训练面临线性层、优化器和注意力的数值不稳定问题。提出Full-Stack FP4框架，采用LoRA-SVD、二阶矩变换和混合精度等模块级策略，在3B/64B规模下损失差距仅1.47%，实现近BF16性能的稳定训练。

<h3 id="339">[339] Transferability Between Understanding and Generation in Unified Multimodal Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04423](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04423) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiwon Kang, Heeji Yoon, Jaewoo Jung, Jaewon Min, Minkyeong Jeon, Biyeon Hwang, Sangwon Jung, Seungryong Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Vision-Language Model, Transfer Learning
- **Venue**: ECCV 2026
- **Summary**: 探究统一多模态模型中理解与生成任务的迁移性。通过实验发现迁移性依赖于架构，并利用跨任务迁移策略：训练理解任务以提升生成性能，避免直接微调带来的分布偏移。在计数、空间关系等能力上验证有效性。

<h3 id="340">[340] UI-MOPD: Multi-Platform On-Policy Distillation for Continual GUI Agent Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04425](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04425) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Niu Lian, Alan Chen, Zhehao Yu, Chengzhen Duan, Fazhan Liu, Hui Liu, Pei Fu, Jian Luan, Yaowei Wang, Shu-Tao Xia, Jinpeng Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CV; cs.LG; cs.MM
- **Tags**: GUI Automation, Continual Learning, Knowledge Distillation
- **Summary**: 针对跨平台GUI代理面临的多平台轨迹稀缺与灾难性遗忘问题，构建Uni-GUI数据集并提出UI-MOPD方法，利用多教师在线策略蒸馏与平台条件约束实现持续学习。在OSWorld和MobileWorld上分别达到38.2%和12.0%的成功率。

<h3 id="341">[341] dOPSD: On-Policy Self-Distillation for Diffusion Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04428](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04428) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Phuong Tuan Dat, Qi Li, Xinchao Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Diffusion Language Model, Knowledge Distillation, LLM Reasoning
- **Summary**: 扩散语言模型后训练面临离线策略暴露偏差和稀疏奖励问题。提出dOPSD在线策略自蒸馏，教师先验来自学生自身去噪轨迹的后续步骤，在数学推理和代码生成上优于监督和在线基线。

<h3 id="342">[342] evalci: A Python Library for Statistically Rigorous Comparison of Language Model Evaluations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04429](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04429) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shreyas K Chandrahas
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Benchmark, Statistical Testing
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Shreyaskc/evalci)
- **Summary**: 语言模型评估中普遍缺乏统计显著性检验。开发evalci库，基于逐项结果表一键生成置信区间、p值等统计量。对9个模型MMLU结果重新分析，发现8个相邻排名间隙中3个不显著。

<h3 id="343">[343] On Pairwise Quantile Regression -- Statistical Guarantees and Applications</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04431](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04431) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Romain Thérézien, Stephan Clémençon, Fantin Girard, Hamza El-Abdouni
- **Subjects**: stat.ML; cs.AI; cs.CV; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Quantile Regression, Learning Theory, Face Recognition
- **Venue**: UAI 2026
- **Summary**: 将分位数回归扩展到成对情形，用于解释相似性函数。基于U过程给出泛化界和快速学习率理论保证，并在模拟数据和面部识别相似性评分上验证。

<h3 id="344">[344] Covert Trait Propagation Is Representation Alignment: Mechanistic Evidence from Hidden-Channel Distillation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04432](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04432) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kargi Chauhan, Aditya Shah
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, Mechanistic Interpretability, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 揭示隐通道蒸馏中教师能力传递的机制：共享初始化导致输出投影作为共同键，KL梯度调整学生隐藏表示与教师对齐。线性CKA与精度相关系数0.98，证明几何对齐是信息访问的门控。

<h3 id="345">[345] RoboDojo: A Unified Sim-and-Real Benchmark for Comprehensive Evaluation of Generalist Robot Manipulation Policies</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04434](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04434) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tianxing Chen, Yue Chen, Zixuan Li, Junyuan Tang, Kailun Su, Weijie Wan, Baijun Chen, Haoran Lu, Haowen Yan, Honghao Su, Zhiyang Dou, Kaixuan Wang, Dandan Zhang, Yunze Liu, Yan Qin, Qiwei Liang, Qiwei Wu, Zijian Lin, Wenwei Lin, Yuran Wang, Minghua He, Tianshu Wu, Ruihai Wu, Jingquan Zhou, Kai-Chong Lei, Haibao Yu, Yuanfeng Ji, Weiyang Jin, Guanyu Lin, Xiaofan Li, Qi Xiong, Renjing Xu, Zhongyu Li, Wenhao Chai, Enze Xie, Ziwei Wang, Yao Mu, Hao Dong, Wojciech Matusik, Mingyu Ding, Wenbo Ding, Ping Luo, Masayoshi Tomizuka
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.CV; cs.GR
- **Tags**: Robot Manipulation, Benchmark, Sim-to-Real
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/RoboDojo-Benchmark/RoboDojo)
- **Summary**: 现有机器人操作基准局限于简单任务或单一环境。提出RoboDojo统一仿真与实物基准，包含42个仿真和18个实物任务，覆盖泛化、记忆、精度等维度，并集成30种策略建立公开排行榜。

<h3 id="346">[346] A Retrieval-Augmented Framework for Detecting and Resolving Pragmatic Ambiguities in Natural Language Requirements</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04436](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04436) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Pavithra PM Nair, Preethu Rose Anish
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Requirement Engineering, RAG, Natural Language Understanding
- **Summary**: 自然语言需求中的语用歧义因利益相关者背景差异而产生。提出基于RAG的检测与消歧框架，利用不同领域知识库模拟专家，GPT-4o-mini在检测中宏F2达0.75，生成的需求清晰一致。

<h3 id="347">[347] ResearchStudio-Reel: Automate the Last Mile of Research from Paper to Poster, Video, and Blog</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04438](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04438) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Lingao Xiao, Yalun Dai, Yangyu Huang, Qihao Zhao, Wenshan Wu, Hugo He, Ruishuo Chen, Jin Jiang, Qianli Ma, Jiahuan Zhang, Xin Zhang, Ying Xin, Yang Ou, Yan Xia, Scarlett Li, Longbo Huang, Zhipeng Zhang, Yang He, Yap Kim Hui, Yan Lu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.HC; cs.MA; cs.MM
- **Tags**: Research Dissemination, AI Agent, Automation
- **Summary**: 研究成果传播（生成海报、视频、博客）仍依赖手工。提出ResearchStudio-Reel，通过共享提取器和确定性原语的技能组合自动化生成，审美和标题超越基线，是唯一支持所有三种可编辑工件的流水线。

<h3 id="348">[348] Generative wave propagator</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04440](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04440) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shijun Cheng, Tariq Alkhalifah
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Scientific Computing, Diffusion Model, Surrogate Model
- **Summary**: 传统地震波场有限差分法受频散和稳定性限制。提出基于条件扩散的波传播器，结合因果时间加权损失稳定递归推演，在Overthrust等模型上准确再现波场，端到端加速2.17倍。

<h3 id="349">[349] Wan-Streamer v0.2: Higher Resolution, Same Latency</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04443](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04443) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Lianghua Huang, Zhi-Fan Wu, Yupeng Shi, Wei Wang, Mengyang Feng, Junjie He, Chen-Wei Xie, Yu Liu, Jingren Zhou, Ang Wang, Bang Zhang, Baole Ai, Chen Liang, Cheng Yu, Chongyang Zhong, Jinwei Qi, Kai Zhu, Pandeng Li, Peng Zhang, Wenyuan Zhang, Xinhua Cheng, Yitong Huang, Yun Zheng, Yuxiang Bao, Yuzheng Wang, Zoubin Bi
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.GR; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Real-Time Systems, Multimodal Learning, Video Generation, Distributed Training
- **Summary**: 实时音视频交互模型Wan-Streamer v0.2在不增加延迟的情况下将输出分辨率从192x336提升至640x368。采用单GPU思考器与多GPU表演者的拆分架构，利用Ulysses上下文并行，总交互延迟约550ms。

<h3 id="350">[350] From Regulation to Requirements: An Automated Requirement Derivation and Explanation Pipeline</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04448](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04448) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Pavithra PM Nair, Preethu Rose Anish
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Requirement Engineering, Legal AI, Automation
- **Summary**: 法规条文（如GDPR、AI法案）到软件需求的转化仍依赖人工。提出自动化流水线，识别需求条款并推导可追溯的需求与解释，在GDPR和AI法案上F1达0.82/0.78，工具Reg2Req获得用户高度评价。

<h3 id="351">[351] A Deep Learning-based surrogate model for Severe Accidents in nuclear reactors using ASTEC</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04450](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04450) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Alessandro Longhi, Danny Lathouwers, Zoltán Perkó
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Surrogate Model, Neural ODE, Nuclear Engineering
- **Summary**: 核反应堆严重事故模拟器ASTEC耗时过长，不适用于实时训练。构建基于自编码器和神经常微分方程的代理模型，预测约80个物理变量，稳定步进5万步，40小时模拟在1分钟内完成。

<h3 id="352">[352] Robustness Verification of an Autonomous Underwater Vehicle-based Plankton Classifier</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04453](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04453) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Abdelrahman Sayed Sayed, Pierre-Jean Meyer, Asgeir J. Sørensen, Mohamed Ghazel
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; eess.SY; math.DS
- **Tags**: Neural Network Verification, Neural ODE, Autonomous Underwater Vehicle
- **Venue**: IEEE OES AUV Symposium 2026
- **Summary**: 自主水下航行器上的浮游生物分类器在动态环境中鲁棒性不足。提出基于可达性分析的鲁棒性验证框架，结合神经常微分方程分类模型，提供对环境扰动的形式化保证，减少人工验证工作量。

<h3 id="353">[353] Operator-on-F complements value-equivalence: a planning-time diagnostic for latent world models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04464](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04464) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Donna Vakalis
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Model Evaluation, World Model
- **Venue**: RLC 2026 World Model Workshop
- **Summary**: 针对基于模型的强化学习中世界模型评估仅关注奖励和价值预测而忽略潜在规划相关误差的问题，论文提出了Operator-on-F诊断方法，通过比较模型在可观测子集F上的k步潜在推演与环境真实值来评估模型。实验表明，该诊断与回报损失的相关性高达-0.90，并能区分不同架构，补充了值等价性评估。

<h3 id="354">[354] Regime-Conditional Stabilisation of LLM-Augmented Cooperative Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04470](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04470) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Faid Keddouri, Sohaib Houhou, Aissa Boulmerka, Nadir Farhi
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; math.OC
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Multi-Agent System, Reward Shaping
- **Summary**: 该论文揭示了在LLM增强的协作多智能体强化学习中，动态更新LLM生成的奖励权重会违反奖励塑造的平稳性假设并污染经验回放，提出了阶段冻结调度和指数移动平均两种稳定化策略。实验根据基线性能将场景分为三类，稳定化策略在各类场景下均有效，而不稳定策略可能导致性能崩溃。

<h3 id="355">[355] PulmoSight-XAI: An Explainable Multi-View Attention Ensemble with Gradient Boosting Meta-Learning for Multi-Label Chest X-Ray Classification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04478](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04478) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Moshiur Rahman, Shafqat Alam, Tasnia Binte Mamun
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Medical Imaging, Explainable AI, Multi-Label Learning
- **Summary**: 针对胸部X光分类中的类别不平衡、病理共现和局部特征丢失问题，论文提出了PulmoSight-XAI可解释多层次多视角集成框架。该框架采用五个互补CNN集成、CBAM多尺度特征融合、混合损失函数以及基于梯度提升的元学习策略，在CheXpert数据集上取得了最优AUROC（正面0.9319，侧面0.9154），并通过多种可解释性分析验证了临床一致性。

<h3 id="356">[356] Two Black Boxes, One Solver: Encoder Probing and Decoder Attribution for Neural Multi-Attribute VRP under Hard-Mask and Recourse Decoders</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04487](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04487) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sohaib Afifi
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; math.OC
- **Tags**: Neural Combinatorial Optimization, Explainable AI, Attribution
- **Venue**: IJCAI-ECAI 2026 Workshop on Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI)
- **Summary**: 针对神经自回归求解器在多变体车辆路径问题中缺乏步骤级解释的问题，论文提出了同时打开编码器和解码器两个黑箱的方法。通过对编码器进行线性探针分析以及对解码器应用多种归因方法，揭示了不同架构对约束表示和可行性判断的影响，其中图归纳偏置和Recourse训练机制提升了可解释性。

<h3 id="357">[357] Transplanting, inverting, and preventing a misalignment persona: method-conditional emergent misalignment in Qwen2.5</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04510](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04510) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Lyndon Drake, Zandi Eberstadt
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CY; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, LLM Safety, Fine-Tuning
- **Summary**: 该论文研究了语言模型在微调有害数据后产生的新兴错误对齐现象，发现Qwen2.5模型中存在一个潜在的人格方向，该方向具有因果性并且可以通过移植、消融等方法进行操作。实验表明，低秩适应（LoRA）比全微调更容易激活该人格，而移除该方向并非通用预防方法，并提出了基于损失相关性的筛选和预防策略。

<h3 id="358">[358] Failures and Successes to Learn a Core Conceptual Distinction from the Statistics of Language</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04523](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04523) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhimin Hu, Jeroen van Paridon, Gary Lupyan
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Cognitive Science, Natural Language Understanding, LLM Evaluation
- **Venue**: Evolang XV
- **Summary**: 该论文探究语言模型能否从语言统计中学习原则性与统计性的核心概念区分。实验发现，早期的语言模型只能捕捉统计频率，而难以区分原则性陈述与统计性陈述，直到GPT-4才成功掌握这一区分，暗示语言经验可能引导核心概念的建立。

<h3 id="359">[359] Language Models Represent and Transform Concepts with Shared Geometry</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04525](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04525) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhimin Hu, Lanhao Niu, Sashank Varma
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Representation Learning, Interpretability, Geometric Deep Learning
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了一种将概念表示视为点云流形、上下文转换视为向量场的框架，并在多种语言模型家族中实验发现：上下文对不同概念的移动不同，且这种位移结构在不同模型间共享。这表明语言模型不仅在概念表示上具有共同几何，在上下文变换方式上也存在共享结构。

<h3 id="360">[360] Training-Free Model Selection and Domain-Aware Score Calibration for First-Shot Anomalous Sound Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04526](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04526) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Grach Mkrtchian
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Anomaly Detection, Audio Processing, Domain Adaptation
- **Summary**: 针对首次异常声音检测中源域和目标域AUC负相关且开发集性能无法预测测试集性能的问题，论文提出了免训练的后处理层，包括按域分位数校准和无标签交叉验证的域平衡准则。在DCASE 2025上，该准则与官方评估分数秩相关达0.91，显著提升性能，但在前两年数据上表现受限。

<h3 id="361">[361] Lyapunov-Guided Training for Hardware-Safe Neural Networks Under Fixed-Point Arithmetic</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04531](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04531) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Anis Hamadouche, Amir Hussain
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; eess.SP; math.NA
- **Tags**: Model Compression, Quantization, Hardware Acceleration
- **Summary**: 针对低精度神经网络在定点算术中因补码溢出导致数值不稳定的问题，论文提出了基于Lyapunov稳定性理论的量化框架，通过层状Lyapunov函数监控隐状态能量并施加单调投影约束。实验表明，该方法将激活溢出率从超过11%降至0.012%以下，在12位定点精度下恢复稳定训练并达到86.55%准确率。

<h3 id="362">[362] Obey, Diverge, Collapse: Blind Obedience to Incorrect Instructions Drives Code LLMs to Irrecoverable Code Semantic Collapse</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04537](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04537) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Raj Jaiswal, Anany Singh Divy, Savar Bhasin, Adi Bajpai, Tanuja Ganu, Rajiv Ratn Shah
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Code Generation, LLM Safety, Instruction Following
- **Summary**: 该论文研究了代码语言模型在面对错误指令时的行为，发现模型会正确识别指令错误但仍然盲目服从，导致引入额外错误且无法通过后续迭代修复恢复，称之为“盲目服从”现象。实验量化了语义损坏的不可恢复比例，并指出传统通过率评估无法检测此类问题。

<h3 id="363">[363] CRISP: A Spatiotemporal Camera-Radar Backbone for Driving via Forecasting-Based World-Model Pretraining</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04541](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04541) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jingyu Song, Yi Liu, Katherine A. Skinner
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.RO
- **Tags**: Autonomous Driving, BEV Perception, World Model
- **Summary**: 针对相机-雷达融合在自动驾驶中的表示学习问题，论文提出了CRISP框架，通过预测未来激光雷达点云进行预训练，学习统一的鸟瞰图表示。该方法在nuScenes上提升了长时点云预测能力，并有效迁移到多种下游任务，验证了基于预测的相机-雷达预训练的有效性。

<h3 id="364">[364] Auto: The AGI Compiler</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04542](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04542) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jaber Jaber, Osama Jaber
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.SE
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Program Synthesis, Cost Optimization
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/RightNow-AI/auto)
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了Auto系统，一个将LLM agent行为编译为确定性程序或专家的AGI编译器，通过记录、提取和验证将边际成本降低至1/30。在AUTO-BENCH基准上，87.1%的agent执行可被编译，端到端成本降低6.4倍，并揭示了校准和参考保真度是保持正确性的关键。

<h3 id="365">[365] Mask2Real-WM: Segmentation Masks as a Sim-to-Real Bridge for Controllable Dexterous World Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04546](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04546) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Riccardo O. Feingold, Davide Liconti, Chenyu Yang, Robert K. Katzschmann
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.CV; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Robot Manipulation, Sim-to-Real, World Model
- **Summary**: 针对灵巧操作中的世界模型预测问题，论文提出了Mask2Real-WM，将像素预测解耦为动力学模型（预测分割掩码）和渲染模型（生成RGB图像）。通过利用分割空间的较小仿真-真实差距，使用大规模仿真数据预训练，再在少量真实数据上微调，实现了对23个自由度动作的细粒度控制。

<h3 id="366">[366] Explainable Novel Category Discovery in Semantic Concept Space</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04548](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04548) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ifrat Ikhtear Uddin, Yang Zhou, KC Santosh, Longwei Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Novel Category Discovery, Explainable AI, Concept Bottleneck Model
- **Summary**: 针对新类别发现方法在隐式潜在空间中缺乏可解释性的问题，论文提出了xNCD框架，直接在语义概念空间中进行表示学习和伪标签分配。通过视觉-语言相似性先验构建概念瓶颈，每个发现的类别都能通过稳定概念签名得到内在解释，在保持高性能的同时提供了可读的聚类和实例级解释。

<h3 id="367">[367] Lights, Camera, Carbon: Architectural Scaling Laws for Video Generation Energy Consumption</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04553](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04553) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nidhal Jegham, Boris Gamazaychikov, Sasha Luccioni
- **Subjects**: cs.MM; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Text-to-Video, Energy Efficiency, Scaling Laws
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了一个双向框架，从架构原理和生成参数预测文本到视频/音频模型的能耗，并反向从推理时间恢复架构扩展行为。实验验证了六种开源模型在不同GPU配置下的能耗符合理论扩展律，MAPE低于3%，为视频生成模型的可持续性评估提供了标准化方法。

<h3 id="368">[368] Predicting Therapeutic Outcome via Aligning Patient-Specific Knowledge Graph and Gene-Level Perturbation Representations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04557](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04557) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dongmin Bang, Sugyun An, Inyoung Sung, Ilho Yun, Sun Kim, Sangseon Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; q-bio.QM
- **Tags**: Drug Discovery, Knowledge Graph, Representation Learning
- **Venue**: BIOKDD 2026
- **Summary**: 针对患者特异性治疗反应预测中缺乏标签和动态扰动信息的问题，论文提出了PREDIKTOR多视角框架，通过对齐个性化知识图谱和基因级扰动表示来预测药物反应。在TCGA和I-SPY2数据集上，该方法优于现有基线，AUROC提升5.6%，并提供可解释的基因和通路归因。

<h3 id="369">[369] EEG-SpikeAgent: Agentic Closed-Loop Program Synthesis for Automated EEG Spike Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04558](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04558) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sonali Santhosh, Kelly Shuhong Yu, Eugene Chang, Jonathan Kim, Kie Shidara, Danilo Bernardo
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Medical AI, Program Synthesis
- **Summary**: 针对脑电图棘波检测中深度学习模型可解释性不足的问题，提出基于LLM代理的闭环程序合成框架EEG-SpikeAgent，通过迭代生成信号处理特征并进行评估优化。在VEPISET数据集上达到AUC 0.935，平衡准确率0.699，F1分数0.557。

<h3 id="370">[370] A Few Teacher Steps Go a Long Way: Cost-Efficient On-Policy Data Augmentation for Agent Post-Training</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04574](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04574) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Junze Ye, Jiayi Cheng, Miao Lu, Michal Mankowski, Jose Blanchet, Mohsen Bayati
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Instruction Tuning, Data Augmentation
- **Venue**: ICML 2026 Workshop: RLxF: Reinforcement Learning from World Feedback
- **Summary**: 针对LLM代理监督微调中训练与测试上下文不匹配的问题，将在线数据构建形式化为预算分配问题，提出在学习者诱导上下文中使用少量无筛选教师延续。在HotpotQA等任务上，少步延续匹配或优于筛选方法，在预算有限时更高效。

<h3 id="371">[371] LLM-Driven CI-CD Workflow Intelligence for Cyber Systems Engineering</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04579](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04579) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Bonan Shen, Jiazhou Gao, Tao Ning, Wei-Jung Huang, Xin Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Application, Software Engineering, Workflow Automation
- **Summary**: 针对CI/CD工作流分析困难的问题，提出基于LLM的CI/CD分析流水线，结合仓库增强、反模式检测和推荐生成。在75,201个工作流中检测到434,769个问题，少样本提示生成最佳，平均每仓库8.25条建议。

<h3 id="372">[372] Simple-to-Complex Structured Demonstrations for Vision-Language-Action Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04591](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04591) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xinchuan Qiu, Yi Yu
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Robot Manipulation, Imitation Learning, Embodied AI
- **Summary**: 针对视觉-语言-动作模型模仿学习中演示组织被忽视的问题，提出从简单到复杂的结构化演示收集策略。在积木抓取和毛巾折叠任务上，一致提升了成功率和训练稳定性。

<h3 id="373">[373] TORINO: Token Reduction via Interpretable Concept Overlap in Vision-Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04593](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04593) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Riccardo Renzulli, Gabriele Spadaro, Shruthi Gowda, Alaa Eddine Mazouz, Van-Tam Nguyen
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Model Compression, Token Merging
- **Summary**: 针对视觉-语言模型视觉令牌过多导致计算成本高的问题，提出TORINO框架，利用稀疏自编码器将令牌投影到可解释潜在空间，基于概念重叠进行分组缩减。在多个基准上以最小性能损失减少了视觉令牌数量。

<h3 id="374">[374] LCPNet: Latent Consistent Proximal Unfolding Network for Infrared Small Target Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04603](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04603) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tianfang Zhang, Fengyi Wu, Lei Li, Chang Liu, Zhenming Peng, Huaping Zhang, Xiangyang Ji
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Object Detection, Remote Sensing, Infrared Small Target Detection
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Tianfang-Zhang/LCPNet)
- **Summary**: 针对红外小目标检测中深度学习缺乏物理分解约束的问题，提出LCPNet，在潜在空间进行潜一致近端展开，并引入共享优化记忆。在四个公开基准上优于现有方法，实现了准确且低虚警的检测。

<h3 id="375">[375] Do All Visual Tokens Matter Equally? Object-Evidence Preserving Token Merging for Vision-Language Retrieval</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04605](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04605) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Suhyeong Park, Junha Jung, Jungwoo Park, Jaewoo Kang
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Token Merging, Information Retrieval
- **Summary**: 针对多向量视觉-语言检索中令牌压缩可能丢失目标证据的问题，提出SaMer，利用目标标注作为合并先验，将令牌压缩为K个质心。在K=64时移除93%令牌，存储减少16倍，并在Flickr30K和MSCOCO上提升R@1。

<h3 id="376">[376] G2VD: Generalizable AI-Generated Video Detection via Counterfactual Intervention and Causal Disentanglement</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04607](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04607) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Meng Du, Hongchang Chen, Ran Li, Junjie Zhang, Qi Ouyang, Shuxin Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Deepfake Detection, Video Understanding, Causal Inference
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/dumeng98/G2VD)
- **Summary**: 针对AI生成视频检测跨域泛化差的问题，提出G2VD框架，通过反事实干预和因果解耦来鼓励模型关注内在伪造痕迹。在GenVidBench上准确率超90%，AUC近0.95，仅用10%训练数据。

<h3 id="377">[377] SILO: Simulation-in-the-Loop Sim-to-Real Transfer for Multi-Stage Cable Routing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04616](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04616) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Stone Tao, Jie Xu, Hesam Rabeti, Yashraj Narang, Yijie Guo, Iretiayo Akinola
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Robot Manipulation, Sim-to-Real, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 针对线性可变形物体（如电缆）操作难的问题，提出基于GPU并行模拟的sim-to-real强化学习框架SILO。在真实电缆布线任务上成功率高，周期时间减少2倍，首次实现多阶段电缆布线的sim-to-real迁移。

<h3 id="378">[378] Hierarchical Evidence-Driven Reasoning for Long Document Understanding</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04625](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04625) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Junyu Xiong, Yonghui Wang, Rongjian Gu, Chenyu Liu, Bing Yin, Wengang Zhou, Houqiang Li
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: RAG, Document Understanding, Question Answering
- **Summary**: 针对多模态RAG在长文档理解中检索干扰页和级联错误的问题，提出HIEVI-RAG层次化证据驱动框架，包括问题分解、粗检索、细粒度验证和记忆引导生成。在四个基准上平均准确率超过最强基线8.05%。

<h3 id="379">[379] Retroactive Chain-of-Thought (RetroCoT): Forensic Reconstruction Prompts as a Safety Diagnostic Across Model Generations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04645](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04645) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Samira Hajizadeh
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CR; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Safety, Jailbreak Attack, Chain-of-Thought
- **Summary**: 针对LLM安全对齐对语用寄存器敏感的问题，提出RetroCoT单轮攻击，将有害请求重构为取证重建任务。在gpt-4o上攻击成功率58%，而直接请求为0%；GPT-5家族虽拒绝，但通过对抗反馈可绕过。

<h3 id="380">[380] Machine Learning for Depression Screening and Intervention: an Original Circadian Rhythm Score-based Methodology</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04648](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04648) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Bin Wang, Shuo Lian, Yuanyuan Hou, Dexian Wang, Peilan He, Feng Hong, Yanwei Yu, Tianrui Li
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Mental Health, Healthcare Monitoring, Machine Learning
- **Summary**: 针对抑郁症筛查中行为指标碎片化和缺乏干预分析的问题，提出昼夜节律评分（CRS）并构建可解释筛查框架。在CHARLS数据集上ROC-AUC=0.825，识别出可干预的行为阈值，如运动剂量约300 MET-min/周。

<h3 id="381">[381] Targeted Structure Completion for Sparse-View 3D Reconstruction in Autonomous Driving</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04661](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04661) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Guoqing Wang, Pin Tang, Xiangxuan Ren, Liping Hou, Chao Ma
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: 3D Reconstruction, Autonomous Driving, Gaussian Splatting
- **Summary**: 针对自动驾驶稀疏视角3D重建中计算冗余的问题，提出FocusGS框架，将结构补全解耦到几何不确定区域。在驾驶基准上减少约74%的高斯数和约34%的渲染时间，实现了更优的效率-质量权衡。

<h3 id="382">[382] Elastic Gang: Per-Token Membership Change for a Hard-Barriered LLM Inference Gang Co-Scheduled with OS Processes</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04668](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04668) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Daeyeon Son
- **Subjects**: cs.OS; cs.AI; cs.PF
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, LLM Serving, Resource Management
- **Summary**: 针对设备端LLM解码与操作系统进程争用核心的问题，提出弹性线程组（Elastic Gang），允许令牌间改变核心成员。在AMD Zen 5上，推理输出位精确，中间占空比下通用吞吐量提升1.75倍等，核心归还延迟0.22微秒。

<h3 id="383">[383] Do Vision-Language-Action Models Mean What They Say? On the Role of Faithfulness in Embodied Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04681](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04681) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Matthew Foutter, Matteo Cercola, Lena Wild, Yunshan Wang, Michelle Li, Daniele Gammelli, Marco Pavone
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language-Action Model, Embodied AI, Faithfulness
- **Summary**: 针对具身推理中VLA模型思维链可能不忠实于决策过程的问题，引入忠诚度判别器Pinocchio并作为密集奖励进行后训练。在保留驾驶任务上忠诚度提升4%和18%，对罕见反事实场景响应提升1.6倍。

<h3 id="384">[384] ToolFailBench: Diagnosing Tool-Use Failures in LLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04686](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04686) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Harsh Soni
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.SE
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Tool Learning, Benchmark
- **Venue**: Workshop on Agents in the Wild: Safety, Security, and Beyond (AIWILD) and Workshop on Failure Modes of Agentic AI (FAGEN) at ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 针对LLM代理工具调用失败类型诊断不足的问题，提出ToolFailBench基准，涵盖1000个任务和5个领域，标注四种失败类型。最佳模型清洁工具使用率86.33%；参数规模相同的模型有不同失败模式。

<h3 id="385">[385] URSA: Chemistry-Aware Benchmark for Utilitarian Retrosynthesis Assessment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04688](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04688) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Bogdan Zagribelnyy, Ivan Ilin, Nikita Bondarev, Anton Morgunov, Arkadii Lin, Maksim Kuznetsov, Rim Shayakhmetov, Vladimir Aladinskiy, Alex Aliper, Alex Zhavoronkov
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CE; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Benchmark, Retrosynthesis
- **Summary**: 针对逆合成分析中缺乏灵活且化学可解释的基准协议的问题，本文提出了URSA评估框架，从形式合理性和化学合理性两个角度评估合成路线。结果表明，大型语言模型在高层次策略规划上表现良好，但在可靠解决合成规划任务上不如专用逆合成模型。

<h3 id="386">[386] Strategic Buying Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04708](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04708) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mingyang Fu, Ming Hu
- **Subjects**: econ.TH; cs.AI; cs.CY; cs.GT; cs.HC
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Decision Making, Dynamic Pricing
- **Summary**: 针对自主购买代理在有限购物窗口内决定购买时机的问题，本文在平稳、贝叶斯和鲁棒三种信息机制下制定了最优购买策略。在Amazon价格历史数据上的评估表明，平稳和贝叶斯策略在平均消费者剩余上表现良好，而鲁棒策略在分布第10百分位表现最佳。

<h3 id="387">[387] RSPO: Reward-Swap Policy Optimization for Multi-Turn LLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04713](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04713) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Qiang Liu, Taian Guo, Ruizhi Qiao, Xing Sun
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, Reinforcement Learning, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 针对多轮LLM代理任务中稀疏结果奖励导致收敛慢的问题，本文提出RSPO方法，通过奖励交换机制利用密集过程奖励促进训练，同时保证与真实结果奖励的一致性。在WebShop和ALFWorld基准上，RSPO在多种强化学习算法中实现了一致的性能提升。

<h3 id="388">[388] Geometry-Aware Motion Latents for Learning Robust Manipulation Policies</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04714](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04714) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yunchao Zhang, Yijia Weng, Ruizhe Liu, Ming Hu, Leonidas Guibas, Yanchao Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Robot Manipulation, 3D Vision, Point Cloud
- **Summary**: 针对机器人操作中动作抽象需要理解三维几何变换的问题，本文提出GeoMoLa，通过预测点云随时间演进来学习离散运动潜码，仅需单视图RGB-D输入。该方法在多种操作基准上达到最先进性能，且仅需少量演示即可实现鲁棒操作。

<h3 id="389">[389] Dashboard2Code: Evaluating Multimodal Models on Reconstructing Interactive Dashboards</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04727](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04727) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tianhao Niu, Ziyu Han, Qiguang Chen, Shiqi Zhou, Baocai Shan, Hengjie Fang, Qingfu Zhu, Wanxiang Che
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Multimodal Learning, Code Generation
- **Venue**: ACL 2026 Main Conference
- **Summary**: 针对现有自动数据可视化生成忽略交互式仪表板的问题，本文提出Dashboard2Code任务，要求模型主动探索仪表板并生成重现代码，并引入DashboardMimic基准（180个Plotly+Dash对）。实验表明，最强模型在高复杂度仪表板上仍困难，且开源模型与闭源模型存在显著差距。

<h3 id="390">[390] Turning Off-Policy Tokens On-Policy: A Plug-in Approach for Improving LLM Alignment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04728](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04728) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yu Li, Xiuyu Li, Mingyang Yi, Jiaxing Wang, zhangliangxu, Zhaolong Xing, Zhen Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, Reinforcement Learning, Off-Policy RL
- **Summary**: 针对LLM强化学习后训练中存在非策略数据的问题，本文提出选择性重要性采样（SIS），通过拒绝采样将非策略token转换为策略token，减少方差。SIS可即插即用，在数学和代理基准上持续改进所有目标，并提供了更强的鲁棒性。

<h3 id="391">[391] RustMizan: A Compilable, Contamination-Aware Benchmarking Framework for Rust Vulnerabilities</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04729](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04729) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tarek Elsayed, Shiping Yang, Eunsong Koh, Sanika Goyal, Vincent Huang, Paul Ngo, Nathan Young, Mohammad Omidvar Tehrani, Alvyn Kang, Arnell Kang, Zeyu Chen, Angélica Moreira, Xuan Feng, Angel X. Chang, Nick Sumner, Steven Y. Ko
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.SE
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Benchmark, Vulnerability Detection
- **Summary**: 针对现有漏洞基准缺乏可编译代码且未考虑数据污染的问题，本文提出RustMizan框架，包含可编译变体、注释和突变测试。在前沿模型上的评估显示，二元分类准确率56-65%，行级定位F1约20%，对抗性提示使行级F1下降约27%。

<h3 id="392">[392] Wasserstein Residuals: Learning Gradient Flows from Population Dynamics</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04738](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04738) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Markus Heinonen, Yair Shenfeld, Ricardo Baptista, Daniel Waxman, Dmitry Batenkov, Tim Cooijmans, Eli Bingham
- **Subjects**: stat.ML; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Optimal Transport, Scientific Computing, Particle Methods
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/BasisResearch/wasserstein-residuals)
- **Summary**: 针对通过JKO方案重建群体动力学计算昂贵的问题，本文提出残差方法，通过连续性方程的非负损失函数来学习Wasserstein梯度流，并基于粒子方法实现了无缝拼接技术。在轨迹推断基准上该方法取得了最先进性能。

<h3 id="393">[393] Trust Region Policy Distillation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04751](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04751) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhengpeng Xie, Li Lyna Zhang, Zeke Xie, Mao Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, LLM Reasoning, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 针对策略蒸馏（OPD）不稳定且方差大的问题，本文提出信任区域策略蒸馏（TOP-D），通过动态构建近端教师来稳定训练。理论上证明了梯度方差控制和单调改进保证，实验上在数学推理任务上显著提升了训练稳定性和最终性能。

<h3 id="394">[394] Multi-Turn On-Policy Distillation with Prefix Replay</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04763](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04763) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Baohao Liao, Hanze Dong, Christof Monz, Xinxing Xu, Li Dong, Furu Wei
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 针对多轮代理任务中在线策略蒸馏成本高的问题，本文提出ReOPD，利用预收集的教师轨迹作为重放前缀，避免环境交互。通过可靠性感知的采样调度，ReOPD在保持或提升准确率的同时，训练速度提升至少4倍。

<h3 id="395">[395] Predicting Drafted Deck Strength for "Magic: the Gathering"</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04782](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04782) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tomas Rigaux, Hisashi Kashima
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Game AI, Representation Learning
- **Venue**: IEEE Conference on Games (CoG) 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/akulen/MtGDraftEncoder)
- **Summary**: 针对万智牌轮抽中卡牌组合协同效应驱动的决策问题，本文提出基于编码器的模型，生成套牌上下文感知的卡牌嵌入以预测套牌强度。在大规模真实数据上，该模型一致优于线性基线，建立了首个轮抽结果预测的学习基准。

<h3 id="396">[396] An Exploration of Agentic Information Fusion for Test Maintenance Prediction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04786](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04786) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jingxiong Liu, Nasser Mohammadiha, Gregory Gay
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Software Testing, Multi-Agent System, Data Fusion
- **Summary**: 针对测试维护预测中生产代码与测试代码关系复杂的问题，本文提出MAST多代理框架，融合静态、词法和语义分析。在21个工业Java仓库上的评估表明，MAST在精确率、F1和F2分数上优于基线，但召回率有所下降。

<h3 id="397">[397] Evaluating the Effect of Linguistic Relatedness on Cross-Lingual Transfer in Large Multilingual Automatic Speech Recognition</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04814](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04814) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Andrei Florian, Cynthia Jayne Amol, Hope Kerubo Ombaba, Xiaoyu Cui, Boniface Mwau, Biatus Maina Kamau, Lilian Diana Awuor Wanzare, Christiane Fellbaum, Happy Buzaaba
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Automatic Speech Recognition, Cross-Lingual, Low-Resource NLP
- **Summary**: 针对利用语言相关性扩展大型ASR到低资源非洲语言的有效性问题，本文进行了系统实验。结果表明，在最少目标语言数据条件下，相关辅助语言的预适应并未带来实际意义上的迁移增益，提示语言相关性并非可靠预测因素。

<h3 id="398">[398] HamQASBench: A Hamiltonian-Informed Diagnostic Benchmark for Evaluating Quantum Architecture Search</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04845](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04845) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiayang Niu, Akib Karim, Yan Wang, Jie Li, Ke Deng, Azadeh Alavi, Muhammad Usman, Yongli Ren
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Quantum Computing, Benchmark, Quantum Architecture Search
- **Summary**: 针对现有量子架构搜索（QAS）基准忽略哈密顿量结构的问题，本文提出HamQASBench，将11个分子组织为五个结构层级，并引入后验关键结构提取。基准测试揭示了过参数化、特征态陷入等传统指标无法发现的失败模式。

<h3 id="399">[399] Pretraining Curricula Enable Selective Fine-tuning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04846](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04846) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sebastian A. Bruijns, Jirko Rubruck, Mia H. Whitefield, Kai J. Sandbrink, Fazl Barez, Christopher Summerfield
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Curriculum Learning, Fine-Tuning, AI Safety
- **Summary**: 针对预训练课程如何影响学习、泛化和微调选择性的问题，本文比较了平衡与非平衡课程。非平衡课程促进上下文学习并提升拒绝微调的选择性，通过解纠缠的神经回路实现，为安全微调的精确性提供了工具。

<h3 id="400">[400] SynSFX: Multi-Model Sound Effects Synthesis Dataset for Deepfake Detection and Evaluation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04848](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04848) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Linxi Li, Yuncong Yu, Qianwei Guo, Liwei Jin, Yechen Wang, Carsten Maple
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Audio Generation, Deepfake Detection, Dataset
- **Summary**: 针对音频深度伪造检测器对合成音效泛化能力有限的问题，本文提出了SynSFX数据集，包含来自7个文本到音频模型的43374个片段（26452合成，16922真实），为音效深度伪造研究提供大规模资源。

<h3 id="401">[401] EventCoT: Event-centric Video Chain-of-thought for Reasoning Temporal Localization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04872](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04872) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Youngkil Song, Yoonjae Baek, Dongwon Kim, Inho Kim, Dongkeun Kim, Suha Kwak
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Video Understanding, Chain-of-Thought
- **Summary**: EventCoT针对推理时间定位任务中需要同时生成答案及其时间间隔的挑战，提出首个事件中心视频思维链框架EventCoT，通过事件级标记化和嵌入匹配实现高效的推理与定位。在ActivityNet-RTL上达到SOTA，且视觉标记更少；在ReXTime上零样本表现优异。

<h3 id="402">[402] Graph Representation Learning of Longitudinal Medical Imaging Trajectories for Treatment Response Prediction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04912](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04912) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Johannes Kiechle, Richard Osuala, Daniel M. Lang, Stefan M. Fischer, Ivana Janíčková, Karim Lekadir, Julia A. Schnabel, Jan C. Peeken
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Graph Neural Network, Self-Supervised Learning
- **Summary**: 针对乳腺癌新辅助化疗反应预测中个体差异大的问题，提出基于纵向DCE-MRI的3D时空图神经网络框架，集成关系建模和自监督轨迹学习。在585例ISPY-2数据集上显著超越视觉和自监督基线。

<h3 id="403">[403] Efficient Perception in Automotive Detection and Tracking Using Neuromorphic Computing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04921](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04921) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Manish Kolachalam, Rani Malhotra
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Neuromorphic Computing, Spiking Neural Network, Object Detection
- **Summary**: 针对深度学习的能耗和计算瓶颈，首次全面评估脉冲神经网络(SNN)在自动驾驶多目标检测与跟踪中的性能。采用SpikeYOLO迁移学习，在KITTI上检测mAP达0.937，跟踪HOTA达0.701，性能堪比传统方法且能效更高。

<h3 id="404">[404] Input Pathways Shape Few-Shot, Not Zero-Shot, Binding in Tiny Transformers: A Fully-Enumerable Study</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04926](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04926) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yoshiyuki Ootani
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Compositional Generalization, Few-Shot Learning, In-Context Learning
- **Summary**: 研究小规模Transformer中信息输入方式（符号、代码、感知向量）对组合绑定能力的影响，通过穷举枚举分析。发现零样本绑定均低于随机水平，而少样本绑定效率由参数共享和代码可读性双因素决定。

<h3 id="405">[405] DSWAM: A Dual-System World Action Foundation Model for Fine-Grained Robot Manipulation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04927](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04927) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jian Zhu, Jianjun Zhang, Taiyi Su, Tianbin Liu, Zhangyuan Wang, Kai Xie, Zitai Huang, Chong Ma, Youzhang He, Tianjian Wang, Hanyang Wang, Weihao Ding, Yi Xu
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Robot Manipulation, Vision-Language Model, World Model
- **Summary**: 针对世界动作模型缺乏语言级分解能力的问题，提出双系统世界动作基础模型DSWAM，包含System 1执行器和System 2子任务规划器，并结合TensorRT加速。在DeMaVLA真实机器人操作场景中实现细粒度操控。

<h3 id="406">[406] MemPose: Category-level Object Pose Estimation with Memory</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04930](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04930) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xiao Lin, Minghao Zhu, Yun Peng, Liuyi Wang, Qiyi Wang, Chengju Liu, Qijun Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Pose Estimation, Memory Architecture
- **Venue**: ECCV 2026
- **Summary**: 针对类别级物体位姿估计中固定形状先验泛化性不足的问题，提出记忆增强框架MemPose，引入外部记忆缓冲存储动态结构表示。在REAL275等四个基准上达到SOTA。

<h3 id="407">[407] Multi-Robot Open Adaptive Teaming Across Unseen Environments, Partners, and Scales</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04972](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04972) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yang Li, Feng Xue, Fan Mo, Yunhao Liu, Jianhong Wang, Ying Wen, Qingrui Zhang, Shaoshuai Mou, Wei Pan
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Game Theory, Multi-Robot System
- **Summary**: 针对多机器人团队在未知环境、伙伴和规模下的自适应问题，提出超图形式博弈公式和超图开放学习算法HOLA，逐步扩展多样性。在协同追捕任务中超越基线，并成功迁移至物理机器人。

<h3 id="408">[408] Joint Velocity Slope Diffusion Prior for Structurally Constrained Velocity Model Building</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04982](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04982) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Francesco Brandolin, Tariq Alkhalifah
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Inverse Problem, Seismic Imaging
- **Summary**: 针对地震速度模型分辨率受限的问题，提出扩散引导框架，融合平面波PDE正则化、结构预条件反演和测量引导扩散采样。在Volve和Viking Graben数据集上改善了结构连续性和地质真实性。

<h3 id="409">[409] LLM for the development of FCM</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04983](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04983) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Alexis Kafantaris
- **Subjects**: cs.NE; cs.AI; cs.ET; cs.GL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Fuzzy Logic, Information Extraction, Fuzzy Cognitive Map
- **Summary**: 针对从文本数据构建模糊认知图的需求，利用本地大语言模型Qwen2.5-32B从酒店评论中提取定量数据并训练FCM。实验表明该FCM能有效关联评论星级与满意度。

<h3 id="410">[410] The Map Behind the Flow: Finite-Step Gradient Descent as a Dynamical System</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.04993](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04993) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Thomas Hofmann
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Optimization, Training Dynamics
- **Summary**: 针对深度学习中梯度下降的动力学现象（如边缘稳定性）无法用梯度流解释的问题，将固定步长梯度下降作为离散动力系统进行精确可解模型分析。揭示了学习率作为结构参数导致分岔和振荡，驱动表示对齐。

<h3 id="411">[411] TACTIC-KG: Toward Small Agent Teams for Cyber Threat Intelligence Knowledge Graph Construction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.05001](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05001) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mouhamed Amine Bouchiha, Gregory Blanc
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.MA
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Knowledge Graph, Cybersecurity
- **Summary**: 针对网络威胁情报知识图谱构建中单一大模型成本高且不稳定的问题，提出TACTIC-KG智能体框架，将任务分解为模块化LLM智能体（抽取、类型、验证、整理）。使用3B-8B模型在F1、类型准确率和图结构相似性上超越大模型基线。

<h3 id="412">[412] Comparison of Loss Functions for Robust Deep Learning-based Echocardiography Segmentation when Learning with Partially Labelled Data from Multiple Domains</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.05008](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05008) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Iman Islam, Esther Puyol-Antón, Bram Ruijsink, Andrew J. Reader, Andrew P. King
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical Image Segmentation, Domain Adaptation, Echocardiography
- **Venue**: Journal of Machine Learning for Biomedical Imaging (MELBA)
- **Summary**: 针对多来源部分标注数据下的超声心动图分割挑战，评估三种损失函数（aCCE、marginal loss、aBCE）处理部分标签的能力。实验表明marginal loss在多标签缺失场景下鲁棒性最强。

<h3 id="413">[413] ImputeECG: Deep Learning Reconstruction of Complete 12-Lead Electrocardiograms from Incomplete Recordings for Cardiac Assessment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.05009](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05009) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xiaocheng Fang, Haoyu Wang, Jieyi Cai, Qinghao Zhao, Jun Li, Shanwei Zhang, Guangkun Nie, Yujie Xiao, Shun Huang, Jiarui Jin, Hongmin Liu, Guodong Wang, Shuohua Chen, Liming Lin, Shouling Wu, Hongyan Li, Shenda Hong
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Transformer, ECG Analysis
- **Summary**: 针对不完整心电图记录阻碍AI评估的问题，提出掩码条件一维Transformer自编码器ImputeECG，补全12导联10秒心电图。在PTB-XL上MAE降低41.7-51.0%，下游分类AUROC达92.28%，并在真实队列中改善性别和年龄预测。

<h3 id="414">[414] Hyperparameter Transfer in Graph Neural Networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.05017](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05017) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Gage DeZoort, Boris Hanin
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Graph Neural Network, Hyperparameter Optimization, Transfer Learning
- **Summary**: 针对图神经网络超参数迁移研究不足的问题，提出适用于SGD/Adam/AdamW的迁移参数化方案，并通过理论缩放分析和实验验证。该方案在宽度和深度增大时保持稳定的特征更新和学习率迁移，提升性能。

<h3 id="415">[415] Your Agent's Memories Are Not Its Own: Forged Reasoning Attacks on LLM Agent Memory and Defenses</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.05029](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05029) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Neeraj Karamchandani, Piyush Nagasubramaniam, Sencun Zhu, Dinghao Wu
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, LLM Security, Adversarial Attack
- **Summary**: 针对LLM智能体持久记忆带来的推理历史投毒攻击，提出FARMA攻击和SENTINEL防御。FARMA通过伪造推理痕迹实现高达100%攻击成功率，SENTINEL将成功率降至0%且无假阳性。

<h3 id="416">[416] LLM-Based Test Oracles: Source-of-Authority Taxonomy -- A Systematic Literature Review</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.05031](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05031) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ali Hassaan Mughal, Muhammad Bilal
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Software Testing, LLM Evaluation, Test Oracle
- **Summary**: 针对LLM测试预言缺少权威性来源分类的问题，依据PRISMA 2020进行系统文献综述，分析54篇研究。发现规格派生权威占一半，其余无规格；提出权威来源、形式和裁决机制三个分析维度。

<h3 id="417">[417] RUFNet: Query-Guided Support Mask Refinement and Uncertainty Fusion based on Hybrid Mamba for Few-Shot Brain Tumor Segmentation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.05035](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05035) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dongyi He, Xiangkai Wang, Binbing Xu, Bin Jiang, Hongjie Yan, Weixiang Liu, Wai Ting Siok, Nizhuan Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Few-Shot Learning, Image Segmentation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/hdy6438/RUFNet)
- **Summary**: 针对少样本脑肿瘤分割中的支持掩模噪声、患者间差异及置信度缺失问题，提出RUFNet框架，采用混合Mamba骨干网络、注意力引导掩模精化和不确定性融合模块。在BraTS 2020数据集上，1-shot和5-shot Dice分别达到84.3%和86.1%，优于现有方法。

<h3 id="418">[418] Beyond Independent Labels: Schwartz-Geometry Decoding for Human Value Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.05052](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05052) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Víctor Yeste, Paolo Rosso
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CY; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Human Value Detection, Psychometrics, Label Space Geometry
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/VictorMYeste/schwartz-geometry-value-detection)
- **Summary**: 人类价值检测常忽略Schwartz理论中的圆形连续体结构。本文提出Schwartz感知能量解码器，在DeBERTa-v3-base上通过后处理使标签集与连续体更一致，且不损害F1分数，验证了理论感知解码的有效性。

<h3 id="419">[419] AIFS-SUBS: Extending Data-Driven Forecasting to Sub-Seasonal Timescales</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.05100](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05100) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jakob Schloer, Steffen Tietsche, Christopher D. Roberts, Lorenzo Zampieri, Simon Lang, Gert Mertes, Gareth Jones, Matthew Chantry, Frederic Vitart
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Weather Forecasting, Climate Science
- **Summary**: 将数据驱动模型扩展到次季节预报面临误差累积和系统性偏差等挑战。AIFS-SUBS通过24小时自回归步长、加入平流层和顶辐射预测因子，在2-6周匹配IFS概率技能，MJO预报延长8天，能耗降低约200倍。

<h3 id="420">[420] Grokking Is Conditional and Fragile: A Fully-Tractable, Multi-Seed Study at 12K Parameters</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.05104](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05104) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yoshiyuki Ootani
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Grokking, Mechanistic Interpretability, Training Dynamics
- **Summary**: Grokking现象通常在大模型中研究且依赖单次运行。本文在12K参数Llama风格transformer上通过多种子率测量发现，grokking是有条件的、脆弱的相变，依赖于训练集覆盖率和权重衰减，且对数值环境敏感。

<h3 id="421">[421] Localized LoRA-MoE: Block-wise Low-Rank Experts With Adaptive Routing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.05114](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05114) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Babak Barazandeh, Subhabrata Majumdar, Vinay Prithyani, George Michailidis
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning, Mixture-of-Experts, Low-Rank Adaptation
- **Summary**: 标准LoRA在多任务中易受梯度冲突影响。提出局部化LoRA-MoE，通过块级或单元级动态路由实现上下文自适应微调，在矩阵模拟和空间视觉任务中解决了优化死锁，提供了梯度防火墙。

<h3 id="422">[422] Agent Data Injection Attacks are Realistic Threats to AI Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.05120](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05120) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Woohyuk Choi, Juhee Kim, Taehyun Kang, Jihyeon Jeong, Luyi Xing, Byoungyoung Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Security, Agent Security, Data Poisoning
- **Summary**: 现有AI代理安全研究主要关注指令注入，忽略了数据注入攻击。本文定义代理数据注入（ADI）攻击，在Claude、Codex等真实代理中发现漏洞，可执行任意点击和远程代码执行，表明当前代理缺乏数据隔离。

<h3 id="423">[423] Three-Phase Evaluation of AI-Assisted Software Development Life Cycle</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.05125](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05125) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Joshua Strubel, Professor Carrie Russell, Carson Crockett, Jason Ferraro, Nathan Londhe, Uzayr Syed, Jacob Viehe
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Human-AI Interaction, Software Engineering
- **Summary**: 探索AI自主性水平对软件开发生产率、需求遵从性和认知负荷的影响。通过三阶段实验（部分AI辅助、AI专属工作流），发现更高自主性减少开发时间并提高需求遵从性，但增加挫折感。

<h3 id="424">[424] PDEFlow: Autonomous Agentic PDE Pipelines for Neural Operator Learning and Solver-Free Inference</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.05134](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05134) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Akshat Jani, Prathamesh Gadekar, Sakhinana Sagar Srinivas, Venkataramana Runkana
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Neural Operator, PDE Solver, Automated Research
- **Summary**: 神经算子训练需要手动构建从问题描述到推理的流水线。PDEFlow是一个自主代理框架，将ODE/PDE描述转化为基于求解器的神经算子流水线，集成FEniCSx后端和多分支贝叶斯DeepONet，实现自动化。

<h3 id="425">[425] Open Problems in AI Incident Governance</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.05163](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05163) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI

- **Venue**: ICML Technical AI Governance Research workshop 2026
- **Summary**: AI事故治理在定义、分类、监控和报告方面存在不一致。本文审查现有框架，发现数据收集和分类缺乏一致性，导致分析深度和准确性不足。

<h3 id="426">[426] Relational Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Dynamic Pricing in High-Speed Railway Markets</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.05179](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05179) (cross-listed)

- **Summary**: 自由化铁路系统中，多代理动态定价面临部分可观测和禁止通信的挑战。提出基于实体图的关系图卷积网络与MA-TD3算法，在铁路定价环境中实现了更高收入和稳定性。

<h3 id="427">[427] When Claws Remember but Do Not Tell: Stealthy Memory Injection in Persistent Personal Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.05189](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05189) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yechao Zhang, Shiqian Zhao, Jiawen Zhang, Jie Zhang, Gelei Deng, Xiaogeng Liu, Chaowei Xiao, Tianwei Zhang

- **Summary**: 持久个人代理的长时记忆可能被外部内容静默注入并复用为可信状态。提出WhisperBench基准和MemGhost单次载荷生成框架，在多种代理架构和后端上实现87.5%成功率，且能绕过防御。

<h3 id="428">[428] Unified Audio Intelligence Without Regressing on Text Intelligence</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.05196](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05196) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhifeng Kong, Sang-gil Lee, Jaehyeon Kim, Boxin Wang, Zihan Liu, Sungwon Kim, Yang Chen, Arushi Goel, Rajarshi Roy, Wenliang Dai, Zhuolin Yang, Yangyi Chen, Dongfu Jiang, Sreyan Ghosh, Tuomas Rintamaki, Andrew Tao, Jonathan Raiman, Mohammad Shoeybi, Bryan Catanzaro, Wei Ping
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.SD; eess.AS

- **Summary**: 统一音频文本LLM常导致文本智能退化。Audex基于Nemotron-Cascade-2-30B-A3B，采用单一Transformer解码器，通过多阶段训练和蒸馏，在音频理解、生成等任务上达到SOTA，且保持文本能力。

<h3 id="429">[429] Noisy-Channel Minimum Bayes Risk Decoding</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.05198](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05198) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yusuke Sakai, Hidetaka Kamigaito, Taro Watanabe
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Decoding Strategy, Text Generation, Minimum Bayes Risk Decoding
- **Summary**: 最小贝叶斯风险解码忽略假设和参考间双向方向性。引入噪声信道分解，包含四个交互分量，提供统一解释和度量特定可解释性，发现信道权重可能改进原始MBR。

<h3 id="430">[430] Optimizing ML Workload Partitioning between CPUs and CIM Accelerators for Heterogeneous Computing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.05240](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05240) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.ET; cs.AI; cs.AR; cs.DC; cs.LG

- **Venue**: 34th IFIP/IEEE International Conference on Very Large Scale Integration SoC (VLSI-SoC)
- **Summary**: 现有CIM加速器工作负载分区忽略RRAM约束和CPU资源。提出基于整数线性规划的分区框架，在边端和高性能CPU上分别获得30.9倍和7.3倍加速。

<h3 id="431">[431] CanniUplift: A Holistic Framework for Mitigating Seller and Incentive Cannibalization in E-commerce Uplift Modeling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.05242](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05242) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zuwang He, Shihao Shu, Yuli Qu, Hanyu Gao, Ziliang Zhang, Diwei Chen, Xiangda Yan, Buyu Gao, Tanchao Zhu, Yumeng Li, Junxiong Zhu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.IR
- **Tags**: Causal Inference, Treatment Effect Estimation, Recommender System
- **Venue**: KDD 2026
- **Summary**: 传统提升建模在多卖家环境中忽略卖家间和激励间的蚕食效应。CanniUplift通过全局GMV一致性约束和赎回解噪机制，在工业数据集上显著提升wAUUC和wQINI，在线部署实现4.08%增量GMV提升。

<h3 id="432">[432] Privacy-Preserving Robustness Verification for Neural Networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.05251](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05251) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nianyun Song, Xiaokun Luan, Yu Guo, Rongfang Bie, Meng Sun, Xiyue Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.LO
- **Tags**: Neural Network Verification, Privacy, Secure Multi-Party Computation
- **Venue**: UAI 2026
- **Summary**: 神经网络验证需要完全访问模型和数据，与隐私冲突。SecureCROWN基于安全两方计算实现隐私保护鲁棒性验证，结果与明文匹配，在合理时间内完成。

<h3 id="433">[433] Shifting from Discrete to Continuous Reference Data: QSM-Derived Horizontal Tree Biomass Distribution for Deep Learning Biomass Estimation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.05260](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05260) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nils Griese, Christoph Kleinn, Nils Nölke
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Biomass Estimation, LiDAR Analysis
- **Summary**: 本文针对LiDAR生物量估计中离散样地汇总引起的边界效应问题，提出使用定量结构模型（QSM）导出的连续水平生物量分布（HBD）作为参考。通过训练稀疏3D U-Net模型，在100平方米样地上，HBD参考将相对均方根误差降低16.84%，R²提高0.22。该方法有效校正了边缘效应，提升了小样地尺寸下的模型性能。

<h3 id="434">[434] Adaptive Inference Batching using Policy Gradients</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.05272](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05272) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ruslan Sharifullin
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.DC; cs.PF
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, Reinforcement Learning, Resource Allocation
- **Summary**: 针对推理服务系统中静态批处理策略无法适应突发、异构工作负载的问题，提出使用强化学习（REINFORCE和PPO）学习自适应批处理和路由策略。在单GPU场景下RL仅带来微小提升（+0.1%至+1.0%），但在多GPU异构路由中，RL发现工作负载隔离策略，相比轮询策略提升348%，相比最短队列提升48%。结果表明RL的优势集中在组合性、多资源决策上。

<h3 id="435">[435] ProPS: Prompted Profile Synthesis for Natural Language-Conditioned Speaker Embedding Distributions</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.05276](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05276) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Thomas Thebaud, Junhyeok Lee, Laureano Moro-Velazquez, Jesus Villalba Lopez, Najim Dehak
- **Subjects**: eess.AS; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Text-to-Speech, Speaker Embedding, Voice Cloning
- **Summary**: 现有说话人嵌入提取器是描述性而非生成性的，本文提出ProPS框架，根据自然语言提示（如“三十岁男性印度口音”）生成说话人嵌入的分布。使用混合密度网络在大规模数据集上训练，预测x-vector空间中的高斯混合模型。实验表明ProPS能生成保留年龄、性别、口音等属性的嵌入分布，可用于可控的语音合成。

<h3 id="436">[436] Wavelet Scattering Transform for Interpretable Schizophrenia Biomarker Discovery and Classification from Resting-State EEG</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.05282](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05282) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Md. Taksimul Ahsan Tawhid, Nasif Ahmed Rafe, Alif Tahmid Priyom, K. M. Mustafizur Rahman
- **Subjects**: eess.SP; cs.AI; cs.LG; stat.ME
- **Tags**: EEG Processing, Medical AI, Interpretability
- **Summary**: 针对精神分裂症EEG生物标记物缺乏的问题，提出结合多阶小波散射变换（WST）、严格留一法交叉验证和SHAP可解释性的诊断框架。在单受试者独立评估下，随机森林达到90.48%准确率（AUC=0.9339，敏感度=95.56%）。二阶散射系数（编码交叉频率耦合）是最具区分性的特征，P3电极是最具判别力的部位。

<h3 id="437">[437] Air Quality Downscaling with Station-Guided Pseudo-Supervision</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.05292](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05292) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Guorun Wang, Simone Foti, Andreas D. Demou, Leonidas Kotoulas, Theodoros Christoudias, Alexandros Koliousis, Mihalis Nicolaou, Stefanos Zafeiriou
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Environmental AI, Image Super-Resolution, Downscaling
- **Summary**: 针对粗分辨率大气场到局部PM2.5变化的超分辨率挑战，提出站点引导的伪监督框架。利用多尺度Transformer网络联合超分辨率（×40，约1 km）和偏差校正，通过时间无关的高斯混合传播策略缓解稀疏实地数据的密集监督难题。在欧洲范围的评估表明模型恢复了细粒度空间结构并有效减轻了局部CAMS偏差。

<h3 id="438">[438] Topological Shape Representation for Aneurysm -- Bifurcation Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.05317](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05317) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Akshay Gokhale, Mansi Dhamne
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Topological Deep Learning, Aneurysm Detection
- **Summary**: 针对颅内动脉瘤CTA检测中高假阳性率的问题，提出基于光滑欧拉特征变换（SECT）的拓扑感知假阳性减少框架。SECT在RSNA 2025数据集上达到AUC 0.943，在亚3毫米病灶上保持相同AUC和78.5%敏感度（95%特异度），且在四个制造商的扫描仪上泛化良好。该方法通过捕捉不对称几何不变量可靠区分动脉瘤和血管分叉。

<h3 id="439">[439] Steering Optimisation Trajectories in Diffusion Representation Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.05319](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05319) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Rajat Rasal, Avinash Kori, Tian Xia, Ben Glocker
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Representation Learning, Disentanglement
- **Summary**: 本文研究了扩散自编码器中优化动力学对潜在结构的影响，发现模型早期训练轨迹分为重建优先和解缠优先两种模式。提出SteeringDRL方法，通过门控残差U-Net和噪声级课程训练引导优化走向强表征。在解缠基准上改善了表征质量并降低了种子敏感性，且能扩展到目标中心学习中的空间解缠。

<h3 id="440">[440] TREK: Distill to Explore, Reinforce to Refine</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.05339](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05339) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuanda Xu, Zhengze Zhou, Kayhan Behdin, Jelena Markovic-Voronov, Hejian Sang, Xiaomin Li, Wenhui Zhu, Xinchen Du, Aida Rahmattalabi, Ran He, Sen Na, Zhipeng Wang, Alborz Geramifard
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Reinforcement Learning, Knowledge Distillation
- **Summary**: 针对GRPO在困难提示上因策略样本不足而停滞的问题，提出TREK方法：先通过蒸馏从教师（可为外部或自上下文）中获取验证过的解，扩展学生策略支持，再返回标准GRPO。在数学推理上，TREK使Qwen3-8B在AIME 2025上从36.9提升至40.3（avg@16）；在ALFWorld上成功率从75.8%提升至82.8%，且困难任务上早期即获得高成功率。

<h3 id="441">[441] Multiplayer Interactive World Models with Representation Autoencoders</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.05352](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05352) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Anthony Hu, Václav Volhejn, Adrien Ramanana Rahary, Chris Mulder, Aditya Makkar, Amélie Royer, Manu Orsini, Alyx Liao, Adam Jelley, Eloi Alonso, Florian Laurent, Fredrik Norén, James Swingos, Jan Hünermann, Kent Rollins, Lucas Hosseini, Matthieu Le Cauchois, Maxim Peter, Pim de Witte, Tim Brown, Vincent Micheli, Moritz Böhle, Gabriel de Marmiesse, Viktoriia Sharmanska, Lucia Specia, Michael Black, Patrick Pérez
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Game AI, Multi-Agent System, World Model
- **Summary**: 本文首次提出了用于高度动态、复杂物理交互环境的多玩家世界模型，在《火箭联盟》游戏中，模型以多个玩家的动作序列为条件，学习将场景变化归因于正确玩家。50亿参数潜扩散模型在单Nvidia B200 GPU上以20 FPS实时生成四玩家对局，且滚动稳定时间远超训练时长的五分钟。开源了数据集和代码。

<h3 id="442">[442] Selective Disclosure Watermarking for Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.05353](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05353) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xuyang Chen, Xiang Li, Yangxinyu Xie, Qi Long
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Watermarking, Data Privacy
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/xuyangc03/hero-watermark)
- **Summary**: 现有大语言模型多比特水印方法不支持选择性披露，导致隐私问题。本文提出层次化词汇路由（HeRo）框架，递归划分词汇表并将水印信息分布到层次化层中，使不同验证者只能解码其访问级别的有效载荷。该方法保持无偏采样，实验表明支持细粒度访问控制，同时实现高检测精度和低延迟。

<h3 id="443">[443] REDDIT: Correcting Model-Generated Timestamp Drift in ASR without Forgetting via Replay-Based Distribution Editing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.05364](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05364) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Cheng-Kang Chou, Ming-To Chuang, Ke-Han Lu, Chan-Jan Hsu, Hung-yi Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.SD
- **Tags**: Speech Recognition, Model Editing, Timestamp Correction
- **Summary**: 针对自回归ASR系统在长非语音段中生成的漂移时间戳问题，提出REDDIT轻量级后训练框架，通过重放分布编辑校正时间戳同时避免灾难性遗忘。仅更新1.6%参数、使用34.9小时纠正音频，将长间隔mIoU从38.7%提升至95.0%，混合间隔平均绝对对齐偏差从2752 ms降至223 ms，且不降低非目标ASR行为。

<h3 id="444">[444] SPEARBench: A Benchmark for Naturalness Evaluation in Streaming Speech-to-Speech Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.05365](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05365) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Thomas Thebaud, Yuzhe Wang, Hao Zhang, Sathvik Manikantan Napa Ugandhar, Ashish Hallur, Georgi Tinchev, Venkatesh Ravichandran, Laureano Moro-Velazquez
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; eess.AS
- **Tags**: Spoken Language Model, Dialogue System, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 现有基准无法评估流式语音到语音语言模型在对话中的自然性，本文提出SPEARBench基准，从Seamless Interaction语料库构建控制对话提示，多维度评估响应延迟、中断、语音质量、语言一致性等。结果表明现有模型在信号质量上表现良好，但在延迟、重叠、方言保持、情感适应等方面与人类存在差异。

<h3 id="445">[445] GaP: A Graph-as-Policy Multi-Agent Self-Learning Harness For Variational Automation Tasks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.05369](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05369) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kaiyuan Chen, Shuangyu Xie, Letian Fu, Justin Yu, William Pacini, Sandeep Bajamahal, Hudson Kim, Jaimyn Drake, Daehwa Kim, Haoru Xue, Jonathan Francis, Christian Juette, Peter Schaldenbrand, Muhammet Yunus Seker, Ruwan Wickramarachchi, Uksang Yoo, Guanzhi Wang, Adithyavairavan Murali, Balakumar Sundaralingam, S. Shankar Sastry, Spencer Huang, Yuke Zhu, Linxi &#34;Jim&#34; Fan, Ken Goldberg
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Robotics, Multi-Agent System, Task Planning
- **Summary**: 针对变体自动化任务中模型无关策略可靠性不足的问题，提出Graph-as-Policy (GaP)多智能体编程框架，从模块化机器人技能库生成包含感知、规划和控制节点的有向计算图，并通过内部模拟迭代优化图结构和参数。在8个新基准任务上（4仿真正实），GaP显著优于基线。

<h3 id="446">[446] Cortex: A Bidirectionally Aligned Embodied Agent Framework for Long-horizon Manipulation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.05377](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05377) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiaqi Peng, Xiqian Yu, Delin Feng, Yuqiang Yang, Wenzhe Cai, Jing Xiong, Ganlin Yang, Jinliang Zheng, Jiafei Cao, Xueyuan Wei, Jiangmiao Pang, Yuan Shen, Tai Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Embodied AI, Vision-Language Model, Robotic Manipulation
- **Summary**: 针对长期操作任务中高层规划语义与低层执行运动学之间的差距，提出Cortex双向对齐具身智能体框架，将操作子任务标准化为32个规范技能原语，并注入可执行性原则。通过事件平衡采样和精心设计的推理工程，在Libero-long上提升3.1%，在RoboTwin上提升4.1%，并零样本完成未见过的真实世界长期任务（如化学实验）。

<h3 id="447">[447] What Does a Discrete Diffusion Model Learn?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.05381](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05381) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Rodrigo Casado Noguerales, Bernhard Schölkopf, Thomas Hofmann, Aran Raoufi
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.IT; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Generative Model, Deep Learning Theory
- **Summary**: 本文严格推导了离散扩散模型的连续时间马尔可夫链ELBO，证明负ELBO等于数据熵加路径KL散度，其唯一优解是真实反向跳跃率的条件期望。对于令牌因式分解噪声，优化器有精确坐标形式（去噪器、腔体、分数）。该框架统一了MDM、UDM、SEDD等损失，解释了去噪器和腔体在掩码扩散中一致而在均匀扩散中不一致的原因，并数值验证了所有恒等式。

<h3 id="448">[448] Search Beyond What Can Be Taught: Evolving the Knowledge Boundary in Agentic Visual Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.05382](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05382) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Haozhe Wang, Weijia Feng, Jinpeng Yu, Che Liu, Ping Nie, Fangzhen Lin, Jiaming Liu, Ruihua Huang, Jimmy Lin, Wenhu Chen, Cong Wei
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Image Generation, LLM Agent, RAG
- **Summary**: 针对视觉生成器对长尾世界知识（新角色、新事件等）无法正确渲染的问题，构建SearchGen-20K基准和SearchGen-Corpus-1M语料库。发现前沿生成器在此基准上仅得21-28分，而朴素搜索会注入噪声。提出“先教后搜”协同训练框架，即使最简版本也能带来单调改进，为工具增强的视觉生成奠定基础。

<h3 id="449">[449] Interpretable Human-Label-Free Deep Learning for Real-Bogus Classification with Uncertainty Quantification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.05393](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05393) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Raphaël Bonnet-Guerrini, Bruno Sanchez, Dominique Fouchez, Benjamin Racine, Maya Guy, Mariam Sabalbal, Manal Yassine, Vincenzo Piuri
- **Subjects**: astro-ph.IM; astro-ph.GA; astro-ph.HE; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Noisy Label Learning, Uncertainty Estimation, Data Synthesis
- **Summary**: 时域巡天中的真实-虚假分类需要昂贵的人工标签或受噪声影响。该方法结合模拟注入瞬变源和受污染巡天数据，使用双网络非对称协同教学进行弱监督训练，并提出混合不确定性量化策略。在标记子集上表现良好，对严重类污染稳定，混合UQ校准效果接近昂贵的集成方法。

<h3 id="450">[450] Weak-to-Strong Generalization via Direct On-Policy Distillation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.05394](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05394) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shiyuan Feng, Huan-ang Gao, Haohan Chi, Hanlin Wu, Zhilong Zhang, Zheng Jiang, Bingxiang He, Wei-Ying Ma, Ya-Qin Zhang, Hao Zhou
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Knowledge Distillation, Weak-to-Strong Generalization
- **Summary**: 基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）提升语言模型推理能力，但在大模型上因大量rollout而昂贵。提出直接在线蒸馏（Direct-OPD），将弱教师模型RL后与RL前的对数比值作为隐式奖励信号，用于强学生模型的在线状态。在AIME 2024上将Qwen3-1.7B从48.3%提升至62.4%，仅用4小时8张A100 GPU。

<h3 id="451">[451] From Fixed to Free Cameras: Calibration-Free View-Robust Vision-Language-Action Model</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.05396](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05396) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.RO
- **Tags**: Embodied AI, Vision-Language Model, Visual Grounding
- **Summary**: 真实机器人部署中相机位置常变，现有视点鲁棒VLA策略需明确相机外参。提出CamVLA，将操作控制与相机几何解耦，预测相机坐标系末端执行器动作和6自由度手眼矩阵，经确定性变换得到基座动作。在仿真和真实数据中跨多种未见视点持续提高成功率。

<h3 id="456">[456] AI's Blind Spots: Geographic Knowledge and Diversity Deficit in Generated Urban Scenario</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.AI

- **Summary**: 需要全面综述强化学习与语言模型结合的技术。该综述涵盖RL算法及针对LLM的RL技术（RLHF、RLAIF、DPO、GRPO等），分析应用、失败模式、稳定性权衡，并给出比较分类。RLHF仍占主导，基于结果的RL显著提升逐步推理，但奖励黑客、计算成本等挑战持续存在。
