- **Summary**: 基于扰动的可解释性方法依赖分布外变异样本。提出激活-去激活（AD）前向传播范式，通过关闭模型相应部分替代输入扰动。为CNN实现ConvAD，无需额外训练即生成更鲁棒且可迁移的解释。

<h3 id="462">[462] RLIE: Rule Generation with Logistic Regression, Iterative Refinement, and Evaluation for Large Language Models</h3>

- **Authors**: Yang Yang, Hua XU, Zhangyi Hu, Yutao Yue
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Neurosymbolic AI, Probabilistic Reasoning, Inductive Reasoning
- **Summary**: LLM可提出规则但忽略交互，概率规则学习未充分探索。RLIE框架：LLM生成候选规则，逻辑回归学习权重，迭代精炼，评估。直接应用加权规则优于用规则提示LLM，LLM擅长语义生成但不擅长概率整合。

<h3 id="463">[463] A Unified Geometric Space for Topological Alignment Between Transformer-Based Models and Human Brain Networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.24342](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.24342) (replaced)

- **Subjects**: cs.AI

- **Summary**: 人工神经网络与大脑信息组织方式的比较仍不清晰，先前对齐受限于特定输入和任务。引入脑-模型拓扑对齐空间，将Transformer注意力拓扑映射到人脑内在连接网络，分析151个Transformer模型。观察到连续弧状分布，全局语义模型与高阶ICN对齐，局部细节模型与感觉ICN对齐。

<h3 id="464">[464] Optimal-Agent-Selection: State-Aware Routing Framework for Efficient Multi-Agent Collaboration</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.GT
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Multilingual Learning, Negotiation System
- **Summary**: 针对LLM谈判能力仅在英语中评估而忽略语言和文化影响的问题，在多语言（英语和四种印度语言）设置下进行受控多智能体模拟。发现语言选择对谈判结果的影响可能大于模型更换，且效果依赖于任务类型。

<h3 id="468">[468] OpenTinker: Separating Concerns in Agentic Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.07376](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.07376) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Siqi Zhu, Jiaxuan You
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.DC
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Reinforcement Learning, LoRA
- **Summary**: 针对基于LoRA的智能体强化学习中管理多个策略的更新和隔离困难的问题，提出OpenTinker系统，支持多LoRA的SFT/RL训练，统一数据路径，隔离适配器状态。实现了多用户、多任务共享基础模型，同时隔离适配器更新。

<h3 id="469">[469] Programming over Thinking: Efficient and Robust Multi-Constraint Planning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.09097](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.09097) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Derrick Goh Xin Deik, Quanyu Long, Zhengyuan Liu, Nancy F. Chen, Wenya Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Automated Planning, Code Generation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/DerrickGXD/SCOPE)
- **Summary**: 针对多约束规划中LLM纯推理不可靠且昂贵、编码策略缺乏通用性的问题，提出SCOPE框架，将推理与代码执行分离，生成可复用的求解函数。在TravelPlanner上成功率达93.1%，比CoT提高61.6%，推理成本降低1.4倍。

<h3 id="470">[470] Graph Neural Networks are Heuristics</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.13465](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.13465) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yimeng Min, Carla P. Gomes
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Graph Neural Network, Combinatorial Optimization
- **Summary**: 针对图神经网络常作为组合优化辅助工具而非独立启发式方法的问题，训练非自回归GNN，仅用可微哈密顿回路目标，无需标签或搜索。在TSP100-500上单次前向传播生成完整回路，优于最近邻贪婪基线。

<h3 id="471">[471] Toward Efficient Agents: Memory, Tool learning, and Planning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.14192](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.14192) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xiaofang Yang, Lijun Li, Heng Zhou, Tong Zhu, Xiaoye Qu, Yuchen Fan, Qianshan Wei, Rui Ye, Li Kang, Yiran Qin, Daizong Liu, Qi Li, Ning Ding, Siheng Chen, Jing Shao
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Survey
- **Summary**: 针对智能体系统效率未被充分研究的问题，综述了智能体记忆、工具学习和规划的效率方法，通过帕累托前沿刻画效果-成本权衡，并总结了评估协议和常用效率指标。

<h3 id="472">[472] Insect-inspired Visual Point-goal Navigation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.16806](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.16806) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yihe Lu, Barbara Webb
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.RO
- **Tags**: Embodied AI, Visual Navigation, Robotics
- **Summary**: 针对自主导航计算成本高的问题，受昆虫神经行为学启发，构建蘑菇体和中央复合体整合模型。在Habitat点目标导航基准上，以数个数量级更低的计算成本达到与最新模型相当的成功率。

<h3 id="473">[473] NEST: Nascent Encoded Steganographic Thoughts</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.14095](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.14095) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Artem Karpov
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CR
- **Tags**: LLM Safety, AI Safety, Chain-of-Thought
- **Summary**: 针对CoT推理监控可能因模型隐藏推理而被破坏的安全问题，探索隐写CoT，评估模型在单次前向中同时推理和隐藏的能力。发现当前前沿模型无法同时进行推理和嵌入，但单独嵌入可行。

<h3 id="474">[474] Framework of Thoughts: A Foundation Framework for Dynamic and Optimized Reasoning based on Chains, Trees, and Graphs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.16512](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.16512) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Felix Fricke, Simon Malberg, Georg Groh
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Prompt Engineering, Chain-of-Thought
- **Venue**: SURGeLLM 2026
- **Summary**: 针对现有推理方案需要静态、问题特定结构而缺乏适应性的问题，提出FoT框架，支持动态构建和优化推理方案，内置超参数调优、提示优化、并行执行和智能缓存。实现显著更快的执行、降低成本和更高的任务得分。

<h3 id="475">[475] ARLArena: A Unified Framework for Stable Agentic Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.21534](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.21534) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xiaoxuan Wang, Han Zhang, Haixin Wang, Yidan Shi, Ruoyan Li, Kaiqiao Han, Chenyi Tong, Haoran Deng, Renliang Sun, Alexander Taylor, Yanqiao Zhu, Jason Cong, Yizhou Sun, Wei Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Reinforcement Learning, Agentic RL
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 针对智能体强化学习(ARL)训练不稳定、容易崩溃的问题，提出ARLArena稳定训练方案和SAMPO优化方法。在多种智能体任务上实现一致稳定的训练和强性能。

<h3 id="476">[476] HVR-Met: A Hypothesis-Verification-Replanning Agentic System for Extreme Weather Diagnosis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.01121](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01121) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Shuo Tang, Jiadong Zhang, Gengxian Zhou, Qizhao Jin, Qinxuan Wang, Yi Hu, Ning Hu, Hongchang Ren, Lingli He, Shiming Xiang, Jingtao Ding, Jian Xu, Jiaolan Fu, Cheng-Lin Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Weather Forecasting, Scientific Reasoning
- **Summary**: 针对极端天气诊断需要多步逻辑推理和专家知识但现有方法不足的问题，提出多智能体气象诊断系统HVR-Met，包含“假设-验证-重规划”闭环机制。在复杂诊断场景中表现优异。

<h3 id="477">[477] Exploring Plan Space through Conversation: An Agentic Framework for LLM-Mediated Explanations in Planning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.02070](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02070) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Guilhem Fouilhé, Rebecca Eifler, Antonin Poché, Sylvie Thiébaux, Nicholas Asher
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.HC; cs.MA
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Automated Planning, Explainable AI
- **Venue**: EUMAS 2026
- **Summary**: 针对自动化规划生成中需要人机交互和解释的问题，提出多智能体LLM架构，支持用户依赖的交互式解释。通过用户研究比较了LLM驱动的交互与模板基线的效果。

<h3 id="478">[478] Correlation-Weighted Multi-Reward Optimization for Compositional Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.18528](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18528) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jungmyung Wi, Hyunsoo Kim, Donghyun Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Text-to-Image, Diffusion Model, Image Synthesis
- **Venue**: ECCV 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/TheDarkKnight-21th/CMO)
- **Summary**: 针对文本到图像模型在组合生成中常遗漏概念的问题，提出基于相关性的多奖励优化(CMO)，自适应加权概念奖励。在ConceptMix、GenEval 2和T2I-CompBench上持续改进。

<h3 id="479">[479] On the Ability of Transformers to Verify Plans</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.19954](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19954) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yash Sarrof, Yupei Du, Katharina Stein, Alexander Koller, Sylvie Thiébaux, Michael Hahn
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Transformer Architecture, Automated Planning, Deep Learning Theory
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 针对Transformer在规划验证任务中的泛化能力缺乏理论理解的问题，引入C*-RASP扩展，分析解码器模型验证计划的能力。识别出一类经典规划域，可证明Transformer能学习验证长计划。

<h3 id="480">[480] Mecha-nudges for Machines</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.23433](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23433) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Giulio Frey, Kawin Ethayarajh
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, AI Ethics, AI Safety
- **Summary**: 针对AI智能体在在线环境中决策时环境本身可能被改变以影响它们的问题，结合贝叶斯说服和V-可用信息框架量化环境变化。在Etsy列表中发现ChatGPT发布后机器可用信息增加了0.143比特，而人类可用信息变化很小。

<h3 id="481">[481] The Anatomy of Uncertainty in LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.24967](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24967) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Aditya Taparia, Ransalu Senanayake, Kowshik Thopalli, Vivek Narayanaswamy
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Uncertainty Estimation, LLM Hallucination
- **Venue**: ICBINB Workshop
- **Summary**: 当前LLM不确定性评估方法缺乏可操作洞察。本文提出将不确定性分解为输入歧义、知识缺口和解码随机性三个语义成分。实验表明这些成分的支配地位随模型规模和任务变化，有助于审计可靠性和检测幻觉。

<h3 id="482">[482] Chronos: The AI Co-Historian</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.03553](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03553) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Lorenz Hufe, Niclas Griesshaber, Gavin Greif, Sebastian Oliver Eck, Philip Torr
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.DL
- **Tags**: Digital Humanities, Document Understanding
- **Summary**: 历史研究中AI应用有限，缺乏专用工具。本文介绍Chronos AI共历史学家，支持自然语言定制工作流并共享扩展。Chronos-Extract在三个历史语料库上实现了高任务准确性，跨越不同世纪、语言和版式。

<h3 id="483">[483] TRACE: Capability-Targeted Agentic Training</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.05336](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05336) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Hangoo Kang, Tarun Suresh, Jon Saad-Falcon, Azalia Mirhoseini
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Fine-Tuning, Self-Improvement
- **Summary**: 主流方法未能针对模型能力缺陷进行训练。本文提出TRACE系统，自动识别缺失能力、合成训练环境、通过RL训练LoRA适配器并组成MoE。在τ2-Bench上提升15.3点，SWE-Bench Verified上提升15.0点，优于基线且样本效率更高。

<h3 id="484">[484] Gypscie: A Cross-Platform AI Artifact Management System</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.10311](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10311) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Fabio Porto, Eduardo Ogasawara, Gabriela Moraes Botaro, Julia Neumann Bastos, Augusto Fonseca, Esther Pacitti, Patrick Valduriez
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.DB
- **Tags**: Knowledge Graph, Provenance, AI Governance
- **Summary**: AI模型生命周期管理涉及异构服务协调复杂。本文提出Gypscie跨平台AI制品管理系统，通过知识图谱实现统一视图、规则查询语言和溯源记录。实验表明其能优化并调度数据流，功能更广泛。

<h3 id="485">[485] Fun-TSG: A Function-Driven Multivariate Time Series Generator with Variable-Level Anomaly Labeling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.14221](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14221) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Pierre Lotte, André Péninou, Olivier Teste
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Data Synthesis, Anomaly Detection, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 现有时序异常检测基准缺乏细粒度标注和透明度。本文提出Fun-TSG可定制时间序列生成器，提供变量和时间戳级别的真实标签。支持自动和手动生成，创建多样、可解释、可复现的基准场景。

<h3 id="486">[486] CAP-CoT: Cycle Adversarial Prompt for Improving Chain of Thoughts in LLM Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.23270](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23270) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Shuxu Chen, Yitian Zhou, Jiaquan Zhang, Haoyu Bian, Wenrui Hu, Aming Wu, Sungyoung Lee, Chaoning Zhang, Hyundong Shin
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Chain-of-Thought, Prompt Engineering
- **Summary**: 思维链推理在多步问题上不稳定。本文提出CAP-CoT循环对抗提示优化框架，通过正向求解器、对抗挑战者和反馈代理迭代改进。在2-3轮内降低变异性，提高推理准确性和鲁棒性。

<h3 id="487">[487] To Use AI as Dice of Possibilities with Timing Computation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.01134](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01134) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jia Li, Vipin Kumar, Rui Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Causal Inference, Temporal Reasoning, Medical AI
- **Summary**: 基于名词的建模范式无法处理时序和未来可能性。本文提出包含可能性空间、时序计算和因果事实的框架。在乳腺癌EHR数据上自动发现轨迹并推断反事实时序。

<h3 id="488">[488] Stop Automating Peer Review Without Rigorous Evaluation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.03202](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03202) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Joachim Baumann, Jiaxin Pei, Sanmi Koyejo, Dirk Hovy
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Peer Review, LLM Evaluation, Robustness Evaluation
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: AI审稿系统缺乏严格评估，存在群聚效应和可游戏性。本文通过实验对比人类与AI审稿，发现AI审稿易被风格改写欺骗。主张需要审稿自动化科学而非随意部署LLM。

<h3 id="489">[489] Agentic Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Financial Document Question Answering</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05409](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05409) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yang Shu, Yingmin Liu, Zequn Xie
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: RAG, Financial AI, Program-of-Thought
- **Summary**: （已撤回）金融文档问答需要多步数值推理。本文提出FinAgent-RAG，结合对比检索、程序化思维和自适应策略路由。在FinQA等数据集上达到76.81%等准确率，优于基线，但论文因方法错误已撤回。

<h3 id="490">[490] Beyond the Black Box: Interpretability of Agentic AI Tool Use</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06890](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06890) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Hariom Tatsat, Ariye Shater
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Mechanistic Interpretability, LLM Agent, Tool Learning
- **Summary**: AI代理的工具使用决策难以诊断。本文利用稀疏自编码器和线性探针在行动前读取内部状态，推断工具需求和风险。识别出与工具决策相关的层和特征，在GPT-OSS和Gemma模型上验证。

<h3 id="491">[491] Attributing Emergence in Million-Agent Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.11404](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11404) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ling Tang, Jilin Mei, Qian Chen, Qihan Ren, Linfeng Zhang, Quanshi Zhang, Jing Shao, Xia Hu, Dongrui Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Social Simulation, Emergent Behavior
- **Summary**: 大规模多代理系统中涌现归因计算困难。本文改进Aumann-Shapley路径积分归因，扩展至百万代理规模。在Bluesky数据上证明小规模与全规模归因存在结构性不一致，非线性指标需要全规模归因。

<h3 id="492">[492] Sign-Separated Asymmetric Finite-Time Error Analysis of Q-Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.16103](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16103) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Donghwan Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Q-Learning
- **Summary**: Q-learning的高估偏差理论理解不足。本文将误差分解为正负分量并推导有限时间速率，证明正分量有更慢指数包络。提供高估偏差的理论证据。

<h3 id="493">[493] When Outcome Looks Right But Discipline Fails: Trace-Based Evaluation Under Hidden Competitor State</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18580](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18580) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Peiying Zhu, Sidi Chang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: AI Evaluation, Reinforcement Learning, Safety Evaluation
- **Venue**: KDD 2026 Workshop on Evaluation and Trustworthiness of Agentic AI
- **Summary**: 仅基于结果的评估可能认证不安全的智能体。本文提出轨迹基评估范式，通过隐藏状态、消融和迁移测试检测纪律缺失。实验表明仅奖励的PPO无法对齐轨迹，而轨迹先验策略更好保持价格分布。

<h3 id="494">[494] HaorFloodAlert: A 72-Hour Machine Learning Early Warning System for Flash Floods in Bangladesh's Haor Wetlands</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.20167](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20167) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Salma Hoque Talukdar Koli, Fahima Haque Talukder Jely, Md. Samiul Alim, Md. Zakir Hossen
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Disaster Response, Remote Sensing, Environmental AI
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/shkoli/HaorFloodAlert)
- **Summary**: 孟加拉国Haor湿地洪水预警困难。本文利用Sentinel-1雷达、降雨、土壤湿度和上游信号，通过月气候异常消除季节偏差。达到90.9%准确率、89.2%F1、AUC 0.939，提前3天发出警报。

<h3 id="495">[495] MUSE-Autoskill: Self-Evolving Agents via Skill Creation, Memory, Management, and Evaluation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27366](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27366) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Huawei Lin, Peng Li, Jie Song, Fuxin Jiang, Tieying Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG; cs.MA
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Skill Learning, Self-Improvement
- **Summary**: 现有技能创建方法将技能视为孤立静态制品。本文提出MUSE-Autoskill框架，统一技能生命周期。在SkillsBench上自创技能超越人类编写技能(85.24% vs 81.17%)，且能有效迁移。

<h3 id="496">[496] Cultural Binding Heads in Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28543](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28543) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Avrile Floro, Luca Benedetto
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Mechanistic Interpretability, Cultural Alignment, LLM Alignment
- **Summary**: LLM在文化组间缺乏差异意识。本文通过机制可解释性识别2-3个注意头对文化绑定有因果贡献。剔除后绑定强度降低9-23%，且知识探针表明模型知道多于其表现。

<h3 id="497">[497] Planning with the Views</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29563](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29563) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Kangrui Wang, Linjie Li, Zhengyuan Yang, Shiqi Chen, Zihan Wang, Li Fei-Fei, Jiajun Wu, Leonidas Guibas, Lijuan Wang, Manling Li
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CV; cs.RO
- **Tags**: Visual Reasoning, 3D Vision
- **Summary**: 当前视觉语言模型（VLM）在单步视图变换上有感知但无法组合多步计划。提出基于自我探索和视图图蒸馏的迭代框架，将探索轨迹构建为视图图并蒸馏到监督任务中。在ScanNet场景上，Qwen2.5-VL-7B从2.5%提升到47.8%，超过GPT-5.4 Pro（18.5%）和Gemini 3.1 Pro（21.4%）。

<h3 id="498">[498] Reasoning4Sciences: Bridging Reasoning Language Models to All Scientific Branches</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01145](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01145) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Teddy Ferdinan, Bartłomiej Koptyra, Mikołaj Langner, Tomasz Adamczyk, Łukasz Radliński, Maciej Markiewicz, Aleksander Szczęsny, Stanisław Woźniak, Tymoteusz Romanowicz, Dzmitry Pihulski, Mateusz Zbrocki, Mateusz Śmigielski, Michał Rajkowski, Mateusz Biedka, Konrad Kiełczyński, Konrad Wojtasik, Jacek Duszenko, Jan Eliasz, Piotr Matys, Michał Bernacki-Janson, Maria Bellaniar Ismiati, Latius Hermawan, Wiktoria Mieleszczenko-Kowszewicz, Anna Kubicka-Sowińska, Grzegorz Chodak, Karol Postawa, Paweł Zyblewski, Tomasz Szandała, Łukasz Sterczewski, Adrian Chajec, Paweł Niewiadomski, Piotr Gruber, Marcin Wdowikowski, Sławomir Czarnecki, Bartłomiej Kryszak, Dominik Drabik, Tomasz Kajdanowicz, Kamil Mamak, Paweł Preś, Katarzyna Paczkowska, Joachim Sobczuk, Tomasz Zięba, Jan Kocoń, Maciej Piasecki, Przemysław Kazienko
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Survey, Scientific Reasoning, LLM Reasoning
- **Summary**: 推理语言模型（RLM）在硬科学中被广泛采用，而其他科学分支滞后。本文对欧洲研究委员会分类下的28个学科进行了全面调查，并提出了一个面向成熟度的评估框架。揭示了学科间RLM成熟度的显著差异，并讨论了未来方向。

<h3 id="499">[499] TokenMizer: Graph-Structured Session Memory for Long-Horizon LLM Context Management</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06337](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06337) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Shweta Mishra
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Memory Architecture, Knowledge Graph, LLM Agent
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Shweta-Mishra-ai/tokenmizer)
- **Summary**: 长窗口LLM会话中标准方法将历史视为扁平文本，丢失任务状态和决策推理。TokenMizer作为开源透明代理，将会话历史维护为类型化知识图，在上下文边界用token预算序列化替换原始文本。在3个合成会话上，决策召回率85% vs 70%，文件召回率100% vs 91.7%。

<h3 id="500">[500] Some hypotheses on how chatbots work in problem-solving-driven conversations. Large Language Models as confirmation of the Innovation Illusion</h3>

<h3 id="514">[514] Agent vs. Parametric World Models: Hybrid Planning for Reliable Language Agents</h3>

<h3 id="515">[515] Customized Generative AI Agent for Transportation Engineering Practice: A Development and Continued Pre-training Guideline</h3>

<h3 id="516">[516] FADE: Mitigating Hallucinations by Reducing Language-Prior Dominance in Large Vision-Language Models</h3>

<h3 id="518">[518] When Does Learning to Stop Help? A Cost-Aware Study of Early Exits in Reasoning Models</h3>

<h3 id="519">[519] A Three-Phase Foundation Model for Tax-Aware Personalized Portfolio Management</h3>

- **Tags**: Continual Learning, Medical AI
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/yufanlu221/IRFE-ECG)
- **Summary**: 针对多源ECG部署中无法重放原始数据的问题，提出IRFE-ECG方法，通过冻结主干网络并分配独立分类器，结合轻量路由器和top-2边缘融合策略。在四个数据集上，源感知专家选择达到0.7915 Macro-F1，接近离线独立头模型（0.7885），但无源ID时性能略有下降，表明自主源推断是主要瓶颈。

<h3 id="531">[531] Repair the Amplifier, Not the Symptom: Stable World-Model Correction for Agent Rollouts</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.01767](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01767) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xinyuan Song, Zekun Cai
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, World Model
- **Summary**: 针对语言智能体长期任务中世界模型修复成本高的问题，提出WM-SAR频谱子图修复方法，通过估计节点-边放大效应，贪婪地选择连接修复区域。理论与实验表明，该方法在紧凑token预算下实现更强的长期稳定性和根因恢复，匹配更大修复上下文的效果。

<h3 id="532">[532] Safety Testing LLM Agents at Scale: From Risk Discovery to Evidence-Grounded Verification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.01793](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01793) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yunhao Feng, Ruixiao Lin, Ming Wen, Qinqin He, Yanming Guo, Yifan Ding, Yutao Wu, Jialuo Chen, Zhuoer Xu, Xiaohu Du, Jianan Ma, Zixing Chen, Xingjun Ma, Yunhao Chen, Xinhao Deng
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Safety, LLM Agent, Software Testing
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Yunhao-Feng/Vera)
- **Summary**: 针对LLM智能体安全测试难以扩展的问题，提出Vera自动化测试框架，通过风险发现、组合组合和自适应执行三个阶段，生成可执行的安全用例并进行证据化验证。在四个生产级智能体框架上测试，平均攻击成功率达93.9%，并发布Vera-Bench基准，包含1600个可执行安全用例。

<h3 id="533">[533] ContextSniper: AntTrail's Token-Efficient Code Memory for Repository-Level Program Repair</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.01916](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01916) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Chiwang Luk, Matin Mohammad Najafi, Zhifeng Jia, Wei Yang, Xiuchang Li, Jinwei Zhu, Yang Ren, Lei Chen, Gao Cong
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Program Repair, Token Efficiency
- **Summary**: 针对仓库级代码修复中token消耗大的问题，提出ContextSniper方法，通过三级抽象索引、混合排序和意图感知过滤，选择精确的证据包。实验显示，token使用减少51.5%，成本降低36.4%，修复质量基本不变。

<h3 id="534">[534] PACE: A Proxy for Agentic Capability Evaluation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02032](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02032) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yueqi Song, Lintang Sutawika, Jiarui Liu, Lindia Tjuatja, Jiayi Geng, Yunze Xiao, Daniel Lee, Aditya Bharat Soni, Vincent Lo, Xiang Yue, Graham Neubig
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, LLM Evaluation, Agent Evaluation
- **Summary**: 针对LLM智能体性能评估成本高昂的问题，提出PACE框架，通过从非智能体基准中选取原子实例构建代理基准。在14个模型和4个智能体基准上，预测的MAE低于4%，Spearman相关性高于0.80，成本不到完整评估的1%。

<h3 id="535">[535] ContextNest: Verifiable Context Governance for Autonomous AI Agent</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02116](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02116) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Misha Sulpovar, Benn R. Konsynski, Qaish Kanchwala, Gabe Goodhart
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, RAG, Context Governance
- **Summary**: 针对自主AI智能体依赖外部知识库但缺乏可验证治理的问题，提出ContextNest规范与实现，提供版本、完整性、可追溯性等治理层。实验表明，在过期版本攻击下，治理选择优于稀疏检索（97% vs 90-93%），且确定性选择器在文档检索中完全稳定。

<h3 id="536">[536] Querying and Repairing Inconsistent Prioritized Knowledge Bases: Complexity Analysis and Links with Abstract Argumentation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2003.05746](https://arxiv.org/abs/2003.05746) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Meghyn Bienvenu, Camille Bourgaux
- **Subjects**: cs.LO; cs.AI; cs.DB
- **Tags**: Knowledge Representation, Computational Argumentation, Complexity Theory
- **Venue**: 17th International Conference on Principles of Knowledge Representation and Reasoning (KR 2020)
- **Summary**: 针对优先知识库的不一致处理问题，研究了基于全局、帕累托和完成最优修复的查询与修复复杂性，并与抽象论证框架建立联系。结果表明帕累托最优修复对应稳定扩展，并提出了基于有根据扩展的新语义。

<h3 id="537">[537] Double Fuzzy Probabilistic Interval Linguistic Term Set and a Dynamic Fuzzy Decision Making Model based on Markov Process with tts Application in Multiple Criteria Group Decision Making</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2111.15255](https://arxiv.org/abs/2111.15255) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zongmin Liu
- **Subjects**: eess.SY; cs.AI; econ.GN; math.OC
- **Tags**: Fuzzy Logic, Decision Making, Group Decision Making
- **Summary**: 针对现有概率语言术语集在群体决策中的缺陷，提出双重模糊概率区间语言术语集（DFPILTS），定义了模糊语义集成、偏好关系及聚合算子，并基于马尔可夫过程构建动态模糊决策模型。通过金融风险案例展示了其在多准则决策中的应用。

<h3 id="538">[538] Domain Knowledge-Informed Self-Supervised Representations for Workout Form Assessment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2202.14019](https://arxiv.org/abs/2202.14019) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Paritosh Parmar, Amol Gharat, Helge Rhodin
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.HC; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Self-Supervised Learning, Action Recognition, Sports Analytics
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/ParitoshParmar/Fitness-AQA)
- **Summary**: 针对运动姿态评估中姿态估计器在健身房场景下表现不佳的问题，提出领域知识启发的自监督方法（姿态对比学习和运动解耦），利用谐波运动特性学习表征。在Fitness-AQA数据集上，该方法优于现成姿态估计器和其他基线。

<h3 id="539">[539] A Transformer-Based Contrastive Learning Approach for Few-Shot Sign Language Recognition</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2204.02803](https://arxiv.org/abs/2204.02803) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Silvan Ferreira, Esdras Costa, Marcio Dahia, Jampierre Rocha
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Sign Language Recognition, Few-Shot Learning, Contrastive Learning
- **Summary**: 针对手语识别中词汇不断增长导致封闭集分类不可行的问题，提出基于对比学习的Transformer模型，学习身体关键点序列的嵌入表示。在LSA64数据集上，仅用48类训练，对16类未见手势以8个参考样本达到88.4%准确率。

<h3 id="540">[540] Restricted Bernoulli Matrix Factorization: Balancing the trade-off between prediction accuracy and coverage in classification based collaborative filtering</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2210.10619](https://arxiv.org/abs/2210.10619) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ángel González-Prieto, Abraham Gutiérrez, Fernando Ortega, Raúl Lara-Cabrera
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Recommender System, Collaborative Filtering
- **Summary**: 针对分类协同过滤中预测准确性与覆盖率之间的权衡问题，提出约束伯努利矩阵分解（ResBeMF），生成用户-项目对的完整概率分布。实验表明，该方法在预测质量、覆盖率和推荐质量之间取得良好平衡。

<h3 id="541">[541] Unveiling the Unborn: Advancing Fetal Health Classification through Machine Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2310.00505](https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.00505) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Sujith K Mandala
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Healthcare Monitoring
- **Summary**: 针对胎儿健康分类中数据复杂和标注样本有限的问题，提出基于LightGBM的机器学习方法，结合胎心率、宫缩和血压等多维特征。在测试集上达到98.31%的准确率，为早期检测提供了客观评估。

<h3 id="542">[542] Learning to Visually Connect Actions and their Effects</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2401.10805](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.10805) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Paritosh Parmar, Eric Peh, Basura Fernando
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.RO
- **Tags**: Video Understanding, Action Recognition, Self-Supervised Learning
- **Summary**: 针对视频理解中动作与效果连接的问题，引入CATE概念，探索动作选择和效果亲和力评估两种任务。实验表明人类大幅优于模型，但模型能学习到对象跟踪等隐含属性，且CATE可作为有效的自监督视频表征学习任务。

<h3 id="543">[543] TERC: A Transfer Entropy Redundancy Criterion for State Variable Selection in Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2401.11512](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.11512) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Charles Westphal, Stephen Hailes, Mirco Musolesi
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.IT
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Feature Selection, Representation Learning
- **Venue**: TMLR
- **Summary**: 针对强化学习中状态变量选择的问题，提出基于传递熵冗余准则（TERC）的信息论方法，从可观测状态变量中排除不影响策略的变量。在多个算法和环境上，推理时间最多减少2.6倍，且不降低性能。

<h3 id="544">[544] Graph Unitary Message Passing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2403.11199](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.11199) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Haiquan Qiu, Quanming Yao
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Graph Neural Network, Graph Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/ucker/gump_code)
- **Summary**: 针对图神经网络中传播算子导致指数衰减的问题，提出图幺正消息传递（GUMP），通过图变换和牛顿-舒尔茨迭代实现幺正传播。理论分析表明其深度稳定性，在长程任务和标准基准上优于或媲美强基线。

<h3 id="545">[545] CausalChaos! Dataset for Comprehensive Causal Action Question Answering Over Longer Causal Chains Grounded in Dynamic Visual Scenes</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2404.01299](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.01299) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Paritosh Parmar, Eric Peh, Ruirui Chen, Ting En Lam, Yuhan Chen, Elston Tan, Basura Fernando
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Video Understanding, Question Answering, Temporal Reasoning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/LUNAProject22/CausalChaos)
- **Summary**: 现有因果视频问答数据集缺乏深度因果推理。该论文利用卡通片特性构建了CausalChaos!数据集，包含多层级答案和困难错误答案挖掘。实验表明模型表现良好但在开放式答案上仍有提升空间。

<h3 id="546">[546] MambaCapsule: Towards Transparent Cardiac Disease Diagnosis with Electrocardiography Using Mamba Capsule Network</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2407.20893](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.20893) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yinlong Xu, Zitai Kong, Yixuan Wu, Yue Wang, Xiaoqiang Liu, Yingzhou Lu, Jian Wu, Hongxia Xu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; eess.SP
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Interpretability, Time Series Classification
- **Summary**: 现有ECG诊断深度学习模型缺乏可解释性。论文提出MambaCapsule，结合Mamba特征提取和胶囊网络预测，提供置信度和信号特征。在MIT-BIH和PTB数据集上分别达到99.54%和99.59%的准确率。

<h3 id="547">[547] Saving GPU Hours in LLM Inference System Development and Online Workloads with Simulation and DBMS-Inspired Cache Replacement Policies</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2411.07447](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.07447) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Kyoungmin Kim, Jiacheng Li, Kijae Hong, Qunyou Liu, Darong Huang, Anastasia Ailamaki
- **Subjects**: cs.PF; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, Cache Policy, LLM Serving
- **Summary**: LLM推理系统缺乏性能上限理论模型且开发耗GPU。论文通过仿真和数据库启发的缓存替换策略节省GPU资源。提出的策略能显著减少在线工作负载的GPU使用。

<h3 id="548">[548] Stroke Prediction using Clinical and Social Features in Machine Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2501.00048](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.00048) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Aidan Chadha
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Clinical Prediction, Health Monitoring
- **Summary**: 中风预测对健康干预很重要。论文比较了神经网络和逻辑回归模型用于中风预测。旨在最小化假阴性率。

<h3 id="549">[549] Robust Counterfactual Explanations under Model Multiplicity Using Multi-Objective Optimization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2501.05795](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.05795) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Keita Kinjo
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Interpretability, Counterfactual Reasoning, Multi-Objective Optimization
- **Summary**: 反事实解释在模型多重性下不鲁棒。论文提出基于帕累托改进和多目标优化的鲁棒反事实解释方法。实验证明该方法既鲁棒又实用。

<h3 id="550">[550] Code Benchmarks Should Prioritize Rigor, Reliability, and Reproducibility</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2501.10711](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.10711) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jialun Cao, Yuk-Kit Chan, Zixuan Ling, Wenxuan Wang, Shuqing Li, Mingwei Liu, Ruixi Qiao, Yuting Han, Chaozheng Wang, Boxi Yu, Pinjia He, Shuai Wang, Zibin Zheng, Michael R. Lyu, Shing-Chi Cheung
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Code Generation, LLM Evaluation, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 代码基准测试质量滞后于意识。论文调查了672个代码基准，提出HOW2BENCH指南含55个检查清单。人类研究发现缺乏意识是主要问题。

<h3 id="551">[551] Evaluating LLM-Based Regression Test Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2501.11086](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.11086) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jing Liu, Seongmin Lee, Eleonora Losiouk, Marcel Böhme
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Code Generation, Test Case Generation, Fuzzing
- **Venue**: PACMSE
- **Summary**: 自动生成回归测试对程序（如解析器）很重要。论文用LLM从提交信息生成测试，原型Cleverest在2分钟内发现与模糊测试24小时一样多的bug。结合模糊测试使bug发现翻倍。

<h3 id="552">[552] Machine Unlearning via Information Theoretic Regularization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2502.05684](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.05684) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Shizhou Xu, Thomas Strohmer
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.IT; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Machine Unlearning, Privacy, Information Theory
- **Summary**: 需要有效去除学习结果中的信息。论文提出基于信息论正则化的统一框架，用于数据点和特征遗忘。提供了可审计的保证和理论联系。

<h3 id="553">[553] Evolutionary Guided Decoding: Iterative Value Refinement for LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2503.02368](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.02368) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zhenhua Liu, Lijun Li, Ruizhe Chen, Yuxian Jiang, Tong Zhu, Zhaochen Su, Wenliang Chen, Jing Shao

<h3 id="554">[554] Using Mechanistic Interpretability to Craft Adversarial Attacks against Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2503.06269](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.06269) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Thomas Winninger, Boussad Addad, Katarzyna Kapusta
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI

<h3 id="555">[555] Explainable Bayesian deep learning through input-skip Latent Binary Bayesian Neural Networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2503.10496](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.10496) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Eirik Høyheim, Lars Skaaret-Lund, Solve Sæbø, Aliaksandr Hubin

- **Summary**: ANN过参数化且难解释。论文提出输入跳跃潜二元贝叶斯神经网络(ISLaB)，允许协变量跳跃或排除，大幅减少网络密度。在MNIST上仅用935个权重达到97%准确率。

<h3 id="556">[556] Empirical Computation: Prompting versus Programming</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2503.10954](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.10954) (replaced)

- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Software Engineering, Prompt Engineering

- **Summary**: LLM智能体无需算法可解决计算问题，但性质不明。论文提出“经验计算”领域，呼吁软件工程社区建立基础。无具体结果。

<h3 id="557">[557] Measuring the Robustness of Audio Deepfake Detection under Real-World Corruption</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2503.17577](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.17577) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xiang Li, Pin-Yu Chen, Wenqi Wei
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.SD

- **Summary**: 真实世界音频污染降低深度伪造检测性能。论文系统评估10个模型对18种污染的鲁棒性。发现模型对噪声鲁棒但对音频修改和压缩脆弱；语音基础模型更优。

<h3 id="558">[558] Prima.cpp: Fast 30-70B LLM Inference on Heterogeneous and Low-Resource Home Clusters</h3>

<h3 id="559">[559] AgentDynEx: Nudging the Mechanics and Dynamics of Multi-Agent Simulations</h3>

<h3 id="560">[560] GenShin: Guiding Rational Liposome Design by Ranking Liposomal Protein Corona through a Docking-Pose-Free GNN</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2504.13853](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.13853) (replaced)

<h3 id="561">[561] MOSAIC: Skill-Centric Manipulation Planning with Physics Simulation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2504.16738](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.16738) (replaced)

- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Robot Manipulation, Motion Planning, Skill Learning
- **Venue**: 2026 IEEE International Conference on Robotics and Automation (ICRA)
- **Summary**: 该论文针对长时域操作规划中技能序列搜索空间巨大、中间步骤非显式的问题，提出了MOSAIC方法。MOSAIC利用物理模拟评估技能执行结果，通过生成器识别'能力岛屿'和连接器链接轨迹，实现多方向技能中心规划。在仿真和真实世界的复杂长时域任务中验证了其有效性。

<h3 id="562">[562] Exploring Context-aware and LLM-driven Locomotion for Immersive Virtual Reality</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2504.17331](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.17331) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Suleyman Ozdel, Kadir Burak Buldu, Enkelejda Kasneci, Efe Bozkir
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Virtual Reality, Human-Computer Interaction, Accessibility Technology
- **Venue**: Proceedings of the ACM on Human-Computer Interaction (PACM HCI)
- **Summary**: 针对传统VR语音导航依赖刚性指令集的问题，提出基于大语言模型的上下文感知自然语言导航方法，通过眼动追踪与标准化问卷评估，显示该方法在可用性、存在感与晕动症方面不逊于传统方式，且能增强用户注意力与参与度。

<h3 id="563">[563] kAgent: An execution-guided crash resolution agent for the Linux kernel</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2504.20412](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.20412) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Alex Mathai, Chenxi Huang, Suwei Ma, Jihwan Kim, Hailie Mitchell, Aleksandr Nogikh, Petros Maniatis, Franjo Ivančić, Junfeng Yang, Baishakhi Ray
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.OS
- **Tags**: Program Repair, LLM Agent, Fuzzing
- **Venue**: Deep Learning for Code Workshop at ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 针对现有LLM代码修复技术不适应内核模糊测试崩溃（缺少自然语言报告、测试预言等）的问题，构建了工作流驱动的崩溃修复智能体kAgent，通过执行日志分析、假设生成、补丁合成与崩溃复现迭代优化，在kBenchSyz上修复54.5%的崩溃，文件提示下修复65%。

<h3 id="564">[564] Towards Understanding Deep Learning Model in Image Recognition via Coverage Test</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2505.08814](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.08814) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Wenkai Li, Xiaoqi Li, Yingjie Mao, Yishun Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Neural Network Verification, Test Generation, Model Evaluation
- **Summary**: 该论文针对深度神经网络覆盖度测试中缺乏度量之间关系及与模型深度、配置信息关联的实证研究问题，对LeNet、VGG、ResNet等不同架构和深度的模型进行了覆盖度指标实验。研究发现主要功能、边界、层次和结构覆盖度与模型深度及配置之间的关系，并提出了三个未来研究方向以促进DNN安全测试。

<h3 id="565">[565] Quick ViTs: Speeding up Vision Transformers through Equivariance</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2505.15441](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.15441) (replaced)

- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG

- **Summary**: 针对视觉Transformer计算效率问题，提出基于八阶群等变线性层的Quick ViTs，在傅里叶域实现反射与旋转等变，相比普通线性层FLOPs降低5.33倍，内存减少8倍，在ImageNet-1K上精度匹配或超越基线。

<h3 id="566">[566] PDFBench: A Benchmark for De novo Protein Design from Function</h3>

<h3 id="567">[567] Seven Security Challenges in Cross-domain Multi-agent LLM Systems</h3>

<h3 id="570">[570] FuseMamba-VD: Dual Branch VideoMamba with Gated Class Token Fusion for Violence Detection</h3>

<h3 id="571">[571] Ensemble Elastic DQN: A Step Dependent Ensemble Approach for Reducing Overestimation in Deep Value-Based Reinforcement Learning</h3>

<h3 id="572">[572] InverseScope: Scalable Activation Inversion for Interpreting Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2506.07406](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.07406) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yifan Luo, Zhennan Zhou, Bin Dong
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI

- **Summary**: 针对特征可解释性方法依赖线性或稀疏假设的局限，提出无假设的激活反演框架InverseScope，通过生成自然语言输入逼近目标激活，揭示LLM表示空间中的丰富几何结构，可扩展至140亿参数模型。

<h3 id="573">[573] ELBO-T2IAlign: A Generic ELBO-Based Method for Calibrating Pixel-level Text-Image Alignment in Diffusion Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2506.09740](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.09740) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Qin Zhou, Zhiyang Zhang, Jinglong Wang, Xiaobin Li, Jing Zhang, Qian Yu, Lu Sheng, Dong Xu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Text-to-Image, Image Generation
- **Summary**: 针对扩散模型像素级文本-图像对齐不佳问题，提出基于证据下界的训练无关校准方法ELBO-T2IAlign，无需额外标注或架构修改，在零样本指代分割、文本引导编辑和组合生成任务中提升对齐质量。

<h3 id="574">[574] Position: Use Sparse Autoencoders to Discover Unknowns</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2506.23845](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.23845) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Kenny Peng, Rajiv Movva, Jon Kleinberg, Emma Pierson, Nikhil Garg
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.CY
