<h3 id="703">[703] Shao: Scaling Acoustic Token Language Models Toward High-Fidelity Music Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.01790](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01790) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jiafeng Liu, Yuanliang Dong, Hongjia Liu, Yuqing Cheng, Zhancheng Guo, Huijing Liang, Wenbo Zhan, Yuming Sun, Xiaobing Li, Feng Yu, Maosong Sun
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Music Generation, Autoregressive Model
- **Summary**: 探索在统一声学令牌层次结构中逐步建模音乐生成。提出两阶段粗到细框架，采用混合注意力训练实现歌词对齐，无需分离语义层。

<h3 id="704">[704] ARISE: A Repository-level Graph Representation and Toolset for Agentic Program Repair and Fault Localization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.03117](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03117) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Shahd Seddik, Fahd Seddik, Amirrezza Esmaeili, Mahdieh Sadatbenis, Fatemeh Fard
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Program Repair, Fault Localization, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 现有图结构代理缺乏语义精度。本文提出ARISE，构建多粒度程序图并引入数据流切片，在SWE-bench Lite上将问题解决率提升至22.0%，行级定位R@1从0.26提升至0.41。

<h3 id="705">[705] Undetectable Backdoors in Model Parameters: Hiding Sparse Secrets in High Dimensions</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.04209](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04209) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Sarthak Choudhary, Atharv Singh Patlan, Nils Palumbo, Ashish Hooda, Kassem Fawaz, Somesh Jha
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Backdoor Attack, Model Security, Adversarial Robustness
- **Summary**: 该论文针对模型参数中的后门攻击，提出了一种在预训练图像分类器中植入不可检测后门的方法，通过向全连接层稀疏子集注入结构化稀疏扰动并添加高斯噪声掩盖，利用稀疏PCA的难解性保证后门在多项式时间内不可区分。实验证明该方法在卷积网络和视觉变换器上均有效，且满足严格的计算不可检测性。

<h3 id="706">[706] IRC-Bench: Recognizing Entities from Contextual Cues in First-Person Reminiscences</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06142](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06142) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yehudit Aperstein, Eden Moran, Alexander Apartsin
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Benchmark, Named Entity Recognition, Narrative Understanding
- **Summary**: 该论文针对回忆叙事中隐含实体识别问题，提出IRC-Bench基准，包含基于Wikidata链接的25,136个样本，覆盖11个主题领域。评估了多种方法，结果发现QLoRA微调的Llama 3.1 8B在开放世界设置中表现最佳（精确匹配38.94%），而微调DPR在封闭世界检索中领先（Hit@1 35.38%）。工作提供了数据、代码和评估工具。

<h3 id="707">[707] BioProVLA-Agent: An Affordable, Protocol-Driven, Vision-Enhanced VLA-Enabled Embodied Multi-Agent System with Closed-Loop-Capable Reasoning for Biological Laboratory Manipulation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.07306](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07306) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zhaohui Du, Zhe Wang, Dongzhan Zhou, Minting Pan, Hongmei Fei, Xiwen Cao, Ting Xiao, Qi Wang, Huanbo Jin, Jiaming Gu, Quan Lu, Zhe Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Robotics, Embodied AI, Multi-Agent System
- **Summary**: 该论文针对生物实验室自动化中可靠操作问题，提出BioProVLA-Agent，一种基于视觉-语言-动作模型的低成本多智能体系统。系统集成了协议解析、视觉状态验证和闭环执行，并开发了AugSmolVLA在线增强策略应对湿实验室视觉干扰。在原子任务、复合工作流和双臂任务上评估，AugSmolVLA在正常和高曝光设置下均提高了执行稳定性。

<h3 id="708">[708] Defense effectiveness across architectural layers: a mechanistic evaluation of persistent memory attacks on stateful LLM agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08442](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08442) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jun Wen Leong
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, LLM Security, Memory Architecture
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/junwenleong/stateful-agent-security-eval)
- **Summary**: 该论文针对LLM智能体持久内存攻击的防御效果进行了系统性评估，在四个架构层（输入、检索、指令、工具门控）测试了六种防御。发现仅记忆层的工具门控（Memory Sandbox）能将攻击成功率降至0%且无实用损失，但推理模型可通过目标导向RAG绕过。研究揭示了防御效果取决于架构层和推理能力，而非分类器质量。

<h3 id="709">[709] Evolutionary Ensemble of Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09018](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09018) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zongmin Yu, Liu Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.NE; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Code Generation, Evolutionary Computation
- **Summary**: 该论文针对代码生成中的算法发现瓶颈，提出了EvE（进化集成）框架，将现有编码智能体组织成共同进化的种群，通过同步竞赛更新Elo评分。在In-Context Operator Networks（ICON）中，EvE自主发现了一种鲁棒的重新缩放-插值机制，实现了可靠的示例计数泛化。消融实验表明，阶段依赖的智能体适应是突破静态性能上限的关键。

<h3 id="710">[710] Clin-JEPA: A Multi-Phase Co-Training Framework for Joint-Embedding Predictive Pretraining on EHR Patient Trajectories</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10840](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10840) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yixuan Yang, Mehak Arora, Ryan Zhang, Baraa Abed, Junseob Kim, Tilendra Choudhary, Md Hassanuzzaman, Kevin Zhu, Ayman Ali, Chengkun Yang, Alasdair Edward Gent, Victor Moas, Rishikesan Kamaleswaran
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; q-bio.QM
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Representation Learning, Time Series Forecasting
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Kamaleswaran-Lab/Clin-JEPA)
- **Summary**: 该论文针对电子健康记录中联合嵌入预测（JEPA）预训练的挑战，提出Clin-JEPA，一个五阶段协同训练框架，稳定地共同训练编码器和潜在轨迹预测器。在MIMIC-IV ICU数据上，潜伏ℓ1 rollout漂移在48小时视界内收敛，编码器学习到临床区分性潜伏几何，单主干网在多任务下游评估中优于强基线（平均AUROC 0.851和0.883）。

<h3 id="711">[711] Active Sensing with Meta-Reinforcement Learning for Emitter Localization from RF Observations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.12569](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12569) (replaced)
- **Authors**: M. Shamail J. Khan, Nisha L. Raichur, Lucas Heublein, Christian Wielenberg, Alexander Mattick, Tobias Feigl, Christopher Mutschler, Felix Ott
- **Subjects**: eess.SP; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Wireless Localization, Active Perception
- **Summary**: 该论文针对GNSS干扰定位问题，将主动感知与元强化学习结合，提出基于部分可观测决策过程的框架，使用DQN和PPO算法，在Sionna射线追踪模拟数据集上达到80.1%定位成功率。方法利用2x2贴片天线和RF观测，通过仿真辅助训练实现了在多径环境中的鲁棒干扰定位。

<h3 id="712">[712] CogAdapt: Adapting Clinical ECG Foundation Models for Wearable Cognitive Load Assessment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.22774](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22774) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Amir Mousavi, Erfan Nourbakhsh, Mohammad Sadegh Sirjani, Mimi Xie, Rocky Slavin, Leslie Neely, John Davis, John Quarles
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Wearable Computing, Human-Computer Interaction
- **Summary**: 该论文针对可穿戴设备中认知负荷评估的挑战，提出CogAdapt框架，包括LeadBridge适配器将3导联可穿戴信号映射到12导联兼容表示，以及ProFine渐进微调策略。在CLARE和CL-Drive数据集上，CogAdapt达到macro-F1 0.626和0.768，相比从头训练基线提升11.2和16.1个百分点，证明了临床ECG预训练支持主体无关的认知负荷评估。

<h3 id="713">[713] High-Risk AI Systems and the Problem of Identity in the European AI Act</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.23922](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23922) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Andrea Ferrario
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: AI Governance, AI Ethics, AI Policy
- **Venue**: FAccT '26
- **Summary**: 该论文针对欧盟人工智能法案中高风险AI系统的身份判定问题，提出了函数+（function+）框架，通过意图功能和可信度标准来界定系统身份。论文指出AIA缺乏同步身份判断的内部标准，并建议通过更精确的意图报告和标准化可信度报告来支持跨时间和部署的可比性。工作提供了审计和争议中的操作流程。

<h3 id="714">[714] Hidden-State Privacy Has an Empty Middle</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24042](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24042) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Alexander Okezue Bell
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Privacy, LLM Inference, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 该论文针对隐藏状态发布的隐私-效用权衡问题，通过理论和实验证明大多数高斯发布机制无法同时实现中等隐私和效用。实验发现对角逆Fisher发布是唯一在多个模型层上保持低攻击成功率的机制，但处于隐私/效用边缘。研究还发现拆分记忆变换器在固定令牌语言建模损失下能以更低的隐私预算维持优势，表明需要架构或发布协同设计。

<h3 id="715">[715] IndexMem: Learned KV-Cache Eviction with Latent Memory for Long-Context LLM Inference</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.25475](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25475) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xintong Yang, Hao Gu, Binxing Xu, Lujun Li, Bei Liu, Jiacheng Liu, Qiyuan Zhu, Yike Guo, Sirui Han
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, KV Cache, Long Context
- **Summary**: 该论文针对长上下文LLM推理中KV缓存增长问题，提出IndexMem，包括学习型索引器预测KV重要性，以及轻量级潜在记忆模块压缩被驱逐的令牌信息。在RULER和LongBench等基准上，该方法在有限KV预算下实现了准确的长期上下文推理，在激进驱逐下性能提升达25分，并保持了稳定的Needle-in-a-Haystack检索。

<h3 id="716">[716] Learning in Low-Dimensional Subspaces: Orthogonal Bottlenecks for Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.26012](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26012) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Aleksandar Todorov, Matthia Sabatelli
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Representation Learning, Dimensionality Reduction
- **Summary**: 该论文针对深度强化学习中高维表示的问题，提出使用固定正交投影将编码器特征约束到低维子空间的简单方法。理论上证明了当瓶颈维度超过最优值函数的特征空间内在秩时，保留表达性且梯度动力学不变。实验表明，一旦瓶颈维度超过小的任务依赖阈值，性能匹配或提升，且值表示可压缩至极低维度而不损失。

<h3 id="717">[717] MemTrace: Tracing and Attributing Errors in Large Language Model Memory Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28732](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28732) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xinle Deng, Ruobin Zhong, Hujin Peng, Xiaoben Lu, Yanzhe Wu, Guang Li, Buqiang Xu, Yunzhi Yao, Jizhan Fang, Haoliang Cao, Junjie Guo, Yuan Yuan, Ziqing Ma, Yuanqiang Yu, Rui Hu, Baohua Dong, Hangcheng Zhu, Ningyu Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Memory Architecture, RAG
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/zjunlp/MemTrace)
- **Summary**: 该论文针对LLM记忆系统错误追踪与归因问题，提出MemTrace框架，将记忆管道转换为可执行记忆演化图，实现细粒度操作信息流追踪。构建了MemTraceBench基准，涵盖长上下文、RAG、Mem0、EverMemOS等系统。引入自动归因方法迭代追踪子图以定位失败根因，并利用归因信号指导提示优化，提升了端任务性能达7.62%。

<h3 id="718">[718] Emergent Semantic Representations in World Models through Physical Interaction without Linguistic Supervision</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28865](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28865) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jiayi Fang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: World Model, Representation Learning, Embodied AI
- **Summary**: 该论文探究了世界模型在没有语言监督的情况下通过物理探索学习到的表示结构。实验表明，基于VAE的世界模型在随机具身探索后，其潜在空间发展出与物理几何结构对应的空间语义结构（方向准确度0.677，位置RSA提升6.6倍）。通过双重敲除实验证实了预测性能与语义对齐共同提升，并指出KL正则化会破坏几何结构，降低β可恢复。

<h3 id="719">[719] Evaluating Skill and Stability of ArchesWeather and ArchesWeatherGen under Multi-Decadal Climate Simulations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29976](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29976) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Renu Singh, Robert Brunstein, Antonia Jost, Yana Hasson, Thomas Rackow, Claire Monteleoni, Christian Lessig, Guillaume Couairon
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Weather Forecasting, Climate Science, Simulation
- **Summary**: 该论文评估了ArchesWeather和ArchesWeatherGen两个机器学习模型在多年气候模拟中的技能和稳定性。通过强制大气模型配置并遵循AIMIP协议，发现它们能够产生稳定的长期气候模拟，具有稳定的年周期，并能捕捉ERA5的气候学、大尺度环流和年际变率以及分布尾部。研究包括与数值气候模型的比较和消融实验。

<h3 id="720">[720] Dissociative Identity: Language Model Agents Lack Grounding for Reputation Mechanisms</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30169](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30169) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Botao Amber Hu, Helena Rong, Max Van Kleek
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, AI Governance, AI Ethics
- **Venue**: FAccT 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文针对语言模型智能体的身份问题，指出其本质上具有解离性（由可变的模块组装而成），缺乏持久身份、行为连续性、制裁敏感性和不可替代性，因此基于身份的信誉机制（如信用评分）在结构上不适用。建议转向基于可观测性的前置、构成性、协议驱动的行为约束，而非事后制裁。论文借鉴了解离性身份障碍法理。

<h3 id="721">[721] De-attribute to Forget for LLM Unlearning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30919](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30919) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xinyang Lu, Jiabao Pan, Rachael Hwee Ling Sim, See-Kiong Ng, Anthony Kum Hoe Tung, Bryan Kian Hsiang Low
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Machine Unlearning, LLM Unlearning
- **Summary**: 针对大语言模型遗忘中的过度遗忘和模型效用下降问题，本文提出基于数据归因奖励的LLM遗忘框架DareU，通过强化学习减少模型回答对遗忘数据所有者的归因分数。实验表明DareU在平衡遗忘质量与模型效用方面优于现有基线。

<h3 id="722">[722] Automatically Differentiable Nonlinear Tensor Networks (ADNTNs) for Exponential Parameter Compression of Deep Neural Networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00130](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00130) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Andrzej Cichocki, Michal Wietczak
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Model Compression, Tensor Network
- **Summary**: 针对大型深度神经网络存储和部署成本高的问题，本文提出自动可微非线性张量网络（ADNTNs）作为紧凑权重生成器，通过层次化收缩和可学习非线性替代稠密层。在CIFAR-10上实现每层参数压缩比约2000倍至430000倍，部分压缩模型匹配或超过稠密基线。

<h3 id="723">[723] ParetoPilot: Zero-Surrogate Offline Multi-Objective Optimization via Infer-Perturb-Guide Diffusion</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.04468](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04468) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ruiqing Sun, Sen Yang, Dawei Feng, Bo Ding, Yijie Wang, Huaimin Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.NE; math.OC
- **Tags**: Multi-Objective Optimization, Diffusion Model
- **Summary**: 针对离线多目标优化需外部代理模型的问题，本文提出ParetoPilot方法，在扩散模型逆向过程中通过推断-扰动-引导引擎引导设计走向帕累托前沿，无需代理模型。在51个任务上取得最佳排名和竞争性超体积改进。

<h3 id="724">[724] FP8 is All You Need (Part 1): Debunking Hardware FP64 as the HPC Holy Grail (June 13th version)</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06510](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06510) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Satoshi Matsuoka
- **Subjects**: cs.AR; cs.AI; cs.DC; cs.PF
- **Tags**: FP8 Computing, High Performance Computing
- **Summary**: 本文论证在AI优化GPU上，原生FP64吞吐量下降而FP8张量核心矩阵乘法可作为双精度科学计算的基础。通过Ozaki Scheme II将核心核归约为FP8矩阵运算，构建张量内存均衡模型预测恢复性能。

<h3 id="725">[725] ResearchClawBench: A Benchmark for End-to-End Autonomous Scientific Research</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07591](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07591) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Wanghan Xu, Shuo Li, Tianlin Ye, Qinglong Cao, Yixin Chen, Hengjian Gao, Yiheng Wang, Qi Li, Kun Li, Sheng Xu, Shengdu Chai, Fangchen Yu, Xiangyu Zhao, Zhangrui Zhao, Weijie Ma, Zijie Guo, Koutian Wu, Haoyu Zhou, Haoxiang Yin, Lixue Cheng, Chaofan Hu, Haoxuan Li, Lu Mi, Xuxuan Xie, Yifan Zhou, Ruizhe Chen, Zhiwang Zhou, Xingjian Guo, Yuhao Zhou, Xuming He, Shengyuan Xu, Xinyu Gu, Jiamin Wu, Mianxin Liu, Chunfeng Song, Fenghua Ling, Dongzhan Zhou, Shixiang Tang, Yuqiang Li, Mao Su, Peng Ye, Siqi Sun, Bin Wang, Xue Yang, Zhenfei Yin, Tianfan Fu, Guangtao Zhai, Wanli Ouyang, Bo Zhang, Lei Bai, Wenlong Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Benchmark, Scientific Reasoning, Autonomous Research
- **Summary**: 针对AI智能体自主科研能力难以验证的问题，本文提出ResearchClawBench基准，包含10个领域40个任务。评估表明最强自主智能体Claude Code平均得分21.5，LLM前沿仅26.5，离可靠再发现差距巨大。

<h3 id="726">[726] Quickest Detection of Hallucination Onset: Delay Bounds and Learned CUSUM Statistics</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.12476](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12476) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Igor Itkin
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Hallucination, Change Point Detection
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/YehudaItkin/quickest-hallucination-onset)
- **Summary**: 针对令牌级幻觉检测器忽略反应时间的问题，本文形式化幻觉起始检测为快速变化检测问题，建立马尔可夫模型得到延迟下界约1.3令牌。因果循环标注器实现11-13令牌检测延迟，但低虚警率下召回率低，分析表明分数缺陷是瓶颈。

<h3 id="727">[727] A Mathematical Theory of Value: a synthesis on goal-directed agency under resource constraints</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.12502](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12502) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Cheng Qian
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Information Theory, Goal-Directedness, AI Alignment
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/macrokit/value)
- **Summary**: 本文提出价值是目标导向智能体在资源约束下转化的结构量，采用对数度量并推导价值编码定理。在语言模型上验证感知互信息跟踪能力（ρ=0.977）和ΔG跟踪I(X;Y)（斜率0.953），提供统一理论和治理映射。

<h3 id="728">[728] Iterative Visual Thinking and the Self-Correction Mirage in VLM Grounding</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.13156](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13156) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Animesh Tripathy, Aswanth Krishnan
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Self-Correction, Visual Grounding
- **Summary**: 针对VLM空间定位中的自校正，本文提出迭代视觉思维方法，报告准确率提升2.4个百分点。分析表明这是测量幻象：提升依赖oracle选择最佳步骤，无标签停止规则下无效，原因在于验证失败。

<h3 id="729">[729] LectūraAgents: A Multi-Agent Framework for Adaptive Personalized AI-Assisted Learning and Embodied Teaching</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16428](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16428) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jaward Sesay, Yue Yu, Siwei Dong, Börje F. Karlsson
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Education Technology, Personalized Learning, Embodied AI
- **Summary**: 针对现有教育智能体缺乏多模态具身教学的问题，本文提出LectūraAgents多智能体框架，实现层级化自适应具身个性化教学。在多个课程评估中，教学内容质量、具身教学质量和个性化均优于现有方法。

<h3 id="730">[730] Lost at the End: Primacy Bias in Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Question Answering</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16494](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16494) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jieyuan Liu, Jianyang Gu, Shijie Chen, Jefferson Chen, Zhen Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: RAG, Multimodal Learning, Position Bias, Question Answering
- **Summary**: 针对多模态检索增强问答中上下文使用模式，本文设计受控实验发现首位优势效应（末尾丢失），首位槽比末位槽高16-26个百分点。消融表明多模态放大了文本首位偏差，定位到首位槽，检索侧修复无效。

<h3 id="731">[731] Attention is Just Another Name for Coupling? A Fast-Slow ODE Perspective on Hierarchical Pretraining</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16730](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16730) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zhengyuan Gao
- **Subjects**: stat.ML; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Transformer Architecture, Pre-training, Attention Mechanism
- **Summary**: 本文重新解释Transformer预训练为快慢奇异摄动流，线性化沿冻结注意力核特征基分解，超饱和深度后通过块粗粒化。参数分可见/不可见方向，块均值池化捕获慢路径信息，无共享结构时交叉通道无效。

<h3 id="732">[732] A Unified Causal-Origin Taxonomy of Distributional Shifts in Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16933](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16933) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ardianto Wibowo, Paulo E Santos, Amer Baghdadi, Matthew Stephenson, Karl Sammut, Jean-Philippe Diguet
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Distribution Shift, Causal Inference
- **Summary**: 针对强化学习中分布漂移因果起源关系不清的问题，本文提出统一因果起源分类法，区分内部/外部漂移和显/隐/混合漂移。基于POMDP分解交互组件，支持系统分析分布漂移下的鲁棒性。

<h3 id="733">[733] TransitNet: A Compact Attention-Augmented Deep Learning Framework for Low-SNR Transit Blind Searches</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.18932](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18932) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xingchen Yan, Jian Ge, Qingtian Liu, Kevin Willis, Quanquan Hu, Jiapeng Zhu
- **Subjects**: astro-ph.EP; astro-ph.IM; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Deep Learning, Astronomy, Attention Mechanism
- **Summary**: 针对中长周期地大小行星观测不完整的问题，本文提出TransitNet，一种紧凑注意力增强深度学习框架。在低信噪比下达到95.2%准确率，恢复率93.0%，远超传统方法。模型仅1.5MB，推理速度提升12-25倍。

<h3 id="734">[734] OrthoReg: Orthogonal Regularization for Hybrid Symbolic-Neural Dynamical Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.19145](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19145) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Till Richter, Niki Kilbertus
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; eess.SY
- **Tags**: Dynamical Systems, Neurosymbolic AI, Symbolic Regression
- **Summary**: 针对混合符号-神经动力学系统中神经网络可能重复学习机械部分的问题，本文提出正交正则化OrthoReg，直接惩罚重叠。实现互补分解，在库错配系统中改善符号恢复和分布外行为。

<h3 id="735">[735] Hard or Just Unreached? Diagnosing the Sampling Blind Spot in Math-Reasoning Difficulty Estimation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.19636](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19636) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Luca Zhou, Sajel Shah, Emanuele Rodolà, Roberto Dessì
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Mathematical Reasoning, LLM Reasoning, Sampling
- **Summary**: 针对数学推理难度估计中pass@k的盲点问题，本文发现10.3-22.9%的pass@k=0样本可通过六链确定性方案求解。恢复量随预算扩展，表明该层在残差流中可识别。

<h3 id="736">[736] Triangular Consistency as a Universal Constraint for Learning Optical Flow</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.19938](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19938) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yi Xiao, Carlos Rodriguez Coronel, Jing Zhan, Haniyeh Ehsani Oskouie, Alex Wong, Dong Lao
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Optical Flow, Computer Vision, Unsupervised Learning, Transfer Learning
- **Venue**: ECCV 2026
- **Summary**: 本文提出三角一致性作为光流学习的通用约束，通过组合两个流并强制一致性，可产生循环一致性、时间链和数据增强。该方法无额外标注需求，在多种学习设置中一致提升性能。

<h3 id="737">[737] ELVA: Exploring Ranking-Driven Universal Multimodal Retrieval</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.20280](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20280) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yuhan Liu, Pei Fu, Hang Li, Yukun Qi, Chao Jiang, Jingwen Fu, Zhen Liu, Bin Qin, Zhenbo Luo, Jian Luan, Jingmin Xin
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Universal Multimodal Retrieval, Contrastive Learning, RLVR
- **Venue**: ECCV 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文指出通用多模态检索中对比学习忽视了负样本间的粒度信息（grain blindness），提出ELVA框架，将基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）扩展到检索任务，通过排序驱动多模态大模型联合优化负样本排序并扩大正负间隔。在标准检索基准上取得最先进结果，在提出的MRBench上提升13.1%，有效缓解了粒度盲视。

<h3 id="738">[738] NRT-Bench: Benchmarking Multi-Turn Red-Teaming of LLM Operator Agents in Safety-Critical Control Rooms</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.20408](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20408) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Hanwool Lee, Dasol Choi, Bokyeong Kim, Haon Park, Seung Geun Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Red Teaming, Safety Evaluation
- **Summary**: 针对LLM代理在安全关键系统中鲁棒性评估不足的问题，论文提出NRT-Bench基准，用于模拟核电站控制室中的多轮红队攻击，以关键安全功能（CSF）作为客观危害信号。实验发现自适应多轮攻击可导致8.7%-12.1%的安全功能失效，不同模型脆弱性几乎不重叠，且防御效果高度依赖模型。

<h3 id="739">[739] A Digital Twin Framework for Traffic-Aware UAV Pavement Monitoring in Open-Traffic Conditions</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.20742](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20742) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yamil Uchani, Grace Luna, Edwin Salcedo, Mauricio Figueroa
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: UAV, Digital Twin, Object Detection
- **Venue**: 6th Annual IEEE International Conference on Digital Twins and Parallel Intelligence (DTPI 2026)
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/EdwinTSalcedo/RDMO-DigitalTwin)
- **Summary**: 现有无人机路面检测在开放交通条件下难以实际部署。论文提出基于Unity的数字孪生框架，集成程序化道路缺陷、动态交通代理和YOLOv8n感知模块，在合成数据上微调获得0.959 mAP@0.5。评估了不同恢复策略对覆盖范围和能耗的影响，证明数字孪生可支持交通感知无人机检测策略开发。

<h3 id="740">[740] The Power of Light: Improving Synthetic-to-Real Domain Adaptation through Physically-Based Indirect Illumination</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22574](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22574) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Hooman Tavakoli Ghinani, Tatjana Legler, Martin Ruskowski
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Sim-to-Real, Object Detection, Data Synthesis
- **Summary**: 为减小合成到真实的域差距，论文系统研究光照和背景复杂度对目标检测的影响，提出基于NVIDIA Isaac Sim的自动化管道SmartSDG及工业基准ILLUM_INTRUCK。实验表明复杂间接光照搭配域相关背景可丰富视觉线索，避免直接镜面反射能保留纹理、减少假阳性并加速收敛，提供了虚拟场景设计指南。

<h3 id="741">[741] VideoAgent: All-in-One Framework for Video Understanding and Editing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23327](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23327) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Hengji Zhou, Lingxuan Huang, Jian Wang, Bing Zhou, Si Wu, Lianghao Xia, Chao Huang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Video Understanding, Multi-Agent System, LLM Agent
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/HKUDS/VideoAgent)
- **Summary**: 现有视频编辑系统局限于短片段和特定任务，缺乏长视频理解和连贯叙事能力。论文提出VideoAgent一体化框架，通过自动镜头创作、多代理编排（30多个专业编辑代理）和文本-梯度图优化组合复杂流水线。在VideoEdit基准上编排成功率87-95%，API成本降低60%，人类评估内容质量仅比人工低4%。

<h3 id="742">[742] DramaDirector: Geometry-Guided Short Drama Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.24107](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24107) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Hengji Zhou, Sijie Liu, Jianrun Chen, Xingchen Zou, Lianghao Xia, Liqiang Nie
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Video Generation, Story Generation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/iLearn-Lab/DramaDirector)
- **Summary**: 短剧视频生成面临快速镜头节奏和电影摄影基础挑战。论文提出DramaDirector框架，利用真实短剧镜头的深度和姿态索引引导生成，解耦静态视觉和动态叙事条件，通过SFT和GRPO训练规划器，并检索几何参考引导首帧和图像到视频合成。在DramaBoard基准上优于现有方法，提升了忠实度、一致性和可控性。

<h3 id="743">[743] UC-Search: Risk-Aware Test-Time Search for Delayed Constrained Time-Series Control</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.25274](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25274) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xibai Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Time Series Forecasting, Optimal Control, Constrained Optimization
- **Summary**: 时间序列部署需要延迟可行决策而非仅精确预测。论文提出UC-Search，一个仅保留轨迹的搜索层用于延迟约束控制，利用冻结骨干输出和硬可行性自动机滚动路径，返回可行轨迹的首个动作。在M4Weekly等数据集上验证了保留前置搜索能在满足条件时改善决策，并给出证书有效性。

<h3 id="744">[744] Reclaim Evaluation: A Lossy Memory Is Worse Than an Empty One</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.25449](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25449) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Alex Kwon
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Hallucination, LLM Evaluation, Memory Architecture
- **Summary**: 语言模型的记忆若只保留错误结论而丢失推导过程，会自信地重复错误，比空记忆更糟。论文提出“脆弱记忆”概念和回收评估方法，发现修正能力瓶颈在于记忆是否保留了可重新推导的源信息。简单的“源优先”策略可在相等预算下恢复可修正性，并在多个记忆系统和对话数据上验证。

<h3 id="745">[745] Hybrid privacy-aware semantic search: SVD-truncated document geometry and CKKS-encrypted query reranking under a restricted threat model</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.26373](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26373) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Sergey Kurilenko
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.IR
- **Tags**: Privacy, Vector Search, Homomorphic Encryption
- **Summary**: 密集嵌入泄露文本信息，现有同态加密检索慢而隐私噪声影响排序。论文提出混合方法：文档向量经SVD截断和秘密旋转保护，查询用CKKS加密，在百万文档规模实现亚秒级延迟，抵御低成本反演攻击。但文档保护是经验性模糊层，已知明文攻击可恢复旋转。

<h3 id="746">[746] Multipath Adaptive Gated Bottleneck Latent ODE with Raman Data Fusion for Cell Culture Process Forecasting</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.26520](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26520) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Johnny Peng, Thanh Tung Khuat, Ellen Otte, Katarzyna Musial, Bogdan Gabrys
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CE
- **Tags**: Time Series Forecasting, Neural ODE, Data Fusion
- **Summary**: 细胞培养过程监测困难，关键参数漂移数天后才能确认。论文提出自适应框架，结合门控瓶颈潜变量常微分方程（GB-Latent ODE）与多路径即时微调（MP-JIT-FT），并融合拉曼光谱数据。在38批运行上，MP-JIT-FT在9个变量中的8个上优于全局模型，多路径方法在局部发散时增益最大。

<h3 id="747">[747] Heavy-Ball Q-Learning with Residual Weighting Correction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.27112](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27112) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Donghwan Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Q-Learning, Convergence Analysis
- **Summary**: 针对标准Q-learning收敛慢的问题，论文提出带残差加权校正的重球Q-learning方法，证明其确定性均值动力学收敛且在一定条件下快于标准Q-learning。扩展到线性函数逼近，基于切换线性系统和联合谱半径框架分析，为理解动量加速Q-learning提供了新视角。

<h3 id="748">[748] The Unverifiability of Artificial General Intelligence (AGI) Alignment, Static and Dynamic: From Trakhtenbrot's Wall to the Safety-Generality Tension</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28639](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28639) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jose Pascual Gumbau Mezquita
- **Subjects**: cs.LO; cs.AI; cs.CC; cs.CL
- **Tags**: AI Safety, AI Alignment, Formal Methods
- **Summary**: 论文建立AGI安全的数学极限：静态下无法完全、可判定且高效验证AGI安全行为（音完善-完备-可判定三难）；动态下自修改系统无法保证安全性质持久。持久认证仅适用于停止演化的窄AI，且监管者本身也是通用AGI导致无限回归。这些结果将AI不可验证性从工程问题提升为结构性张力。

<h3 id="749">[749] Reward-Free Code Alignment from Pretrained or Fine-Tuned LLM: Unpacking the Trade-offs for Code Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28998](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28998) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Sanjeepan Sivapiran, Gias Uddin
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Code Generation, LLM Alignment, Preference Optimization
- **Summary**: LLM对齐技术对编程任务的作用不明，尤其能否同时满足功能和非功能需求，以及应从预训练还是微调版本开始。论文对五个模型使用DPO和BoNBoN无奖励对齐，在功能和非功能基准上评估。发现预训练到对齐提升更大，但预训练变体本身不如微调准确，而微调到对齐提升小甚至下降。

<h3 id="750">[750] A Deep Multiscale Neural Network for Accurate Neurological Disorder Detection from MRI Scans and Real-Time Web Deployment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29106](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29106) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ali Fatahi, Hoda Zamani, Mohammad H. Nadimi-Shahraki
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical Imaging, Neurological Disorder Detection, Data Augmentation
- **Summary**: 现有深度CNN多优化于二分类，难以捕捉多类神经疾病（阿尔茨海默病、脑肿瘤等）的细微差异。论文提出End-Net，含24卷积层和21个优化Inception模块提取多尺度特征，使用WGAN-GP和降采样解决不平衡，在公共数据集上性能优于现有架构，并集成在线系统实现实时推理。

<h3 id="751">[751] Automating the Design of Embodied Agent Architectures</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30111](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30111) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jian Zhou, Sihao Lin, Jin Li, Shuai Fu, Gengze Zhou, Qi Wu
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Embodied AI, Agent Architecture Search, Visual Navigation
- **Summary**: 具身代理通常手动设计模块组合，架构搜索（AAS）未在感知具身代理上系统评估。论文引入AgentCanvas运行时和KDLoop搜索流程，在四种执行器（视觉语言导航、具身问答、操作）上评估。发现架构级搜索可提升成功率，但存在被投掷噪声掩盖、陷入局部盆地、学分分配不完整等新约束。

<h3 id="752">[752] Curvature-Guided Sheaf Diffusion for Unsupervised Community Detection on Heterophilic Graphs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30249](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30249) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Feifan Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Graph Neural Network, Community Detection, Heterophilic Graph
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/woodywff/cgsd)
- **Summary**: 异配图中社区检测困难。论文提出曲率引导的层扩散（CGSD），利用离散Forman-Ricci曲率作为拓扑信号，通过曲率门控编码器和曲率感知谱聚类实现无监督社区检测。在五个异配基准上，CGSD在Wisconsin和Chameleon上最优，聚类器贡献大于编码器，曲率分布可解释。

<h3 id="753">[753] A Stochastic--Geometric Theory of Scaling Laws in Grokking</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30388](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30388) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Róisín Luo, Christian Gagné, Jonas Ngnawé, Ihsan Ullah, Karyn Morrissey
- **Subjects**: stat.ML; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Grokking, Scaling Laws
- **Summary**: 本文针对神经网络中延迟泛化（grokking）现象，提出一种基于Adam优化动力学和权重衰减正则化的随机几何理论，揭示了解空间的壳核拓扑结构。通过停时理论分析了学习率、批次大小和L2正则化系数对泛化时间的影响，并推导出相应的缩放律。实验验证了理论结果与先前文献一致。

<h3 id="754">[754] TRACE: Temporal Relationship-Aware Conversational Entrainment Detection in Dyadic Speech</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30543](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30543) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Sathvik Manikantan Napa Ugandhar, Hao Zhang, Alison Gunzler, Yuzhe Wang, Thomas Thebaud, Georgi Tinchev, Venkatesh Ravichandran, Laureano Moro-Velázquez
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Affective Computing, Speech Emotion Recognition
- **Summary**: 针对对话语音中情感趋同检测问题，本文提出了DyadEE数据集和TRACE框架。TRACE将对话视为有序声学嵌入序列，利用情感微调Whisper模型提取特征，并融入对话上下文和关系信息。实验结果表明，TRACE在情感趋同检测上达到97.01%的准确率。

<h3 id="755">[755] Modeling Cell-Cycle-Aware Single-Cell Drug Perturbation Responses</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30695](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30695) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Dingping Zhao, Jie Lin, Feng Xu, Zhengwei Xie
- **Subjects**: q-bio.QM; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Single-cell Analysis, Drug Discovery
- **Summary**: 针对单细胞药物扰动响应建模中细胞周期变化被忽视的问题，本文提出scCycleMol框架，通过从处理状态提取细胞周期监督信号并应用于预测表达，避免将相位作为输入。在SciPlex3基准上，该模型在全部基因R平方达到0.9093，差异表达基因R平方为0.6843，并显著提升相位预测准确率。

<h3 id="756">[756] From Materials Database to Materials Bank: Assetizing Data for AI Driven Materials Innovation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.31366](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31366) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Chenyao Ma, Di Zhang, Weibo Gong, Wei Du, Rui Su, Yuhang Chen, Kan Xu, Huan Gu, Limin Li, Piao Ma, Zhenghao Li, Hao Li
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Material Discovery, Data Curation
- **Summary**: 针对传统材料数据库作为被动存储库、缺乏价值筛选的问题，本文提出工业化导向的材料银行（Materials Bank），通过多维度银行卡片框架将合格候选材料转化为标准化可升级资产，实现从数据到知识的闭环。该框架作为决策基础设施，可加速AI驱动的材料创新。

<h3 id="757">[757] CVE-TTP KG: Knowledge Graph Linking Software Vulnerabilities to Attack Behaviors</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.31557](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31557) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Swati Yadav, Dincy R. Arikkat, Basant Agarwal, Serena Nicolazzo, Antonino Nocera, Vinod P
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Cyber Threat Intelligence, Knowledge Graph, Information Extraction
- **Summary**: 针对漏洞数据库缺乏与攻击行为关联的问题，本文构建了CVE-TTP知识图谱，将CVE与MITRE ATT&CK战术和技术相链接。使用Transformer模型进行行为识别，CySecBERT在战术和技术的宏F1分别达到96.16%和87.71%，实体和关系抽取也取得高F1值。

<h3 id="758">[758] WorldRoamBench: An Open-World Benchmark for Long-Horizon Stability of Interactive World Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.31672](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31672) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ting-Bing Xu, Jiacheng Sui, Zhe Gao, Kewei Shi, Wenjin Yang, Zhicheng Liu, Zhaoxu Sun, Mingchao Sun, Hongyu Pan, Fan Jiang, Mu Xu, Qi Fan, Yang Gao, Yong Li, Baoquan Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: World Model, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 针对现有交互世界模型基准忽略长期稳定性和记忆的问题，本文提出WorldRoamBench，涵盖动作、视觉、物理和记忆四个维度的评估。包含600多个测试用例，对10多个模型评估发现无一能在所有维度上表现良好。

<h3 id="759">[759] GR2 Technical Report</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.31984](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31984) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yufei Li, Zaiwei Zhang, Mingfu Liang, Kavosh Asadi, Jay Xu, Jimmy Kim, Chongyang Bai, Jieyi Zhang, Hongye Xie, Prachi Agrawal, Dian Yu, Tianyi Chen, Jean-Pascal Billaud, Garret Buell, Yongkang Zhu, Sachin Patil, Brooke Bian, Zhou Fang, Kevin Huang, Shiva Sudanagunta, Yuzhen Huang, Emma Lu, Chris O&#39;Brien, Yang Song, Lihong Li, Jacob Tao, Zhicheng Zhu, Chao Li, Gaoxiang Liu, Neil Wu, Zhongyin Hu, Li Han, Loki Chen, Ming Lei, Greg Rehm, Siyuan Song, Tianwei Zhang, Li Li, Ketan Singh, Yavuz Yetim, Ilyas Atishev, Satendra Gera, Ashkan Sadeghi, Rachel Yan, Nikko Mizutani, Shuaiwen Wang, Song Yang, Zhijing Li, Jiang Liu, Mengying Sun, Fei Tian, Xiaohan Wei, Chonglin Sun, Shuo Gu, Parish Aggarwal, Senthil Manickavelu, Kaushik Rangadurai, Zhi Hua, Frank Shyu, Ruchit Sharma, Liyuan Li, Shike Mei, Wenlin Chen, Santanu Kolay, Ben Schulte, Deepak Chandra, Yang Song, Sandeep Pandey, Xi Liu, Hamed Firooz, Luke Simon
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Recommender System, Reinforcement Learning, LLM Recommendation
- **Summary**: 针对工业推荐系统中重排阶段未被充分利用的问题，本文提出GR2框架，结合语义ID、推理轨迹蒸馏和基于可验证奖励的强化学习。在工业规模流量上，GR2实现R@1提升18.7%、R@3提升7.1%、N@3提升9.6%，并发现奖励设计对防止作弊至关重要。

<h3 id="760">[760] TRIAGE: Role-Typed Credit Assignment for Agentic Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.32017](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32017) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yuanda Xu, Zhengze Zhou, Hejian Sang, Xiaomin Li, Jiaxin Zhang, Xinchen Du, Sen Na, Zhipeng Wang, Alborz Geramifard
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Credit Assignment, Reinforcement Learning, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 针对标准GRPO在智能体强化学习中仅使用最终结果作为统一优势导致无法区分有用探索与冗余动作的问题，本文提出TRIAGE，通过语义角色将每个片段分类并映射为分段过程奖励。实验表明，TRIAGE在多个基准上提升成功率，并减少环境交互轮次。

<h3 id="761">[761] Spectral Geometry and Bosonic-Bloch Probes: Explorations in Quantum Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.00063](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00063) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Santanu Ganguly, Xing Liang, Dimitrios Makris
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Quantum Machine Learning, Anomaly Detection
- **Summary**: 本文研究谱几何在量子学习模型中的出现及其通过物理探针的诊断。在图正则化量子网络中，训练重新组织输出相似性图，增加有效谱维度。混合量子自编码器在无监督良性数据阈值下实现约0.99的ROC-AUC，异常检测性能优异。

<h3 id="762">[762] Hate Speech Detection in Turkish and Arabic: A Comprehensive Study</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.00143](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00143) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Somaiyeh Dehghan, Gökçe Uludoğan, Mehmet Umut Şen, Elif Erol, Arzucan Özgür, Berrin Yanikoglu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Hate Speech Detection, Multilingual Learning, Text Classification
- **Summary**: 针对仇恨言论检测资源缺乏的问题，本文构建了涵盖土耳其语和阿拉伯语的仇恨言论数据集，涉及难民、巴以冲突等多个话题。开发了基于BERT的模型，实现仇恨类别分类、强度预测、目标识别和跨度检测等多维度分析。

<h3 id="763">[763] Real-Time Hard Negative Sampling via LLM-based Clustering for Large-Scale Two-Tower Retrieval</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.00448](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00448) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ivan Ji, Liuyi Hu, Harrison, Zhao, Lei Huang, Qunshu Zhang, Aameek Singh
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Recommender System, Hard Negative Mining, LLM Recommendation
- **Summary**: 针对双塔模型中传统负采样生成简单负例的问题，本文提出基于LLM聚类的硬负采样方法，利用LLM学习媒体表示生成同一簇内的硬负样本。该框架可处理数十亿数据点，在公开数据集和在线系统中优于现有方法，并能减少流行度偏差。

<h3 id="764">[764] Phantom References: Hallucinated Citations That Survive Peer Review at Top-Tier Conferences</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.00738](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00738) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Mark Russinovich, Ram Shankar Siva Kumar, Ahmed Salem
- **Subjects**: cs.DL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Hallucination, AI-Generated Text Detection, Fact Checking
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/markrussinovich/refchecker)
- **Summary**: 针对大型语言模型生成的科学文本中引用幻觉问题，本文构建RefChecker管道验证顶会论文中的引用真实性。在ICLR、ICML、NeurIPS等会议中发现了幻觉引用，约5%的论文包含至少两个疑似幻觉引用。审计成本低廉，开源RefChecker用于出版前验证。

<h3 id="765">[765] Pano2World: End-to-End 3D Generation via Unified Multi-View Sequences</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.00832](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00832) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zhenjia Li, Jinrang Jia, Yifeng Shi
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: 3D Generation, Novel View Synthesis, Indoor Scene Synthesis
- **Summary**: 针对单张全景图无法自由探索场景的问题，本文提出Pano2World，输入单张室内全景图直接输出可探索的3D高斯场景。通过全景扩散模型联合去噪，并引入潜在特征适配器避免信息损失。实验结果表明，该方法在多位置全景新视角合成基准上显著优于现有方法。

<h3 id="766">[766] From World Models to World Action Models: A Concise Tutorial for Robotics</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.00836](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00836) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xiaoxiong Zhang, Xiong Zeng, Wei Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; eess.SY
- **Tags**: World Model, Embodied AI
- **Summary**: 本文以教程形式梳理了世界模型和世界动作模型的设计空间，将其分为观测空间和状态空间两类，并比较了视觉保真度、可解释性等权衡。进一步介绍了世界动作模型的四种范式，为具身智能和机器人控制提供了结构化分类。

<h3 id="767">[767] Post-Training Pruning for Diffusion Transformers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.00927](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00927) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Chengzhi Hu, Xuewen Liu, Jing Zhang, Mengjuan Chen, Zhikai Li, Qingyi Gu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Model Compression, Diffusion Model, Pruning
- **Summary**: 针对扩散变换器（DiTs）计算开销大的问题，本文提出DiT-Pruning，通过基于能量的新度量平衡权重和激活贡献，并采用聚类感知的剪枝粒度。在FLUX.1-dev上，50%稀疏度时CLIP分数仅下降0.001，显著优于现有剪枝方法。

<h3 id="768">[768] Learning Cardiac Motion Priors for Implicit Neural Representations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.00955](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00955) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Andrew Bell, George Webber, Andrew P King, Steffen E Petersen, Muhummad Sohaib Nazir, Alistair Young
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical Imaging, Implicit Neural Representation, Meta-Learning
- **Summary**: 针对心脏运动隐式神经表示（INR）学习中优化耗时长且敏感的问题，本文比较了四种学习先验的策略，包括种群先验、共识先验、自动解码器和元学习。实验表明，所有学习先验均显著提升早期适应性能，其中元学习在50次迭代内保持最佳适应轨迹。

<h3 id="769">[769] Cheap Code, Costly Judgment: A Case Study on Governable Agentic Software Engineering</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.01087](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01087) (replaced)
- **Authors**: James C. Davis, Paschal C. Amusuo, Tanmay Singla, Berk Çakar, Kirsten A. Davis
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Agentic AI, AI-Assisted Development
- **Summary**: 本文探讨了在AI辅助软件工程中代码成本降低但判断成本上升的问题，通过12周的第一人称案例研究，开发了“治理转换”过程模型，说明如何从失败中发现控制机制。实验记录了88份现场笔记、420 KLOC生产代码和1.16 MLOC测试代码。

<h3 id="770">[770] Diffusion-GR2: Diffusion Generative Reasoning Re-ranker</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.01170](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01170) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zhuoxuan Zhang, Kangqi Ni, Yuhang Chen, Mingfu Liang, Xiaohan Wei, Yunchen Pu, Fei Tian, Chonglin Sun, Frank Shyu, Adam, Song, Sandeep Pandey, Luke Simon, Tianlong Chen, Xi Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Generative Recommendation, Diffusion Model, On-Policy Distillation
- **Summary**: 针对自回归生成式重排序器推理速度慢的问题，提出Diffusion-GR2，将AR重排序器转换为块扩散模型，通过转换微调、在策略蒸馏和强化学习恢复精度。在Amazon Beauty上，吞吐量提升2.4-3.5倍，精度接近AR基线。

<h3 id="771">[771] KARA: Efficient Reasoning LLM Serving via Sliding-Window KV Cache Compression</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.01237](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01237) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Shen Han, Yuyang Wu, Junpu Yu, Olexandr Isayev
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, KV Cache Management, Token Compression
- **Summary**: 针对推理LLM长链思维导致的KV缓存大、延迟高问题，提出Kara滑动窗口KV缓存压缩方法，使用双向注意力评分和Token2Chunk模块灵活保留重要语义信息。实验表明，该方法在vLLM框架上降低了内存使用并提高了吞吐量。

<h3 id="772">[772] TurnNat: Automatic Evaluation of Turn-Taking Naturalness in Dyadic Spoken Dialogue</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.01345](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01345) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Hao Zhang, Thomas Thebaud, Georgi Tinchev, Venkatesh Ravichandran, Laureano Moro-Velazquez
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Dialogue System, Speech Evaluation, Turn-Taking
- **Summary**: 针对对话系统中话轮转换自然度的自动评估问题，提出TurnNat框架，基于因果话轮预测模型的负对数似然评估时序异常。在扰动基准上，成功识别了不同类型的非自然话轮转换。

<h3 id="773">[773] Spin-Weighted Spherical Harmonics Enable Complete and Scalable $\mathrm{E}(3)$-Equivariant Networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.01408](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01408) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Chenxing Liang, Yuchao Lin, Andrii Kryvenko, Wendi Yu, Chuan Li, Jianwen Xie, Xiaofeng Qian, Shuiwang Ji
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Equivariant Neural Networks, Graph Neural Network, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 为解决E(3)等变网络可扩展性差及Gaunt张量积表达力不足的问题，提出SpinGTP，利用自旋权重球谐函数恢复反对称交互。在Tetris、3BPA、SPICE-MACE-OFF和OC20基准上，精度与完整CGTP相当，在手性材料任务上表现更优。

<h3 id="774">[774] MultAttnAttrib: Training-Free Multimodal Attribution in Long Document Question Answering</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.01420](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01420) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Dang Quang Thien Tran, Quang V. Dang, Vinamra Tyagi, Sai Soorya Rao Veeravalli, Trang Nguyen, Ryan A. Rossi, Franck Dernoncourt, Nedim Lipka, Koustava Goswami, Samyadeep Basu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Question Answering, Multimodal Learning, Attribution
- **Summary**: 针对多模态问答中的归因问题，提出无需训练的多模态归因方法MultAttnAttrib，利用预填充阶段和注意力头定位证据。在MultAttrEval基准上，该方法优于多种提示方法，匹配GPT 5.4，且延迟降低至1/7。

<h3 id="775">[775] DiPS: Dialogue Policy Selection for High-Stakes Persuasion Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.01557](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01557) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Tianyi Zhang, Mousumi Das, Abrar Anwar, Jesse Thomason, David Traum
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Dialogue System, Reinforcement Learning, AI Persuasion
- **Summary**: 为解决高风险场景下LLM说服策略单一的问题，提出DiPS框架，使用Q学习动态选择说服策略。在火灾疏散场景中，DiPS的撤离成功率高于零样本LLM和RAG增强方法。

<h3 id="776">[776] Predicting Closed-Loop Performance of Latent World Models: Offline Checkpoint Selection for MPC and Model-Based RL Under Non-Markovian Rewards in LunarLander</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.01736](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01736) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Nikolai Smolyanskiy
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; eess.SY
- **Tags**: Model-Based RL, World Model, Reinforcement Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/nsmoly/LunarLander_RSSM)
- **Summary**: 针对潜在世界模型检查点选择困难的问题，提出奖励可观测分数（ROF）和复合ROF（CROF）作为离线选择指标。CROF选择的模型驱动基于模型的A2C策略，击败无模型基线约24.5个回报点，且交互次数减少65倍。

<h3 id="777">[777] AI Virtue: What is "Good" Knowledge in the Age of Artificial Intelligence?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.01776](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01776) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Alan Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI
- **Tags**: AI Ethics, Philosophy of AI
- **Summary**: 本文探讨AI时代“好知识”的标准，应用数字人文方法分析553篇AI论文中的认知美德（如“创造性”），并提出评估AI知识价值（生成性）的框架。

<h3 id="778">[778] kNNGuard: Turning LLM Hidden Activations into a Training-Free Configurable Guardrail</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02072](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02072) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Mahmoud Abdelfattah, Hamid Nasiri, Peter Garraghan
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CR
- **Tags**: LLM Safety, Guardrail, Training-Free
- **Summary**: 针对现有护栏依赖微调导致泛化差和延迟高的问题，提出无需训练的kNNGuard，利用LLM隐藏激活进行多层kNN分类。在六个领域上F1与微调方法相当或更优，速度快2.7倍，领域适应仅需数秒。

<h3 id="779">[779] Guided Action Flow: Q-Guided Inference for Flow-Matching Vision-Language-Action Policies</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02092](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02092) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Liuhaichen Yang, Zhuang Jiang, Chenchao Sheng, Zezhi Tang
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Robot Manipulation, Vision-Language Model, Flow Matching
- **Summary**: 为在预训练的流匹配视觉-语言-动作策略中实现测试时引导，提出Guided Action Flow，使用动作块评论家引导逆向流采样。在LIBERO任务上，单任务评论家将成功率从68.0%提升至82.0%。

<h3 id="780">[780] ART for Diffusion Sampling: Continuous-Time Control and Actor-Critic Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02137](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02137) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yilie Huang, Wenpin Tang, Xun Yu Zhou
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; eess.SY; math.OC
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Diffusion Sampling, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 针对扩散采样中时间步分配次优的问题，提出自适应重参数化时间（ART）的连续时间控制公式，并通过ART-RL用演员-评论家学习调度。实验表明，该方法在图像生成等任务上一致提升样本质量，且学到的调度可跨预算、数据集和求解器泛化。

<h3 id="781">[781] GAP-GDRNet: Geometry-Aware Monocular Visual Pose Sensing on a Single-Target Synthetic Spacecraft Dataset</h3>

- **Authors**: Zongwu Xie, Yonglong Zhang, Yifan Yang, Yang Liu, Guanghu Xie
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Pose Estimation, Monocular Vision, Satellite Systems
- **Summary**: 针对单目航天器相对位姿感知中纹理弱、遮挡等问题，提出GAP-GDRNet，包含注意力特征细化和补丁几何自注意力模块。在合成数据集上旋转误差1.96°，平移误差0.0165 m，ADD@0.02m精度95.16%，速度35.97 FPS。

<h3 id="782">[782] Understanding Agent-Based Patching of Compiler Missed Optimizations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02370](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02370) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Batu Guan, Zirui Wang, Shaohua Li
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Program Repair, Code Optimization, Agentic AI
- **Summary**: 本文系统研究了智能体修复编译器漏优化的问题，发现修复需要泛化到类似案例。基于LLVM真实问题构建基准，并引入历史知识增强技术，提高了开发者对齐的泛化性。

<h3 id="783">[783] DemoPSD: Disagreement-Modulated Policy Self-Distillation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02502](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02502) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yunhe Li, Hao Shi, Wenhao Liu, Mengzhe Ruan, Hanxu Hou, Zhongxiang Dai, Shuang Qiu, Linqi Song
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Knowledge Distillation, On-Policy Distillation
- **Summary**: 针对在策略自蒸馏中教师特权信息泄露和探索抑制的问题，提出DemoPSD，通过选择性采用教师指导，使用逆KL重心目标平衡学习。在SciKnowEval上优于GRPO和SDPO，保持更高训练熵，并泛化到GPQA。
